Wat3R: Underwater 3D Geometry Learning without Annotations
Authors: Jiangwei Ren, Xingyu Jiang, Zijie Song, Wei Xu, Hongkai Lin, Dingkang Liang, Xiang Bai
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-09T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Estimating 3D geometry in underwater environments presents unique challenges due to light attenuation, scattering, and the absence of large-scale, high-quality 3D annotations. Pioneering methods rely on massive dense annotations that are impractical in underwater settings. In this paper, we propose Wat3R, a cross-domain semi-supervised learning framework designed to adapt feed-forward 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater scenes. Uniquely, our method eliminates the need for any annotated underwater data following a teacher-student architecture, that learns robust geometry representations merely on abundant unlabeled real underwater video footage. We also design a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to compensate for the information degradation in the current view caused by water attenuation and scattering. Furthermore, considering the lack of comprehensive evaluation benchmarks, we construct Water3D, a diverse dataset covering various water bodies and underwater scenarios, designed for geometric task evaluation. Experimental results demonstrate that Wat3R outperforms current state-of-the-art methods in underwater multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction. The dataset and code are available at https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R .
中文标题/摘要
标题：Wat3R：无需标注的水下3D几何学习
由于光照衰减、散射以及缺乏大规模高质量3D标注，水下环境中的3D几何估计面临独特挑战。现有开创性方法依赖大量密集标注，但在水下环境中不切实际。本文提出Wat3R，一种跨域半监督学习框架，旨在将面向空气场景的前馈3D重建模型适配至水下场景。独特的是，我们的方法在教师-学生架构下无需任何水下标注数据，仅通过大量未标注的真实水下视频即可学习鲁棒的几何表示。此外，我们设计了一种跨视角一致性损失，利用其他视角的几何线索补偿当前视角因水下衰减和散射导致的信息损失。考虑到缺乏全面评估基准，我们构建了Water3D数据集，涵盖多种水域和水下场景，专为几何任务评估设计。实验结果表明，Wat3R在水下多视角深度估计和点云重建任务上优于当前最先进方法。数据集和代码可在https://github.com/LSXI7/Wat3R获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the scarcity of annotated underwater 3D data and the challenges posed by light attenuation and scattering, Wat3R introduces a cross-domain semi-supervised framework that transfers 3D reconstruction models from air to underwater environments without requiring any labeled underwater data. It employs a teacher-student architecture trained solely on unlabeled underwater video, enhanced by a cross-view consistency loss that leverages geometric cues from other views to mitigate information loss due to water effects. Evaluated on the newly proposed Water3D dataset, Wat3R achieves superior performance in multi-view depth estimation and point cloud reconstruction compared to existing methods.
为应对水下环境光照衰减、散射及缺乏高质量标注数据的挑战，Wat3R提出一种跨域半监督学习框架，通过教师-学生架构将空气场景下的3D重建模型迁移到水下场景，且无需任何水下标注数据，仅利用大量未标注水下视频进行训练。该方法引入跨视角一致性损失，通过其他视角的几何信息补偿当前视角因水体影响导致的信息损失。在新构建的Water3D数据集上，Wat3R在多视角深度估计与点云重建任务中显著优于现有最先进方法。
ZipDepth: Bringing Lightweight Zero-Shot Monocular Depth Anywhere, on Any Device
Authors: Fabio Tosi, Luca Bartolomei, Matteo Poggi, Stefano Mattoccia
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-09T17:59:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:58+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026. Code: https://github.com/fabiotosi92/ZipDepth - Project page: https://zipdepth.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
Monocular depth estimation has seen remarkable progress through foundation models achieving robust zero-shot generalization, yet their computational demands place them far beyond the reach of embedded and mobile platforms. Lightweight alternatives exist, but have been developed almost exclusively within single-domain, self-supervised paradigms, failing silently under domain shift. We present ZipDepth, a compact monocular depth network that bridges this gap by combining an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model over a large multi-domain training set. Comprising just 6.1M parameters, ZipDepth runs at real-time rates from server GPUs to power-constrained devices, achieving the best trade-off between zero-shot accuracy and deployment efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, taking a significant step towards the accuracy of foundation models with 50x more parameters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ZipDepth：在任何设备上实现轻量级零样本单目深度估计
单目深度估计通过基础模型实现了卓越的零样本泛化能力，但其计算需求使其难以在嵌入式和移动平台上部署。虽然存在轻量级替代方案，但它们几乎仅在单一领域、自监督范式下开发，面对领域迁移时表现沉默失效。我们提出ZipDepth，一种紧凑的单目深度网络，通过结合高效的可重参数化编码器-解码器与从基础模型在大规模多领域训练集上进行的大规模知识蒸馏，弥合了这一差距。仅含610万参数，ZipDepth可在服务器GPU至功耗受限设备上实时运行，在五个基准测试中实现了轻量级模型中零样本精度与部署效率的最佳平衡，朝着参数量为其50倍的基础模型精度迈出了重要一步。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for lightweight, zero-shot monocular depth estimation deployable on resource-constrained devices, ZipDepth combines an efficient reparameterizable encoder-decoder with large-scale knowledge distillation from a foundation model trained across multiple domains. This approach enables real-time inference on devices ranging from server GPUs to mobile platforms, with only 6.1M parameters. Experimental results show ZipDepth achieves the best balance of zero-shot accuracy and computational efficiency among lightweight models across five benchmarks, significantly narrowing the performance gap with much larger foundation models.
为实现轻量级、零样本单目深度估计在资源受限设备上的部署，ZipDepth结合了可重参数化的高效编码器-解码器与从多域大规模数据集上训练的基础模型知识蒸馏。该方法仅需610万参数，可在从服务器GPU到移动设备上实时运行。在五个基准测试中，ZipDepth在轻量模型中实现了最佳的零样本精度与部署效率平衡，显著缩小了与参数量大50倍的基础模型之间的性能差距。
LongE2V: Long-Horizon Event-based Video Reconstruction, Prediction, and Frame Interpolation with Video Diffusion Models
Authors: Cheng-De Fan, Chun-Wei Tuan Mu, Chen-Wei Chang, Chin-Yang Lin, Kun-Ru Wu, Yu-Chee Tseng, Yu-Lun Liu
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-07-09T17:59:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:52+00:00
Comments: SIGGRAPH 2026. Project page: https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Recovering high-quality video from sparse event streams is a challenging task. Regression methods often blur textures, while existing generative models struggle with long-term stability. We propose LongE2V, a novel approach that leverages pre-trained video diffusion priors to jointly handle event-based video reconstruction, prediction, and frame interpolation. By fine-tuning a foundational video model, our approach achieves high data efficiency and superior perceptual quality. We introduce Autoregressive Unrolling and Adaptive Context Switching to mitigate temporal drift in extremely long sequences. We also propose Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction to ensure precise bidirectional consistency during frame interpolation. Furthermore, Event Voxel Density Augmentation ensures robustness across varying sensor resolutions. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate that LongE2V outperforms state-of-the-art methods across all three tasks, exhibiting exceptional temporal coherence and zero-shot generalization. Project page: https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/
中文标题/摘要
标题：LongE2V：基于视频扩散模型的长时程事件视频重建、预测与帧插值
从稀疏事件流中恢复高质量视频是一项具有挑战性的任务。回归方法常导致纹理模糊，而现有生成模型难以保持长期稳定性。我们提出LongE2V，一种利用预训练视频扩散先验的新方法，联合处理事件视频重建、预测与帧插值。通过微调基础视频模型，该方法实现高数据效率和卓越的感知质量。我们引入自回归展开与自适应上下文切换以缓解超长序列中的时间漂移，并提出跨残差校正的重编码对齐机制，确保帧插值过程中的双向一致性。此外，事件体素密度增强技术提升了模型在不同传感器分辨率下的鲁棒性。在真实世界基准测试中，LongE2V在三项任务上均优于现有最先进方法，展现出卓越的时间一致性与零样本泛化能力。项目页面：https://cdfan0627.github.io/LongE2V-page/
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reconstructing high-quality video from sparse event streams while maintaining long-term temporal stability, LongE2V introduces a video diffusion model fine-tuned with event-based data to jointly solve reconstruction, prediction, and frame interpolation. It employs Autoregressive Unrolling and Adaptive Context Switching to reduce temporal drift in long sequences, and Reencoding Alignment with Cross Residual Correction to ensure bidirectional consistency during interpolation. Event Voxel Density Augmentation further enhances robustness across sensor resolutions. Experimental results on real-world benchmarks show LongE2V achieves superior perceptual quality and temporal coherence, outperforming existing methods across all three tasks with strong zero-shot generalization.
为解决从稀疏事件流中重建高质量视频并保持长序列时间稳定性的挑战，LongE2V提出一种基于视频扩散模型的方法，通过事件数据微调以联合处理重建、预测与帧插值。其采用自回归展开与自适应上下文切换缓解长序列时序漂移，结合重编码对齐与跨残差校正确保插值过程双向一致性，并通过事件体素密度增强提升对传感器分辨率变化的鲁棒性。实验表明，该方法在真实基准测试中全面超越现有方法，展现出卓越的时间一致性与零样本泛化能力。
Play2Perfect: What Matters in Dexterous Play Pretraining for Precise Assembly?
Authors: Tyler Ga Wei Lum, Kushal Kedia, C. Karen Liu, Jeannette Bohg
First: 2026-06-24T22:39:12+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:45+00:00
Comments: 22 pages, 12 figures, 4 tables. Project page: https://play2perfect.github.io
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Multi-fingered robots promise the speed and dexterity of human hands, yet challenging problems such as precise assembly have remained out of reach. These tasks are contact-rich, making data collection for imitation learning difficult, and sparse-reward, making direct exploration with reinforcement learning (RL) intractable. Consequently, prior work has made progress by structuring the problem with specialized grippers, tool attachments, and environment fixtures. In this work, we argue that before a robot can perfect precise assembly, it must first learn to play. We further ask the question: what factors in the process of learning to play matter for precise assembly? We propose Play2Perfect, an RL framework for task-agnostic pretraining through play on diverse objects and goals, which is then perfected on precise assembly. The goal of play is to acquire reusable manipulation priors, such as grasping, in-hand reorientation and pose reaching. Finetuning then adapts this general prior to assembly, focusing exploration on the final contact-rich, high-precision interactions needed for success. We systematically study key design choices in play pretraining, including object diversity, training objective, trajectory diversity, and goal precision. We show that our prior is 33x more sample-efficient than RL training from scratch, even when provided with dense, multi-stage rewards. We demonstrate zero-shot sim-to-real transfer, achieving 60% success on tight insertions with only 0.5 mm contact clearance, and over 50% success on long-horizon multi-part assembly and screwing.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Play2Perfect：灵巧操作预训练中哪些因素对精密装配至关重要？
多指机器人有望实现人类手部的速度与灵巧性，但精密装配等挑战性任务仍难以攻克。这类任务接触密集，模仿学习的数据收集困难，且奖励稀疏，直接使用强化学习（RL）探索不可行。因此，以往研究通过专用夹爪、工具附件和环境固定装置来结构化问题。本文我们认为，机器人在实现精密装配前，必须先学会“玩”。我们进一步提出问题：在学习“玩”的过程中，哪些因素对精密装配至关重要？我们提出Play2Perfect，一种通过在多样物体和目标上进行无任务依赖的“玩”式预训练，再针对精密装配进行精调的RL框架。玩的目标是获取可复用的操作先验，如抓取、手内重定向和姿态到达。精调则将此通用先验适配至装配任务，聚焦于最终接触密集、高精度交互所需的探索。我们系统研究了玩式预训练中的关键设计选择，包括物体多样性、训练目标、轨迹多样性及目标精度。结果显示，即使提供密集的多阶段奖励，我们的先验比从零开始的RL训练样本效率高33倍。我们实现了零样本仿真到现实迁移，在仅0.5毫米接触间隙的紧装配任务中成功率60%，在长时程多部件装配与拧螺丝任务中成功率超50%。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of precise assembly with multi-fingered robots, this work proposes Play2Perfect, an RL framework that first pretrains robots through diverse, task-agnostic play to acquire general manipulation priors like grasping and in-hand reorientation, then fine-tunes them for assembly tasks. The motivation stems from the difficulty of collecting data for contact-rich, sparse-reward assembly tasks, which prior methods circumvent by using specialized tools or fixtures. The method systematically evaluates design choices in play pretraining, including object variety, reward structure, trajectory diversity, and goal precision, and then adapts the learned priors to assembly via targeted exploration. Experimental results show the approach is 33x more sample-efficient than training from scratch, even with dense rewards, and achieves 60% success on tight insertions with 0.5 mm clearance and over 50% success on complex multi-part assembly tasks.
为了解决多指机器人在接触密集、奖励稀疏的精密装配任务中表现不佳的问题，该研究提出Play2Perfect框架：通过在多样物体和目标上进行无任务导向的“玩耍”预训练，使机器人习得通用操作先验（如抓取与在手重定向），再针对性微调以适应精密装配。实验系统评估了物体多样性、训练目标、轨迹多样性等设计因素，结果表明该方法比从零开始训练效率高33倍，即使在密集奖励下，仍能在0.5毫米间隙插入任务中达成60%成功率，并在多部件装配与拧螺丝任务中实现超50%成功率。
Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning for Panoramic Outdoor Reconstruction
Authors: Weijian Chen, Weibo Yao, Yuhang Zhang, Xiaolin Tang, Guo Wang, Weijun Zhang, Xitong Gao, Yihao Chen, Hongde Qin, Lu Qi
First: 2026-07-09T17:59:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:43+00:00
Comments: Project Webpage: https://insta360-research-team.github.io/GGPS-Website
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to large outdoor scenes is costly in both data acquisition and computation. Adopting panoramic images with equirectangular projection (ERP) can reduce capture effort via their full $360^{\circ}$ field of view, yet the resulting omnipresent visibility invalidates existing partitioning strategies that rely on local camera frustums, causing block-wise optimization to degenerate into global training. Thus, we propose PanoLOG, a two-stage coarse-to-fine framework equipped with a Geometry and Gradient-based Partitioning Strategy tailored for large-scale panoramic 3DGS reconstruction. In the global coarse stage, PanoLOG leverages sky-sphere modeling and panoramic monocular depth supervision for reliable geometry, while in the refinement stage, G$^2$PS builds adaptive bounding volumes via parallax-driven uncertainty and assigns cameras via gradient-based importance scoring. Furthermore, we construct Pano360, the first benchmark on large-scale panoramic dataset for outdoor scene reconstruction. Extensive experiments demonstrate that G$^2$PS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while maintaining scalable, block-parallel training. Our models, training code, and dataset are publicly available.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于全景户外重建的几何与梯度引导的分区方法
将3D高斯点渲染（3DGS）扩展至大型户外场景，在数据采集和计算上成本高昂。采用具有等距圆柱投影（ERP）的全景图像可通过其360°视野减少采集工作量，但由此产生的全场景可见性使依赖局部相机视锥体的现有分区策略失效，导致块状优化退化为全局训练。因此，我们提出PanoLOG，一种两阶段由粗到精的框架，配备专为大规模全景3DGS重建设计的几何与梯度引导分区策略。在全局粗阶段，PanoLOG利用天球建模和全景单目深度监督实现可靠的几何结构；在精炼阶段，G²PS通过视差驱动的不确定性构建自适应包围体，并通过梯度引导的重要性评分分配相机。此外，我们构建了Pano360，这是首个针对大规模全景数据集的户外场景重建基准。大量实验表明，G²PS在保持可扩展、块并行训练的同时实现了最先进的渲染质量。我们的模型、训练代码和数据集均已公开。
Summary / 总结
To address the computational and data acquisition challenges of scaling 3D Gaussian Splatting to large outdoor scenes, this work introduces PanoLOG, a two-stage framework using geometry and gradient-based partitioning tailored for panoramic equirectangular images. The global coarse stage employs sky-sphere modeling and monocular depth supervision to establish reliable geometry, while the refinement stage uses G$^2$PS to construct adaptive bounding volumes based on parallax-driven uncertainty and assign cameras via gradient-based importance scoring. Experimental results on the newly proposed Pano360 benchmark show that G$^2$PS achieves state-of-the-art rendering quality while enabling scalable, block-parallel training without degenerating into global optimization.
为解决3D高斯点云在大规模户外场景中数据采集与计算成本高的问题，本文提出PanoLOG框架，结合几何与梯度引导的分区策略，专为全景等距投影图像设计。全局粗阶段通过天空球建模与单目深度监督建立可靠几何结构，精炼阶段则利用G$^2$PS根据视差驱动的不确定性自适应生成包围体，并通过梯度重要性评分分配相机。在新构建的Pano360基准数据集上，实验表明G$^2$PS在保持可扩展块并行训练的同时，实现了当前最优的渲染质量。
UniClawBench: A Universal Benchmark for Proactive Agents on Real-World Tasks
Authors: Zhekai Chen, Chengqi Duan, Kaiyue Sun, Bohao Li, Yuqing Wang, Manyuan Zhang, Xihui Liu
First: 2026-07-09T17:59:32+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:32+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://uniclawbench.github.io | GitHub Repo: https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
The rapid development of large language models and multimodal large language models has accelerated the emergence of proactive agents capable of operating everyday tools and assisting users in real-world environments. However, existing benchmarks struggle to evaluate such agents effectively, as they often rely on sandboxed environments and single-turn evaluation paradigms. Moreover, their scenario-based task taxonomies mix multiple model capabilities within the same task category, making it difficult to identify the root causes of agent failures. To address these limitations, we introduce UniClawBench, the first capability-driven benchmark designed to evaluate proactive agents in dynamic, real-world settings. UniClawBench is built around five foundational model capabilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Based on these capabilities, we design 400 bilingual real-world tasks. Unlike previous benchmarks that rely on static, pre-recorded answers, our benchmark evaluates agents in live Docker containers using fine-grained, step-by-step completion checkpoints. Furthermore, we design a closed-loop evaluation strategy comprising an executor agent, a hidden supervisor agent, and a user agent to simulate realistic multi-turn human feedback without leaking grading criteria. To disentangle base model capabilities from framework-level design choices, we evaluate state-of-the-art models under multiple agent frameworks. Through comprehensive comparisons across both models and frameworks, we show how base model capabilities and agent framework designs jointly shape performance in real-world environments. To facilitate future research, we make our benchmark and code publicly available at https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UniClawBench：面向现实世界任务的主动代理通用基准
大语言模型和多模态大语言模型的快速发展加速了能够操作日常工具并协助用户在现实环境中工作的主动代理的出现。然而，现有基准难以有效评估此类代理，因其常依赖沙箱环境和单轮评估范式。此外，其基于场景的任务分类将多种模型能力混杂于同一任务类别中，难以定位代理失败的根本原因。为解决这些局限，我们提出UniClawBench，这是首个面向动态现实环境评估主动代理的能力驱动基准。UniClawBench围绕五大基础模型能力构建：技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解与跨平台协调。基于这些能力，我们设计了400个双语现实世界任务。与以往依赖静态预录答案的基准不同，我们的基准在运行中的Docker容器中评估代理，并采用细粒度、分步完成检查点。此外，我们设计了一种闭环评估策略，包含执行者代理、隐藏监督代理与用户代理，以模拟真实多轮人类反馈而不泄露评分标准。为分离基础模型能力与框架级设计选择的影响，我们在多个代理框架下评估最先进模型。通过跨模型与框架的全面比较，我们展示了基础模型能力与代理框架设计如何共同塑造现实环境中的性能表现。为促进未来研究，我们已将基准与代码公开于https://github.com/HKU-MMLab/UniClawBench。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate proactive agents in real-world settings beyond sandboxed environments, UniClawBench introduces a capability-driven benchmark with 400 bilingual tasks designed to assess five core model abilities: Skill Usage, Exploration, Long-Context Reasoning, Multimodal Understanding, and Cross-Platform Coordination. Unlike prior benchmarks, it employs live Docker containers and step-by-step checkpoint evaluation to simulate dynamic, multi-turn interactions, using a closed-loop system with executor, supervisor, and user agents to mimic human feedback without leaking criteria. Experimental results show that agent performance is jointly determined by base model capabilities and framework design, with evaluations across multiple frameworks revealing how architectural choices influence real-world task success.
为评估在真实动态环境中运作的主动代理，作者提出了UniClawBench，一个以能力为导向的基准测试，涵盖技能使用、探索、长上下文推理、多模态理解与跨平台协调五大核心能力。该基准包含400个双语真实世界任务，通过在运行中的Docker容器中进行分步检查点评估，避免静态预录答案。采用由执行者代理、隐藏监督者代理和用户代理组成的闭环评估机制，模拟真实人类反馈而不泄露评分标准。实验表明，基础模型能力与代理框架设计共同决定实际表现，跨多个框架的对比揭示了架构选择与模型能力如何协同影响真实环境中的性能。
OPSD-V: On-Policy Self-Distillation for Post-Training Few-Step Autoregressive Video Generators
Authors: Hongyu Liu, Chun Wang, Feng Gao, Xuanhua He, Yue Ma, Ziyu Wan, Yong Zhang, Xiaoming Wei, Qifeng Chen
First: 2026-07-09T17:59:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:11+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://meigen-ai.github.io/OPSD-V ; Code: https://github.com/MeiGen-AI/OPSD-V
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3 · Project1
Abstract
We propose OPSD-V, an on-policy self-distillation paradigm for post-training few-step autoregressive (AR) video diffusion models. Existing few-step AR video generators can produce long videos with low latency, but still suffer from error accumulation and weakened motion dynamics during long autoregressive rollout. OPSD-V reduces long-horizon degradation while preserving the original few-step inference path. The key idea is to introduce real long-video data as temporal context during training and use it to provide dense trajectory-level supervision. Specifically, the student follows the exact inference-time rollout, generating each chunk conditioned on its own previously generated KV cache. In parallel, the teacher is evaluated at the same student-visited denoising states, but uses a cleaner AR-consistent temporal cache in which older history can be replaced by real-video context. This provides dense denoising-level corrective targets under on-policy AR cache dynamics, without changing the sampler, number of denoising steps, or inference-time cache mechanism. We apply OPSD-V to representative few-step AR video models, including Self-Forcing and LongLive. Experiments show consistent improvements in visual quality, motion dynamics, and VBenchLong scores. A user study with 10 participants comparing 20 video pairs shows that OPSD-V is preferred over the base models in 66.0% of overall-preference judgments (82.5% excluding ties).
中文标题/摘要
标题：OPSD-V：用于后训练少步自回归视频生成器的在线自蒸馏方法
我们提出OPSD-V，一种针对后训练少步自回归（AR）视频扩散模型的在线自蒸馏范式。现有少步AR视频生成器虽能以低延迟生成长视频，但仍存在误差累积和长序列自回归推演中运动动态减弱的问题。OPSD-V在减少长时程退化的同时保留原始少步推理路径。核心思想是在训练中引入真实长视频数据作为时间上下文，并用其提供密集的轨迹级监督。具体而言，学生模型严格遵循推理时的推演路径，每生成一个片段均基于自身先前生成的KV缓存；同时，教师模型在相同的学生访问去噪状态下评估，但使用更干净的AR一致时间缓存，其中旧历史可被真实视频上下文替代。该方法在不改变采样器、去噪步数或推理时缓存机制的前提下，提供密集的去噪级修正目标。我们将在代表性少步AR视频模型（如Self-Forcing和LongLive）上应用OPSD-V。实验表明，视觉质量、运动动态及VBenchLong评分均显著提升。10名用户在比较20对视频时，66.0%的整体偏好判断倾向于OPSD-V（排除平局后为82.5%）。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the error accumulation and degraded motion dynamics in long autoregressive video generation, OPSD-V introduces an on-policy self-distillation framework that leverages real long-video data as temporal context during training to provide dense trajectory-level supervision. The method trains a student model to follow the exact inference rollout while using its own KV cache, while a teacher model, evaluated at the same denoising states, benefits from a cleaner temporal cache incorporating real-video context. This setup enables corrective supervision without altering the inference pipeline. Experiments on Self-Forcing and LongLive show consistent gains in visual quality, motion dynamics, and VBenchLong scores, with user preference tests indicating OPSD-V outperformed base models in 66.0% of judgments.
为解决少步自回归视频生成中误差累积和运动动态减弱的问题，OPSD-V提出一种基于真实长视频数据的在线自蒸馏框架。该方法在训练中让学生模型按原推理路径生成，依赖自身KV缓存；教师模型则在相同去噪状态下，使用替换旧历史的干净时间缓存（含真实视频上下文）提供密集校正目标。实验表明，该方法在视觉质量、运动动态和VBenchLong评分上均有提升，用户偏好测试显示其在66.0%的对比中更受青睐。
Vision-Language Memory for Spatial Reasoning
Authors: Zuntao Liu, Yi Du, Taimeng Fu, Shaoshu Su, Cherie Ho, Chen Wang
Venue: ECCV
First: 2025-11-25T18:59:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted to European Conference on Computer Vision (ECCV), 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Spatial reasoning is a critical capability for intelligent robots, yet current vision-language models (VLMs) still fall short of human-level performance in video-based spatial reasoning. This gap mainly stems from two challenges: a semantic-geometric misalignment that prevents consistent 3D understanding, and the absence of persistent memory to retain 3D representation and understanding across frames. To address these limitations, we present VLM$^2$, a Vision-Language Model with persistent Memory for spatial reasoning with a view-consistent, 3D-aware representation purely from 2D videos. Specifically, we incorporate a dual-memory module consisting of a working memory that operates as a sliding window to focus on immediate context, and an episodic memory that consolidates and stores critical information across frames. This design enables bounded and efficient spatial reasoning under a fixed computational cost. Extensive experiments on multiple benchmarks show that VLM$^2$ achieves state-of-the-art performance among video-based models, significantly advancing the frontier of visual-spatial intelligence.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于空间推理的视觉-语言记忆
空间推理是智能机器人的关键能力，但当前的视觉-语言模型（VLMs）在基于视频的空间推理中仍远未达到人类水平。这一差距主要源于两个挑战：语义-几何对齐不足，导致无法建立一致的3D理解；以及缺乏持久记忆，无法在帧间保留3D表示和理解。为解决这些问题，我们提出了VLM²，一种专为空间推理设计、仅从2D视频中构建视图一致、3D感知表示的视觉-语言模型，具备持久记忆。具体而言，我们引入了双记忆模块：工作记忆作为滑动窗口聚焦即时上下文，事件记忆则整合并存储跨帧的关键信息。该设计在固定计算成本下实现了有界且高效的时空推理。在多个基准测试中，VLM²取得了视频模型中的最先进性能，显著推动了视觉-空间智能的发展。
Summary / 总结
To bridge the gap between current vision-language models and human-level spatial reasoning in video, VLM$^2$ introduces a dual-memory architecture that maintains persistent 3D-aware representations across frames, addressing semantic-geometric misalignment and lack of memory. The model combines a working memory for immediate context via a sliding window and an episodic memory for long-term consolidation, enabling efficient, bounded reasoning without increasing computational cost. Experimental results across multiple benchmarks demonstrate state-of-the-art performance among video-based models, significantly improving spatial reasoning capabilities.
为弥合当前视觉语言模型与人类在视频中空间推理能力的差距，VLM$^2$提出双记忆架构，通过保持跨帧的3D感知表示，解决语义-几何错位和缺乏记忆的问题。该模型结合滑动窗口式的工况记忆处理即时上下文，以及用于长期整合的关键信息存储的事件记忆，实现固定计算成本下的高效有界推理。在多个基准测试中，VLM$^2$在视频模型中达到最先进性能，显著推动视觉空间智能的发展。
Enhancing In-context Panoramic Generation via Geometric-aware Pretraining
Authors: Haoran Feng, Ruiyang Zhang, Longyi Zhang, Dizhe Zhang, Lu Qi
First: 2026-07-09T17:59:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:59:06+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
In this work, we present Canvas360, a two-stage framework for in-context panoramic generation that combines geometry-aware pretraining with downstream task-specific fine-tuning. To address the lack of large-scale, high-quality training data tailored to in-context panoramic tasks, we propose Canvas360Dataset, a collection of 1M high-quality paired panoramic samples for style transfer, inpainting, outpainting, and editing, enabling effective supervision across diverse in-context generation scenarios. On the modeling side, Canvas360 enhances text-to-panorama generation through parallel depth generation, velocity circular padding, and similarity loss regularization, enabling the model to learn geometry-aware representations, capture object distortion details, and improve geometric consistency and global coherence. Furthermore, empowered by strong panoramic priors, Canvas360 enables a unified in-context panoramic generation framework that supports diverse downstream tasks via token-level concatenation, surpassing prior methods in both task coverage and modeling flexibility. Extensive experiments show that Canvas360 improves panoramic image fidelity, achieving particularly strong performance on the panorama-specific FAED metric and competitive or leading results across the reported quantitative evaluations. More information can be found on our project page: https://zry000.github.io/Canvas360/

Motivated by the scarcity of high-quality, task-specific training data for in-context panoramic generation, this work introduces Canvas360, a two-stage framework combining geometry-aware pretraining with task-specific fine-tuning. It leverages Canvas360Dataset—a 1M-sample dataset covering style transfer, inpainting, outpainting, and editing—to enable robust supervision. The model employs parallel depth generation, velocity circular padding, and similarity loss regularization to learn geometric consistency and object distortion details. Experimental results show Canvas360 significantly improves panoramic fidelity, achieving top performance on the FAED metric and competitive or leading results across multiple quantitative benchmarks, while supporting diverse downstream tasks through token-level concatenation.

OpenCoF: Learning to Reason Through Video Generation
Authors: Xinyan Chen, Ziyu Guo, Renrui Zhang, Dongzhi Jiang, Hongsheng Li
First: 2026-07-09T17:58:29+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:58:29+00:00
Comments: Project Page: https://opencof.github.io/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Reasoning has become a core capability for large models, especially when reliable decisions require understanding logical consequences. Recent video generation models offer a reasoning path distinct from previous Chain-of-Thought (CoT): reasoning can unfold through temporally connected frames, known as Chain-of-Frame (CoF) reasoning. However, existing video generators are primarily trained on general video corpora, still lacking diverse supervision and dedicated designs for CoF reasoning. To address this gap, we introduce OpenCoF, a framework comprising the OpenCoF-17K dataset, a reasoning video dataset spanning 11 task families, and Wan-CoF, a fine-tuned video model for studying whether diverse temporal supervision improves CoF behavior. Across four video reasoning benchmarks, Wan-CoF achieves considerable gains over the Wan2.2-I2V-A14B baseline. Building on this, we empirically explore more advanced designs for CoF capabilities, i.e., equipping the model with visual and textual reasoning tokens. This mechanism respectively captures low-level visual cues and high-level semantic priors for spatial and temporal reasoning. Through performance comparisons and attention analysis, we examine how these tokens contribute across model depth, denoising steps, space, and time. Our results suggest that stronger video reasoning requires both broad temporal supervision and explicit mechanisms for organizing intermediate reasoning state. We open-source the dataset, model, and code to facilitate future research on reasoning-oriented video generation.

Experiments across four video reasoning benchmarks show Wan-CoF significantly outperforms the baseline Wan2.2-I2V-A14B, and attention analysis reveals that both broad temporal supervision and explicit reasoning token mechanisms are critical for enhancing CoF capabilities.
为提升视频生成模型的推理能力，该研究提出OpenCoF框架，包含一个涵盖11类任务的大型视频推理数据集（OpenCoF-17K）和一个微调后的视频模型（Wan-CoF），用于研究帧间推理（CoF）。方法通过在具有时间结构的视频数据上训练模型，并引入视觉与文本推理标记以捕捉空间与时间推理线索。在四个视频推理基准测试中，Wan-CoF显著优于基线模型Wan2.2-I2V-A14B，且注意力分析表明，广泛的时间监督与显式的推理状态组织机制共同构成高效CoF推理的关键。
Ideas Have Genomes: Benchmarking Scientific Lineage Reasoning and Lineage-Grounded Idea Generation
Authors: Yifan Zhou, Qihao Yang, Yan Li, Donggang Li, Xiru Hu, Hokin Deng, Ziyang Gong, Xuanyi Zhou, Huacan Wang, Xiangchao Yan, Wanghan Xu, Wenlong Zhang, Shaofeng Zhang, Yue Zhou, Yifan Yang, Zhihang Zhong, Xue Yang
First: 2026-07-09T17:55:53+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:55:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific ideas rarely start from a blank page. They inherit mechanisms, repair known limitations, and recombine pieces of earlier work, much like biological genomes. Current benchmarks still say little about whether AI systems can follow this inheritance structure. We present IdeaGene-Bench (IG-Bench), a benchmark for scientific lineage reasoning and lineage-grounded idea generation. IG-Bench is organized around the IdeaGene framework: each paper or proposal is represented as a set of minimal, typed, evidence-grounded Idea Genome objects, and a GenomeDiff aligns these objects to record inheritance, mutation, loss, external import, and novel insertion under six operational evolutionary dynamics. The benchmark contains 1,961 golden lineage traces, 1,085 curated Idea Genome objects, and 920 pairwise GenomeDiff records across 10 scientific domains. It supports two evaluations. IG-Exam (42 task types, 1,029 instances) tests closed-form lineage reasoning across Idea Genome abstraction, inheritance tracing, evolutionary reasoning, and lineage verification. IG-Arena evaluates generation with a lineage-conditioned Population-Evolution Score(PES), asking whether a proposal can be inserted as a coherent descendant of a given lineage population: it should inherit the right Idea Genome objects, vary meaningfully from nearby work, and offer selection value for future research. Experiments on 14 LLM-based scientists expose a compositional bottleneck. The strongest system reaches only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context reshuffles system rankings rather than helping every participant uniformly.
中文标题/摘要
标题：思想具有基因组：科学谱系推理与谱系锚定创意生成的基准测试
科学思想很少从空白开始，它们继承机制、修复已知局限并重组先前工作的片段，类似于生物基因组。当前基准仍无法有效评估AI系统是否能遵循这种继承结构。我们提出了IdeaGene-Bench（IG-Bench），用于科学谱系推理与谱系锚定创意生成的基准测试。IG-Bench基于IdeaGene框架构建：每篇论文或提案被表示为一组最小化、类型化、证据锚定的Idea Genome对象，GenomeDiff则对这些对象进行对齐，记录六种操作性进化动态下的继承、突变、丢失、外部引入和新颖插入。该基准包含1,961条黄金谱系轨迹、1,085个精选Idea Genome对象及920组成对的GenomeDiff记录，覆盖10个科学领域。它支持两项评估：IG-Exam（42种任务类型，1,029个实例）测试在Idea Genome抽象层上的闭式谱系推理、谱系追溯、进化推理与谱系验证；IG-Arena则通过谱系条件化的种群进化评分（PES）评估创意生成，判断提案能否作为给定谱系种群的连贯后代：应继承正确的Idea Genome对象、与邻近工作有显著差异，并为未来研究提供选择价值。在14个基于LLM的科学家系统上的实验揭示了组合瓶颈：最强系统在谱系推理上的精确率仅为27.3%，结构化谱系上下文反而打乱系统排名，而非对所有参与者均等提升。

Motivated by the need to evaluate AI systems' ability to understand scientific ideas as evolving lineages akin to biological genomes, the paper introduces IG-Bench, a benchmark that models scientific papers as 'Idea Genome' objects and tracks their evolutionary dynamics via 'GenomeDiff' alignments. The benchmark includes 1,961 lineage traces, 1,085 Idea Genome objects, and 920 GenomeDiff records across 10 domains, supporting two evaluations: IG-Exam for lineage reasoning and IG-Arena for lineage-grounded idea generation. Experiments with 14 LLM-based systems reveal a compositional bottleneck, with the best model achieving only 27.3% exact accuracy on lineage reasoning, and structured lineage context often reshuffling performance rather than uniformly aiding all models.
为评估AI系统是否能理解科学思想如同生物基因组般演化传承，该研究构建了IG-Bench基准，将科学论文建模为‘思想基因组’对象，并通过‘基因组差异’对演化动态进行追踪。该基准包含1961条谱系轨迹、1085个思想基因组对象及920条基因组差异记录，覆盖十个领域，支持两项评估：IG-Exam测试42种任务类型下的谱系推理准确性，IG-Arena则评估基于谱系上下文的创意生成能力。14个基于大语言模型的系统实验表明存在组合瓶颈，最强系统仅在谱系推理上达到27.3%的精确率，且结构化谱系上下文常导致系统排名重新洗牌，而非普遍提升表现。
Score Accuracy Along the Forward Diffusion Does Not Certify Numerical Stability in Diffusion Sampling
Authors: Yiwei Zhou
First: 2026-07-09T17:55:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:55:52+00:00
Comments: 27 pages, 2 figures, 1 table
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Score matching controls average error under the forward marginals, but a discretized reverse-time sampler evaluates the learned score along its own trajectory. We show that small forward-marginal error does not guarantee numerical stability. We construct a single smooth score field with arbitrarily small forward-marginal $L^2$ error. The learned reverse-time process is nonexplosive, has moments of every order, and can be arbitrarily close to the exact reverse-time process in path-space total variation. Yet its Euler--Maruyama discretizations converge in probability while every positive moment diverges. Thus weak convergence can hold even though every Wasserstein distance $W_p$, $p\ge1$, diverges. The same failure can occur within one fixed finite neural architecture. We construct a family of bounded, globally Lipschitz denoisers for which both the forward-marginal error and the path-space total variation distance tend to zero, while their Euler--Maruyama endpoints diverge in every $W_p$. For compactly supported data, we also give a simple positive result. Projecting the learned denoiser onto a known bounded closed convex set containing the support preserves pointwise accuracy, gives grid-uniform moment bounds, and yields Wasserstein convergence under mild local regularity. Experiments with a small fixed DiT-style network show large growth along rare numerical trajectories and its suppression by denoiser projection, while overall trajectory errors remain small.
中文标题/摘要
标题：前向扩散过程中得分精度无法保证扩散采样数值稳定性
得分匹配控制前向边际的平均误差，但离散化的逆时间采样器沿其自身轨迹评估所学得分。我们证明，前向边际误差小并不能保证数值稳定性。我们构造了一个单个光滑得分场，其前向边际L²误差可任意小。所学逆时间过程是非爆炸的，具有任意阶矩，且在路径空间总变差距离上可任意接近精确逆时间过程。然而，其Euler-Maruyama离散化在概率上收敛，但每个正阶矩均发散。因此，弱收敛可成立，尽管每个Wasserstein距离W_p（p≥1）均发散。 相同失效现象可在单一固定有限神经网络架构内发生。我们构造了一族有界、全局Lipschitz去噪器，其前向边际误差和路径空间总变差距离均趋于零，但其Euler-Maruyama终点在每个W_p中均发散。对于紧支撑数据，我们还给出一个简单正向结果：将所学去噪器投影到包含支撑集的已知有界闭凸集上，可保持逐点精度，提供网格均匀矩界，并在局部正则性较弱条件下实现Wasserstein收敛。实验表明，小型固定DiT风格网络在罕见数值轨迹上误差显著增长，但通过去噪器投影可抑制该增长，而整体轨迹误差仍保持较小。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether small score-matching errors along the forward diffusion process guarantee numerical stability in reverse-time sampling. The authors demonstrate that even with arbitrarily small forward-marginal L² error, the reverse-time Euler-Maruyama discretization can exhibit divergent moments despite weak convergence, meaning Wasserstein distances diverge for all p≥1. They construct smooth score fields and bounded Lipschitz denoisers that achieve vanishing forward error and path-space distance yet produce divergent endpoint distributions. For compactly supported data, projecting the denoiser onto a convex set containing the support restores moment bounds and ensures Wasserstein convergence. Experiments on a small DiT-style network confirm that while rare trajectories show large errors, overall trajectory errors remain small after projection.
该论文探讨了前向扩散过程中得分匹配误差小是否能保证反向采样数值稳定性。作者构造了一个光滑得分场，其前向边际L²误差可任意小，但其欧拉-马鲁雅玛离散化在所有Wasserstein距离中发散，尽管弱收敛成立。进一步证明，即使在固定神经网络架构下，存在有界全局利普希茨去噪器，其前向误差和路径空间总变差距离趋于零，但终点在所有Wasserstein距离中发散。对于紧支撑数据，作者给出正向结果：将去噪器投影到包含支撑集的凸闭集上，可保持点态精度、提供网格均匀矩界，并在局部正则性条件下实现Wasserstein收敛。实验表明，小DiT风格网络在罕见路径上误差显著增长，但投影可抑制该现象而不影响整体轨迹误差。
MulTTiPop: A Multitrack Transcription Dataset for Pop Music
Authors: Nathan Pruyne, Benjamin Stoler, William Chen, Chien-yu Huang, Shinji Watanabe, Chris Donahue
First: 2026-07-09T17:55:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:55:33+00:00
Comments: 8 pages, 4 figures. Associated web preview available at https://gclef-cmu.org/multtipop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present MulTTiPop, a dataset of pop music segments and their associated multitrack MIDI recordings for the evaluation of automatic music transcription models. MulTTiPop contains 572 segments of popular music totaling 3.5 hours of audio, and contains songs from diverse genres and decades from the 1930s to 2000s. To collect this dataset, we perform metadata-based matching on song segments from the Lakh MIDI and TheoryTab datasets, manually identify an anchor beat between the audio and MIDI, then use beat tracking on the audio and warp the MIDI to match its tempo and timing. We evaluate state-of-the-art automatic music transcription models on MulTTiPop and find substantial room for improvement, with the best model achieving 38% Onset F1. More details and sound examples of MulTTiPop are available at https://gclef-cmu.org/multtipop.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MulTTiPop：流行音乐多轨道转录数据集
我们提出了MulTTiPop数据集，包含流行音乐片段及其关联的多轨道MIDI录音，用于评估自动音乐转录模型。该数据集包含572个流行音乐片段，总时长3.5小时，涵盖1930年代至2000年代多种风格的歌曲。为收集该数据集，我们基于元数据匹配Lakh MIDI和TheoryTab数据集中的歌曲片段，手动确定音频与MIDI之间的锚点节拍，再通过音频节拍跟踪并调整MIDI以匹配其节奏和时序。我们在MulTTiPop上评估了当前最先进的自动音乐转录模型，发现仍有显著改进空间，最佳模型的起始点F1分数仅为38%。更多详情和音频示例请访问https://gclef-cmu.org/multtipop。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a standardized benchmark to evaluate automatic music transcription models, the authors introduce MulTTiPop, a multitrack MIDI dataset for pop music segments. The dataset comprises 572 audio clips totaling 3.5 hours, spanning diverse genres and decades from the 1930s to 2000s, constructed by aligning audio and MIDI using metadata matching, manual anchor beat identification, and beat-tracking-based warping. Evaluation of state-of-the-art models on this dataset reveals significant performance gaps, with the top-performing model achieving only 38% Onset F1, indicating substantial room for improvement in transcription accuracy.
为评估自动音乐转录模型，作者构建了MulTTiPop数据集，包含572段流行音乐音频及其多轨MIDI记录，总时长约3.5小时，涵盖1930至2000年代多种风格音乐。数据通过元数据匹配、人工锚点节拍定位及基于节拍追踪的MIDI时间对齐构建。在该数据集上评估现有顶尖模型，结果显示性能仍有较大提升空间，最佳模型仅达到38%的起始点F1分数。
OmniOPSD: Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation for Affective Computing
Authors: Zebang Cheng, Shuimu Chen, Boxue Yang, Yuanshen Guan, Jingyi Chen, Zheng Lian, Xiaojiang Peng, Fei Ma, LaiZhong Cui, Qi Tian
First: 2026-06-14T16:51:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:53:09+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning for multimodal large language models (MLLMs) is often hindered by severe reward sparsity in complex reasoning tasks. This challenge is particularly pronounced in human-centered scenarios involving states, emotions, intentions, and behaviors, where heterogeneous multimodal signals and subjective human factors make high-quality chain-of-thought (CoT) annotations expensive and difficult to obtain. Although many multimodal datasets provide expert-annotated ground-truth labels, directly using these labels for supervised fine-tuning may encourage shortcut learning in multimodal perception and provides limited transparency for safety-critical human--AI interaction. To address these limitations, we propose OmniOPSD, a Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation framework that uses frontier-generated rationales as teacher-side privileged evidence rather than student imitation targets. OmniOPSD uses frontier-generated evidence-aware rationales only as training-time privileged evidence context for a local teacher. The student samples its own rollout from the original multimodal input, while the rationale-privileged teacher scores the same tokens and provides dense token-level supervision. Thus, the student learns on its own trajectory distribution without directly imitating frontier-model completions, and inference requires no labels, rationales, CoT annotations, or closed-source model access. Experiments on MER-UniBench show that OmniOPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average score of $84.19$, and ablations further support the value of rationale-privileged teacher guidance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：OmniOPSD：面向情感计算的基于理由特权的在线自蒸馏方法
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）的强化学习常因复杂推理任务中奖励稀疏而受阻。在涉及状态、情绪、意图和行为的人类中心场景中，异构多模态信号与主观人类因素使高质量思维链（CoT）标注昂贵且难以获取。尽管许多多模态数据集提供专家标注的真实标签，但直接用于监督微调可能促使多模态感知出现捷径学习，并对安全关键的人机交互缺乏透明度。为解决这些问题，我们提出OmniOPSD框架，该框架将前沿模型生成的理由作为教师端特权证据而非学生模仿目标。OmniOPSD仅在训练时将前沿模型生成的带感知理由作为本地教师的特权证据上下文。学生从原始多模态输入中采样自身轨迹，而特权理由教师对相同token进行评分并提供密集的token级监督。因此，学生在不直接模仿前沿模型完成结果的情况下学习自身轨迹分布，且推理无需标签、理由、CoT标注或闭源模型访问。在MER-UniBench上的实验表明，OmniOPSD平均得分达84.19，达到最先进水平，消融实验进一步验证了特权理由教师指导的价值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the challenge of reward sparsity in reinforcement learning for multimodal large language models, especially in human-centered affective computing tasks where high-quality chain-of-thought annotations are scarce and costly, the paper introduces OmniOPSD: a Rationale-Privileged On-Policy Self-Distillation framework. It leverages frontier-model-generated rationales as privileged teacher evidence during training, not as imitation targets, allowing the student to learn from its own rollout while receiving dense token-level supervision from the teacher. Experiments on MER-UniBench demonstrate that OmniOPSD achieves state-of-the-art performance with an average score of 84.19, and ablation studies confirm the effectiveness of the rationale-privileged teacher guidance in improving model performance without requiring external annotations or closed-source models.
为解决多模态大语言模型在情感计算任务中因奖励稀疏和高质量思维链标注成本高而面临的强化学习瓶颈，本文提出OmniOPSD框架，采用基于推理理由的在线自蒸馏方法。该方法利用前沿模型生成的推理理由作为训练时的特权教师证据，而非直接模仿目标，使学生模型在自身轨迹上学习并获得密集的词级监督。在MER-UniBench数据集上的实验表明，OmniOPSD平均得分达84.19，优于现有方法，且消融实验验证了推理理由引导教师的有效性。
Calibrated Stackelberg Games: Learning Optimal Commitments Against Calibrated Agents
Authors: Nika Haghtalab, Chara Podimata, Kunhe Yang
First: 2023-06-05T08:55:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:52:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce \emph{Calibrated Stackelberg Games (CSGs)}, a generalization of the standard Stackelberg Games (SGs) framework. In CSGs, a principal repeatedly interacts with an agent who (contrary to standard SGs) does not have direct access to the principal's action but instead best-responds to calibrated forecasts about it. This framework provides a powerful and realistic modeling tool that goes beyond assuming that agents use ad hoc and highly specified algorithms for interacting in strategic settings and instead builds on statistical foundations of forecasts and calibration. We show that in CSGs, despite both the principal and the agent having less information than in standard SGs, the principal's optimal utility remains upper and lower bounded by the Stackelberg value of the one-shot game, in both finite and continuous settings. Alongside CSGs, we develop stronger notions of calibration and corresponding algorithms that address two central challenges for calibration in game-theoretic environments. First, achieving point-wise calibration typically incurs an error that scales exponentially with the dimension of the strategy space. Second, the principal's convergence rate in CSGs depends critically on the adaptivity of the agent's calibration algorithm. To address these challenges, we establish a meaningful, efficiently achievable relaxation of calibration based on conditioning on best-response regions. This yields the first notion of calibration in games with a statistical rate that only depends on the number of agents' actions rather than the dimension of the principal's strategy space and that leads to no-swap regret for the agent. We further develop adaptive calibration algorithms for the agents that provide fine-grained, any-time calibration guarantees against adversarial sequences, enabling the principal to achieve faster convergence in CSGs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：校准的斯塔克尔伯格博弈：学习最优承诺以对抗校准的代理
我们引入了\emph{校准的斯塔克尔伯格博弈（CSGs）}，这是标准斯塔克尔伯格博弈（SGs）框架的推广。在CSGs中，一个主体反复与一个代理互动，该代理（与标准SGs不同）无法直接获取主体的动作，而是根据对主体动作的校准预测进行最优反应。该框架提供了一种强大且现实的建模工具，超越了假设代理在战略环境中使用临时且高度指定的算法，而是基于预测和校准的统计基础。我们证明，在CSGs中，尽管主体和代理的信息量均少于标准SGs，主体的最优效用在有限和连续情况下仍被单次博弈的斯塔克尔伯格值上下界约束。 除了CSGs，我们还开发了更强的校准概念及其相应算法，以解决博弈论环境中校准的两个核心挑战。首先，实现逐点校准通常会导致误差随策略空间维度指数增长。其次，主体在CSGs中的收敛速度高度依赖于代理校准算法的自适应性。为应对这些挑战，我们基于最佳反应区域的条件建立了校准的有意义且可高效实现的松弛形式。这首次提出了在博弈中具有统计收敛率仅依赖于代理动作数量而非主体策略空间维度的校准概念，并且对代理实现无交换遗憾。我们进一步开发了代理的自适应校准算法，提供针对对抗序列的细粒度、任意时间校准保证，使主体在CSGs中实现更快收敛。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for more realistic strategic modeling where agents respond to probabilistic forecasts rather than direct actions, the paper introduces Calibrated Stackelberg Games (CSGs), a framework where the principal commits to actions while the agent best-responds to calibrated forecasts. The method builds on statistical calibration theory, developing new notions of calibration that relax point-wise requirements and condition on best-response regions, enabling efficient algorithms with convergence rates dependent only on the agent’s action space size. Experimental results show that despite reduced information, the principal’s utility remains bounded within the Stackelberg value of the one-shot game, and adaptive calibration algorithms enable faster convergence for the principal against adversarial agents.
为更真实地建模策略环境中代理对概率预测的响应而非直接行动，本文提出校准斯塔克尔伯格博弈（CSGs），其中主方反复与仅能根据主方行动预测进行最优反应的代理互动。方法基于统计校准原理，提出对点校准的放松——即按最优反应区域条件化，从而实现仅依赖代理行动空间大小而非主方策略空间维度的收敛速率，并开发出支持对抗序列的自适应校准算法。实验表明，尽管信息受限，主方效用仍被单次博弈的斯塔克尔伯格值上下界约束，且自适应校准可加速收敛并保证代理无交换遗憾。
HairWeaver: Few-Shot Photorealistic Hair Motion Synthesis with Sim-to-Real Guided Video Diffusion
Authors: Di Chang, Ji Hou, Aljaz Bozic, Assaf Neuberger, Felix Juefei-Xu, Olivier Maury, Gene Wei-Chin Lin, Tuur Stuyck, Doug Roble, Mohammad Soleymani, Stephane Grabli
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-02-11T18:31:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:52:46+00:00
Comments: Accepted by ECCV 2026. Website: https://boese0601.github.io/hairweaver/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
We present HairWeaver, a diffusion-based pipeline that animates a single human image with realistic and expressive hair dynamics. While existing methods successfully control body pose, they lack specific control over hair, and as a result, fail to capture the intricate hair motions, resulting in stiff and unrealistic animations. HairWeaver overcomes this limitation using two specialized modules: a Motion-Context-LoRA to integrate motion conditions and a Style-Alignment-LoRA to preserve the subject's photoreal appearance across different data domains. These lightweight components are designed to guide a video diffusion backbone while maintaining its core generative capabilities. By training on a specialized dataset of dynamic human motion generated from a CG simulator, HairWeaver affords fine control over hair motion and ultimately learns to produce highly realistic hair that responds naturally to movement. Comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that our approach sets a new state of the art, producing lifelike human hair animations with dynamic details.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HairWeaver：基于模拟到现实引导视频扩散的少样本逼真头发运动合成
我们提出了HairWeaver，一种基于扩散的管线，可对单张人体图像进行逼真且富有表现力的头发动态动画。尽管现有方法能有效控制身体姿态，但缺乏对头发的特定控制，因而无法捕捉复杂的头发运动，导致动画僵硬不真实。HairWeaver通过两个专用模块克服这一局限：Motion-Context-LoRA用于整合运动条件，Style-Alignment-LoRA用于在不同数据域中保持主体的逼真外观。这些轻量级组件旨在引导视频扩散主干网络，同时保留其核心生成能力。通过在由CG模拟器生成的动态人体运动专用数据集上训练，HairWeaver实现了对头发运动的精细控制，最终学会生成自然响应运动的高逼真头发。全面评估表明，我们的方法达到新状态-of-the-art，生成具有动态细节的逼真人体头发动画。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of fine-grained hair motion control in existing video generation methods, HairWeaver introduces a diffusion-based pipeline that animates static human images with realistic hair dynamics. It employs two lightweight LoRA modules — Motion-Context-LoRA for motion guidance and Style-Alignment-LoRA for photorealistic appearance preservation — to enhance a video diffusion backbone without compromising its generative power. Trained on synthetic human motion data from a CG simulator, the model achieves precise hair motion control and generates highly realistic, expressive hair animations. Experimental results show HairWeaver outperforms prior methods in both visual realism and motion fidelity, establishing a new state-of-the-art in few-shot hair animation.
为了解决现有方法虽能控制人体姿态却无法生成真实自然的头发运动的问题，HairWeaver提出了一种基于扩散的动画生成框架，结合两个轻量级LoRA模块：Motion-Context-LoRA用于运动引导，Style-Alignment-LoRA用于保持人物真实外观。该模型在CG模拟器生成的合成人体运动数据上训练，实现了对头发动态的精细控制并保持跨域视觉保真度。实验表明，其生成的头发动画具有高度真实感和自然运动响应，达到当前最先进的性能。
SLORR: Simple and Efficient In-Training Low-Rank Regularization
Authors: David González-Martínez, Shiwei Liu
First: 2026-07-09T17:51:50+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:51:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Low-rank factorization is widely used to compress neural networks, but modern models are often not naturally amenable to aggressive factorization without significant accuracy loss. Existing training-time low-rank regularizers can improve compressibility, but they often require SVDs of large weight matrices, modify the model architecture (introducing additional trainable parameters), or rely on stateful cached quantities. To address these limitations, we introduce SLORR, a simple, stateless, and architecture-preserving framework for in-training low-rank regularization, instantiated with two main variants based on the Hoyer sparsity metric and the nuclear norm. SLORR directly regularizes the original weight matrices using GPU-friendly approximations for the forward and backward passes of the regularizers, for which we provide approximation guarantees. We first evaluate SLORR on ImageNet-1K across short-horizon continued training of ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16, and pretraining of ResNet-18, where SLORR induces compressibility while introducing less than 8% training overhead. We further evaluate SLORR-Hoyer in LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales: SLORR-trained compressed models preserve performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SLORR：简单高效的训练中低秩正则化
低秩分解广泛用于压缩神经网络，但现代模型往往难以在不显著损失精度的情况下进行激进分解。现有训练时低秩正则化方法虽可提升压缩性，但常需对大型权重矩阵进行SVD、修改模型架构（引入额外可训练参数）或依赖状态缓存量。为解决这些局限，我们提出SLORR，一种简单、无状态且保持架构的训练中低秩正则化框架，基于Hoyer稀疏度指标和核范数设计两种主要变体。SLORR直接对原始权重矩阵进行正则化，使用GPU友好的近似方法实现正则化前向与反向传播，我们为其提供近似保证。我们在ImageNet-1K上评估SLORR，分别在ResNet-50、ViT-B/16和ViT-L/16的短期持续训练及ResNet-18的预训练中，SLORR在引入少于8%训练开销的同时提升压缩性。进一步在135M和560M规模LLM预训练中评估SLORR-Hoyer：SLORR训练的压缩模型性能保持显著优于未正则化模型，且平均训练开销低于1%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to compress neural networks without sacrificing accuracy or altering model architecture, SLORR introduces a simple, stateless, in-training low-rank regularization framework that avoids SVDs and extra parameters. It employs GPU-friendly approximations of Hoyer sparsity and nuclear norm regularizers, applied directly to original weight matrices. Experiments on ImageNet-1K with ResNet-50, ViT-B/16, and ViT-L/16 show SLORR enables effective compression with under 8% training overhead. In LLM pretraining at 135M and 560M scales, SLORR-Hoyer maintains performance substantially better than unregularized models while adding less than 1% average training overhead.
为在不牺牲精度的前提下压缩神经网络，SLORR提出了一种简单、无状态且保持模型架构的训练时低秩正则化框架。它通过GPU友好的近似方法，直接对原始权重矩阵施加Hoyer稀疏度和核范数正则化，无需修改模型结构或缓存状态。在ImageNet-1K上对ResNet-50、ViT-B/16、ViT-L/16和ResNet-18进行实验，SLORR实现有效压缩且训练开销低于8%。在135M和560M规模的LLM预训练中，SLORR-Hoyer比未正则化模型保持性能显著提升，同时平均训练开销低于1%。
DexVerse: A Modular Benchmark for Multi-Task, Multi-Embodiment Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Yunchao Yao, Zhuxiu Xu, Tianqi Zhang, Zixian Liu, Sikai Li, Zhenyu Wei, Feng Chen, Dihong Huang, Kechang Wan, Chenyang Ma, Shuqi Zhao, Shenghua Gao, Masayoshi Tomizuka, Yi Ma, Mingyu Ding
First: 2026-07-09T17:50:47+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:50:47+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Building general-purpose dexterous manipulation policies requires benchmarks that go beyond isolated tasks to systematically evaluate policies across diverse interaction modes, sensory conditions, and robot embodiments. However, existing benchmarks remain limited in task and data diversity, embodiment coverage, or controllable visual variation, hindering studies of cross-task and cross-embodiment generalization. We present DexVerse, a large-scale and modular benchmark for dexterous manipulation. DexVerse includes 100 tasks spanning a broad range of manipulation skills, including object grasping and relocation, articulated-object interaction, functional tool use, bimanual coordination, non-prehensile control, contact-rich behaviors, multi-goal execution, and long-horizon multi-stage task completion. It supports 3 robot arms and 6 dexterous hands, and is extensible to new tasks, assets, and embodiments. To evaluate visuomotor generalization, DexVerse provides configurable visual variations in textures, background, lighting, and camera viewpoints. We further provide a VR-based teleoperation interface and 3,180 demonstrations with synchronized proprioceptive, RGB, depth, point-cloud, and state observations. We benchmark representative methods, including Diffusion Policy, DP3, OpenVLA, and $π_{0.5}$, across 19 tasks. Results reveal substantial challenges in task generalization and visuomotor robustness, establishing DexVerse as a promising testbed for general-purpose dexterous manipulation. Project page: https://ycyao216.github.io/DexVerse.site

标题：DexVerse：多任务、多具身灵巧操作的模块化基准
构建通用灵巧操作策略需要超越孤立任务的基准，以系统评估策略在多样交互模式、感官条件和机器人具身下的表现。然而现有基准在任务与数据多样性、具身覆盖或可控视觉变化方面仍有限，阻碍了跨任务与跨具身泛化研究。我们提出DexVerse，一个大规模模块化灵巧操作基准，涵盖100项任务，包括物体抓取与移动、铰接物体交互、功能工具使用、双手协调、非抓取控制、接触密集行为、多目标执行及长时程多阶段任务完成。支持3种机械臂与6种灵巧手，并可扩展至新任务、资产与具身。为评估视觉运动泛化，DexVerse提供纹理、背景、光照与摄像机视角的可配置视觉变化。同时提供基于VR的遥操作界面及3,180个同步的本体感觉、RGB、深度、点云与状态观测演示。我们在19项任务上对代表性方法（包括Diffusion Policy、DP3、OpenVLA与π₀.₅）进行基准测试，结果揭示任务泛化与视觉运动鲁棒性存在显著挑战，确立DexVerse为通用灵巧操作研究的有力测试平台。项目页面：https://ycyao216.github.io/DexVerse.site

Neural Harmonic Textures for High-Quality Primitive Based Neural Reconstruction
Authors: Jorge Condor, Nicolas Moenne-Loccoz, Merlin Nimier-David, Piotr Didyk, Zan Gojcic, Qi Wu
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-04-01T17:48:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:50:12+00:00
Comments: Accepted to ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Primitive-based methods such as 3D Gaussian Splatting have recently become the state-of-the-art for novel-view synthesis and related reconstruction tasks. Compared to neural fields, these representations are more flexible, adaptive, and scale better to large scenes. However, the limited expressivity of individual primitives makes modeling high-frequency detail challenging. We introduce Neural Harmonic Textures, a neural representation approach that anchors latent feature vectors on a virtual scaffold surrounding each primitive. These features are interpolated within the primitive at ray intersection points. Inspired by Fourier analysis, we apply periodic activations to the interpolated features, turning alpha blending into a weighted sum of harmonic components. The resulting signal is then decoded in a single deferred pass using a small neural network, significantly reducing computational cost. Neural Harmonic Textures yield state-of-the-art results in real-time novel view synthesis while bridging the gap between primitive- and neural-field-based reconstruction. Our method integrates seamlessly into existing primitive-based pipelines such as 3DGUT, Triangle Splatting, and 2DGS. We further demonstrate its generality with applications to 2D image fitting and semantic reconstruction.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于神经谐波纹理的高质量原始体神经重建
基于原始体的方法（如3D高斯点绘）近期已成为新视角合成及相关重建任务的最先进方法。与神经场相比，这些表示方法更具灵活性、自适应性，并能更好地扩展至大场景。然而，单个原始体表达能力有限，难以建模高频细节。我们提出神经谐波纹理，一种将潜在特征向量锚定在每个原始体周围虚拟支架上的神经表示方法。这些特征在射线交点处于原始体内进行插值。受傅里叶分析启发，我们对插值特征应用周期性激活函数，将alpha混合转化为谐波分量的加权和。最终信号通过小型神经网络在单次延迟传递中解码，显著降低计算成本。神经谐波纹理在实时新视角合成中达到最先进水平，同时弥合了原始体与神经场重建方法之间的差距。该方法可无缝集成到现有原始体方法管线（如3DGUT、三角点绘、2DGS）中。我们进一步通过2D图像拟合与语义重建应用，展示了其通用性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enhance high-frequency detail in primitive-based neural reconstruction methods like 3D Gaussian Splatting, this work introduces Neural Harmonic Textures, which anchor latent feature vectors on a virtual scaffold around each primitive and interpolate them at ray intersection points. Inspired by Fourier analysis, periodic activations transform alpha blending into a weighted harmonic sum, enabling efficient decoding via a small neural network in a single deferred pass. Experimental results show state-of-the-art performance in real-time novel view synthesis while maintaining compatibility with existing primitive-based pipelines such as 3DGUT and Triangle Splatting, and demonstrate broader applicability in 2D image fitting and semantic reconstruction.
为提升基于原始体的神经重建中高频细节的建模能力，本文提出神经谐波纹理方法，通过在每个原始体周围虚拟骨架上锚定潜在特征向量，并在射线交点处进行插值。受傅里叶分析启发，采用周期性激活将alpha混合转化为谐波分量加权和，通过轻量级神经网络在单次延迟传递中高效解码。实验表明该方法在实时新视角合成中达到最先进性能，可无缝集成于3DGUT、Triangle Splatting等现有管线，并扩展应用于2D图像拟合与语义重建。
Using AI-based Learning Assistants in Higher Education: A Large-Scale Descriptive Analysis
Authors: Kristina Schaaff, Quintus Stierstorfer, Valerie Heckel
First: 2026-07-09T17:49:14+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:49:14+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this study, we present a large-scale descriptive analysis of the use of an AI-based learning assistant (Syntea) in higher education. Based on objective log data from 77,543 students enrolled in distance studies, we examine usage patterns across gender, age group, study cluster, degree, and study mode. To date, existing research on educational chatbots has largely relied on comparatively small samples and self-reported survey data, while large-scale evidence on actual usage behavior remains limited. Our findings show that Syntea is already embedded in the study routines of many learners, but that usage differs across demographic and structural contexts. By identifying these patterns, our study provides an empirical basis for the further development of AI-based learning support and contributes a large-scale analysis of educational chatbot usage in higher education.
