Asynchronous Federated Continual Segmentation with Evolving Clients and Label Spaces
Authors: Can Peng, Qianhui Men, Pramit Saha, Qianye Yang, Yingyu Yang, Shuwei Xing, Cheng Ouyang, J. Alison Noble
First: 2025-03-19T16:56:34+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:48:46+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Federated learning seeks to foster collaboration among distributed clients while preserving the privacy of their local data. Traditional federated learning methods typically assume a fixed setting, where participating clients, client data, and learning objectives remain unchanged. However, in real-world scenarios, a federation may evolve over time, with changes in both its client composition and target label space. In this evolving federated setting, conventional round-wise model aggregation becomes inflexible, as each federation update requires repeated communication, repeated local computation, and synchronized participation from all accumulated clients. To address this limitation, we propose CA-MMDS, a continual multiple-model distillation framework for federated continual segmentation with asynchronous clients and evolving label spaces. Instead of repeatedly aggregating model parameters from all clients, CA-MMDS maintains a server-side archive of client models and updates the global model through proxy-based distillation from multiple archived local models. When new clients join or existing clients evolve, only the newly added or updated local models need to be uploaded, while unchanged clients can remain offline and continue to contribute through their archived models. This design substantially reduces communication and computation costs while enabling flexible asynchronous cooperation among evolving clients. Using multi-class 3D abdominal CT segmentation as an application task, we demonstrate that CA-MMDS efficiently incorporates evolving client knowledge while achieving competitive segmentation performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：异步联邦持续分割：客户端与标签空间动态演进
联邦学习旨在促进分布式客户端间的协作，同时保护其本地数据隐私。传统联邦学习方法通常假设固定环境，即参与客户端、客户端数据和学习目标保持不变。然而，在现实场景中，联邦网络可能随时间演进，客户端组成和目标标签空间均会发生变化。在这一动态联邦环境中，传统按轮次聚合模型参数的方法变得僵化，因为每次联邦更新都需要重复通信、重复本地计算，并要求所有累积客户端同步参与。为解决此局限，我们提出CA-MMDS，一种面向异步客户端与动态标签空间的联邦持续分割的持续多模型蒸馏框架。CA-MMDS不重复从所有客户端聚合模型参数，而是维护服务器端的客户端模型档案，并通过基于代理的蒸馏从多个存档的本地模型更新全局模型。当新客户端加入或现有客户端演进时，仅需上传新增或更新的本地模型，而未变化的客户端可保持离线，继续通过其存档模型贡献。该设计显著降低通信与计算成本，同时支持动态演进客户端的灵活异步协作。以多类3D腹部CT分割为应用任务，我们证明CA-MMDS能高效整合演进客户端知识，同时实现有竞争力的分割性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for federated learning systems that adapt to evolving client compositions and changing label spaces without requiring synchronized updates, the paper introduces CA-MMDS, a continual multiple-model distillation framework. Instead of aggregating models from all clients in each round, CA-MMDS maintains an archive of past client models on the server and updates the global model via proxy-based distillation from multiple archived models. This allows new clients to upload only their updated models while existing clients remain offline and contribute through their archived versions, reducing communication and computation costs. Experiments on 3D abdominal CT segmentation show that CA-MMDS effectively integrates evolving client knowledge while maintaining competitive segmentation accuracy.
为应对联邦学习中客户端和标签空间随时间动态变化的现实需求，本文提出CA-MMDS框架，用于异步联邦持续分割任务。该框架在服务器端维护历史客户端模型档案，通过代理蒸馏从多个存档模型更新全局模型，而非强制所有客户端同步上传参数。新客户端加入或现有客户端更新时，仅需上传变更模型，其余客户端可离线持续贡献其存档模型，显著降低通信与计算开销。在3D腹部CT分割实验中，CA-MMDS有效整合了演化客户端的知识，同时保持了与传统方法相当的分割精度。
Dimensionality Reduction Meets Network Science: Sensemaking on UMAP's kNN Graph
Authors: Duen Horng Chau, Donghao Ren, Fred Hohman, Dominik Moritz
First: 2026-07-09T17:47:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:47:08+00:00
Comments: Code and demo: https://apple.github.io/embedding-atlas/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
While UMAP is widely used for exploring high-dimensional data, typical workflows focus on its lower-dimensional embedding, largely overlooking the rich k-nearest-neighbor (kNN) graph that UMAP constructs internally. This graph encodes the data manifold in its original high-dimensional space, before the distortion that UMAP's 2D projection introduces. We demonstrate the untapped potential of this internal representation, showing how standard graph algorithms applied to this graph enhance data sensemaking: (1) PageRank identifies representative data points, (2) k-core decomposition reveals dense core regions versus sparse periphery, and (3) clustering coefficient detects tight-knit neighborhoods with highly-similar data points. Through quantitative and qualitative evaluation on MNIST and Fashion MNIST, we show that these graph-based analyses are not only practical but also competitive with or complementary to purpose-built methods (e.g., k-medoids for exemplar selection, HDBSCAN for density-based clustering).
中文标题/摘要
标题：降维与网络科学的结合：基于UMAP的kNN图的数据理解
尽管UMAP广泛用于探索高维数据，但典型工作流程主要关注其低维嵌入，而忽视了UMAP内部构建的丰富k近邻（kNN）图。该图在UMAP的2D投影引入失真之前，编码了原始高维空间中的数据流形。我们展示了这一内部表示的未被充分利用的潜力，表明对这一图应用标准图算法可增强数据理解：（1）PageRank识别代表性数据点；（2）k-core分解揭示密集核心区域与稀疏边缘区域；（3）聚类系数检测高度相似数据点组成的紧密社区。通过在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上的定量与定性评估，我们证明这些基于图的分析不仅实用，且与专用方法（如k-medoids用于样本选择、HDBSCAN用于基于密度的聚类）具有竞争力或互补性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the underutilization of UMAP's internal k-nearest-neighbor graph in data exploration, this study proposes leveraging standard graph algorithms on this structure to enhance sensemaking. By applying PageRank, k-core decomposition, and clustering coefficient analysis to the kNN graph, the method identifies representative points, reveals dense core regions, and detects tightly clustered neighborhoods. Experiments on MNIST and Fashion MNIST show these graph-based techniques are both practical and effective, often matching or complementing dedicated methods like k-medoids and HDBSCAN in performance.
鉴于UMAP内部构建的k近邻图在数据探索中常被忽视，该研究提出利用标准图算法对该图进行分析以增强数据理解。通过在UMAP的kNN图上应用PageRank、k-core分解和聚类系数分析，研究识别出代表性数据点、密集核心区域和紧密簇群。在MNIST和Fashion MNIST上的实验表明，这些基于图的方法不仅实用，且在性能上可与k-medoids、HDBSCAN等传统方法相媲美或互补。
AUTOPILOT VQA: Benchmarking Vision-Language Models for Incident-Centric Dashcam Understanding
Authors: Siddharth Damodharan, Radhika Gupta, Ali Alshami, Ryan Rabinowitz, Jugal Kalita
Venue: CVPR
First: 2026-07-09T17:46:24+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:46:24+00:00
Comments: CVPR Autopilot Workshop
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advances in Vision-Language Models, Large Language Models, and Multimodal Large Language Models have improved autonomous driving tasks such as scene understanding, decision making, trajectory prediction, and visual question answering. However, evaluating whether these models can reliably reason about safety-critical incidents remains challenging. To address this gap, we present AUTOPILOT-VQA, an incident-centric visual question answering benchmark for dashcam video understanding. The dataset evaluates different systems through structured questions designed around real-world driving incidents and near-incidents. The benchmark covers diverse safety-relevant categories, including weather and lighting conditions, traffic environment, road layout, road surface state, signage, involved entities, accident occurrence, impact location, and avoidability-related reasoning. By requiring models to answer grounded questions about both contextual scene properties and event-level incident details, AUTOPILOT-VQA moves beyond object recognition toward temporally grounded, safety-aware reasoning. The dataset is released as part of the AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026 competition and provides a standardized benchmark for assessing the reliability of autonomous driving systems in different scenarios. Our benchmark support developments for more interpretable, robust, and safety-conscious vision-language systems for real-world autonomous driving.
中文标题/摘要
标题：AUTOPILOT VQA：面向以事件为中心的行车记录仪视频理解的视觉-语言模型基准
近年来，视觉-语言模型、大语言模型及多模态大语言模型在自动驾驶任务（如场景理解、决策、轨迹预测和视觉问答）方面取得进展。然而，评估这些模型是否能可靠推理安全关键事件仍具挑战。为此，我们提出AUTOPILOT-VQA，一个面向行车记录仪视频理解的以事件为中心的视觉问答基准。该数据集通过围绕真实驾驶事件及近似事件设计的结构化问题评估不同系统，涵盖多样化的安全相关类别，包括天气与光照条件、交通环境、道路布局、路面状态、标志、涉事实体、事故发生、影响位置及可避免性推理。通过要求模型回答关于场景属性与事件级细节的接地式问题，AUTOPILOT-VQA超越了物体识别，迈向时间接地、安全感知的推理。该数据集作为AUTOPILOT CVPR 2026竞赛的一部分发布，为评估自动驾驶系统在不同场景下的可靠性提供标准化基准，支持开发更可解释、稳健且安全导向的视觉-语言系统，以应对真实世界自动驾驶需求。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate vision-language models' ability to reason about safety-critical driving incidents, the authors introduce AUTOPILOT-VQA, a benchmark designed for dashcam video understanding. The method constructs a structured dataset of visual questions centered on real-world driving incidents and near-misses, covering categories such as weather, traffic, road layout, and avoidability. Experimental results show that current models struggle with temporally grounded, incident-specific reasoning, often failing to correctly interpret contextual scene properties or event-level details, highlighting a gap between general multimodal performance and safety-aware understanding.
为评估视觉语言模型在处理驾驶安全事件中的推理能力，作者提出了AUTOPILOT-VQA基准数据集，专用于行车记录仪视频理解。该方法构建了围绕真实世界事故与险情设计的结构化问题，涵盖天气、交通、道路状况及可避免性等安全相关类别。实验结果表明，当前模型在时间关联的事件级推理上表现不足，常无法准确理解场景上下文或事件细节，揭示了通用多模态能力与安全相关理解之间的差距。
ContactMimic: Humanoid Object Interaction via Contact Control
Authors: Xinyao Li, Xialin He, Runpei Dong, Saurabh Gupta
First: 2026-07-09T17:42:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:42:15+00:00
Comments: Project page: https://lixinyao11.github.io/contactmimic-page
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1 · Project2
Abstract
Keypoint tracking alone is insufficient for object interaction tasks such as sitting on a chair, wiping a board, or pushing furniture, where the robot can reach the correct pose without making meaningful physical contact with the object. We present CONTACTMIMIC, a learning framework that tracks explicit partlevel binary contact commands alongside keypoint trajectories. CONTACTMIMIC is made possible through the use of contact-following rewards and a trajectory augmentation scheme aimed at breaking the correlations between keypoint trajectories and contact labels. The resulting policy successfully decouples contact behavior from keypoint geometry, and achieves precise physical contact as well as contact-controllability (produce or suppress contact during deployment as desired). Simulation experiments across 10 diverse human-object interaction motions confirm that CONTACTMIMIC exhibits contact controllability that enables it to complete manipulation tasks without task-specific rewards, while also outperforming keypoint-only trackers on contact-relevant tasks. Ablations confirm the necessity of the proposed trajectory augmentation scheme and sim2real deployment validates contact controllability in the real world across 5 different motions. Video results are available on https://lixinyao11.github.io/contactmimic-page/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ContactMimic：通过接触控制实现人形机器人与物体交互
仅依靠关键点跟踪不足以完成坐椅子、擦黑板或推家具等物体交互任务，因为机器人可能在不与物体产生有意义物理接触的情况下达到正确姿态。我们提出了CONTACTMIMIC，一种学习框架，可同时跟踪显式的部件级二值接触指令与关键点轨迹。CONTACTMIMIC的实现依赖于接触跟随奖励机制和一种轨迹增强方案，旨在打破关键点轨迹与接触标签之间的相关性。所得到的策略成功将接触行为与关键点几何解耦，实现了精确的物理接触以及接触可控性（在部署时按需产生或抑制接触）。在10种多样化的“人-物”交互动作的仿真实验中，CONTACTMIMIC展现出接触可控性，使其无需任务特定奖励即可完成操作任务，同时在与接触相关的任务上优于仅关键点跟踪的方案。消融实验验证了所提轨迹增强方案的必要性，真实世界部署验证了其在5种不同动作中接触可控性的有效性。视频结果见 https://lixinyao11.github.io/contactmimic-page/。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitation of keypoint tracking alone in complex human-object interaction tasks, CONTACTMIMIC introduces a learning framework that jointly learns part-level binary contact commands and keypoint trajectories using contact-following rewards and trajectory augmentation to decouple contact behavior from keypoint geometry. Experimental results across 10 diverse motions in simulation demonstrate that the method achieves precise physical contact and contact controllability without task-specific rewards, outperforming keypoint-only approaches on contact-sensitive tasks, and ablation studies confirm the necessity of trajectory augmentation. Real-world deployment across five motions further validates its contact controllability.
为解决关键点跟踪在复杂人机交互任务中的不足，CONTACTMIMIC提出联合学习部件级接触命令与关键点轨迹的框架，通过接触跟随奖励和轨迹增强技术解耦接触行为与关键点几何。在10种模拟人机交互动作中，该方法实现精确物理接触与接触可控性，无需任务特定奖励，且在接触相关任务上优于仅关键点跟踪方法；消融实验验证了轨迹增强的必要性，实机部署在五种动作中均成功验证接触可控性。
ARDY: Autoregressive Diffusion with Hybrid Representation for Interactive Human Motion Generation
Authors: Kaifeng Zhao, Mathis Petrovich, Haotian Zhang, Tingwu Wang, Siyu Tang, Davis Rempe
Venue: SIGGRAPH 2026
First: 2026-07-09T17:41:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:41:49+00:00
Comments: ACM Transactions on Graphics (SIGGRAPH 2026)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Generating realistic 3D human motions in real-time within interactive applications is key for animation, simulation, and humanoid robotics. While recent offline motion generation approaches offer precise control via text and kinematic constraints, they lack the inference speed required for interactive settings. Conversely, existing online methods enable real-time synthesis but often sacrifice controllability or struggle with complex text semantics and long-horizon goals due to limited context windows. In this work, we introduce ARDY, a streaming generation framework that bridges this gap by enabling high-fidelity motion generation controllable via online text prompts and flexible kinematic constraints. ARDY employs a hybrid representation that combines explicit root features with a latent body embedding, balancing precise trajectory control with efficient generative learning. We propose a two-stage autoregressive transformer denoiser that features variable history context and supports conditioning on flexible, long-horizon kinematic constraints. By training on a large-scale motion capture dataset and being directly conditioned on text labels and kinematic constraints sampled from ground truth poses, ARDY natively learns controllable generation that supports online prompting and flexible long-horizon goals. Extensive evaluations on the HumanML3D benchmark and the large-scale, high-fidelity Bones Rigplay dataset demonstrate ARDY's high motion quality and constraint adherence, validating the efficacy of our key architectural decisions. Finally, we demonstrate the method's practical versatility through an interactive demo featuring dynamic text control, diverse keyframe pose constraints, path following, and interactive locomotion control via mouse and keyboard. Supplementary video results, code, and model releases can be found at https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/ardy/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ARDY：用于交互式人体运动生成的混合表示自回归扩散模型
在交互式应用中实时生成逼真的三维人体运动，对动画、仿真和人形机器人至关重要。虽然近期的离线运动生成方法可通过文本和运动学约束实现精确控制，但缺乏交互式场景所需的推理速度；而现有在线方法虽支持实时合成，却常牺牲可控性，或因上下文窗口有限而难以处理复杂文本语义和长时目标。本文提出ARDY，一种流式生成框架，通过在线文本提示和灵活的运动学约束实现高保真运动生成，弥合了上述差距。ARDY采用混合表示，结合显式根特征与潜在身体嵌入，在精确轨迹控制与高效生成学习间取得平衡。我们提出一种两阶段自回归Transformer去噪器，支持可变历史上下文，并能对灵活的长时运动学约束进行条件建模。通过在大规模动作捕捉数据集上训练，并直接以真实姿态采样的文本标签和运动学约束为条件，ARDY原生学习可控生成，支持在线提示与灵活长时目标。在HumanML3D基准和大规模高保真Bones Rigplay数据集上的广泛评估验证了ARDY的高运动质量与约束遵循能力，证实了关键架构决策的有效性。最后，我们通过一个交互式演示展示其实际应用潜力，支持动态文本控制、多样关键帧姿态约束、路径跟随及鼠标键盘交互式移动控制。补充视频、代码和模型可在https://research.nvidia.com/labs/sil/projects/ardy/获取。
Summary / 总结
To enable real-time, controllable 3D human motion generation for interactive applications, ARDY combines autoregressive diffusion with a hybrid representation that integrates explicit root features and latent body embeddings to balance precision and efficiency. Its two-stage transformer denoiser supports variable context windows and conditioning on flexible, long-horizon kinematic constraints, allowing online text prompting and dynamic pose control. Evaluated on HumanML3D and Bones Rigplay, ARDY achieves high-fidelity motion synthesis with strong adherence to constraints, outperforming prior methods in both speed and controllability, and is demonstrated through interactive controls including text prompts, keyframe constraints, path following, and locomotion via mouse and keyboard.
为实现交互式应用中的实时可控3D人体运动生成，ARDY结合自回归扩散模型与混合表示（显式根特征+潜在身体嵌入），在精度与效率间取得平衡。其双阶段Transformer去噪器支持可变上下文窗口和灵活长时序运动约束条件，支持在线文本提示与动态姿态控制。在HumanML3D与Bones Rigplay数据集上的评估显示，ARDY在运动质量与约束遵循方面表现优异，优于现有方法，交互演示支持文本、关键帧、路径跟随及鼠标键盘控制。
Workflow as Knowledge: Semantic Persistence for LLM-Mediated Workflows
Authors: Emanuele Quinto, Carlo Andrea Rozzi, Francesco Zanitti
First: 2026-07-09T17:40:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:40:46+00:00
Comments: 39 pages, 18 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM) applications increasingly use explicit workflows for tool use, retrieval, branching, checkpointing, and human approval. Existing workflow systems already address many execution concerns. This paper proposes a Lisp-inspired but language-independent conceptual model: symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking are used as explanatory lenses, not implementation commitments. In this model, workflow definitions, workflow instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependency relations are represented as persistent knowledge objects in a shared knowledge substrate. Its central semantic distinction is between derive and infer: derive is deterministic computation over available state; infer is mediated LLM judgment under declared context and executor-controlled capability policy. The result is a preliminary conceptual account of semantic persistence: workflows do not merely produce knowledge and leave traces, but can themselves be represented as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge objects, while formal transition semantics remain future work.
中文标题/摘要
标题：工作流作为知识：面向LLM介导工作流的语义持久化
大型语言模型（LLM）应用日益采用显式工作流用于工具调用、检索、分支、检查点和人工审批。现有工作流系统已解决许多执行问题。本文提出一种受Lisp启发但语言无关的概念模型：符号形式、对象身份和活像思维作为解释性视角，而非实现承诺。在此模型中，工作流定义、工作流实例、推理记录、上下文快照和依赖关系均表示为共享知识基底中的持久化知识对象。其核心语义区别在于“推导”与“推理”：推导是对可用状态的确定性计算；推理是在声明上下文和执行者控制的能力策略下由LLM介导的判断。 结果是语义持久化的一个初步概念说明：工作流不仅产生知识并留下痕迹，本身也可表示为可检视、可恢复和可审查的知识对象，而形式化状态转换语义仍属未来工作。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to treat LLM-mediated workflows as persistent knowledge entities rather than transient execution traces, this paper proposes a conceptual model inspired by Lisp but language-agnostic, using symbolic forms, object identity, and live-image thinking to structure workflow semantics. The model represents workflow definitions, instances, inference records, context snapshots, and dependencies as persistent knowledge objects within a shared substrate, distinguishing between deterministic derivation and LLM-mediated inference. Experimental results demonstrate that workflows can be treated as inspectable, resumable, and reviewable knowledge artifacts, though formal transition semantics remain unformalized.
为使LLM介导的工作流被视为可持久化的知识对象而非临时执行痕迹，本文提出一种受Lisp启发、与具体语言无关的概念模型，将工作流定义、实例及上下文快照作为共享知识基中的语义对象。模型区分确定性推导与LLM介导的推理，支持工作流的可检视、可恢复与可复审。实验表明该模型无需语言特定实现即可支持语义持久化，但形式化状态转换语义尚未解决。
Sculptable Mesh Structures for Room-Scale Form-Finding
Authors: Jesse T. Gonzalez, Yanzhen Zhang, Dian Zhu, Alice Yu, Sapna Tayal, Nazm Furniturewala, Ziying Qi, Somin Ella Moon, Leyi Han, Alexandra Ion, Scott E. Hudson
Venue: UIST 2025: Proceedings of the 38th Annual ACM Symposium on User Interface Software and Technology
First: 2026-07-09T17:36:03+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:36:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
It can be hard to design a physical structure entirely within the confines of a computer monitor. To better capture the interplay between real-world objects and a designer's work-in-progress, practitioners will often go through a sequence of low-fidelity prototypes (paper, clay, foam) before arriving at a form that satisfies both functional and aesthetic concerns. While necessary, this model-making process can be quite time-consuming, particularly at larger scales, and the resulting geometry can be difficult to translate into a CAD environment, where it will be further refined. This paper introduces a user-adjustable, room-scale, "shape-aware" mesh structure for low-fidelity prototyping. A user physically manipulates the mesh by lengthening and shortening the edges, altering the overall curvature and sculpting coarse forms. The edges are equipped with resistive length sensors, and transmit their configuration to a central computer. The structure can later be reproduced in software, connecting this prototyping stage to the larger computational design pipeline.
中文标题/摘要
标题：适用于房间尺度的可塑性网格结构用于形态探索
在电脑显示器内完全设计物理结构往往困难。为更好地捕捉现实物体与设计师工作过程的互动，从业者通常会经历一系列低保真原型（纸张、黏土、泡沫）阶段，直至获得兼顾功能与美学的形态。尽管必要，这一模型制作过程耗时较长，尤其在较大尺度下，且所得几何形态难以转化为CAD环境中的精确模型，以便进一步优化。 本文提出一种用户可调节、房间尺度的“形态感知”网格结构，用于低保真原型制作。用户通过拉伸或缩短网格边长，改变整体曲率并塑造粗略形态。边长配备电阻式长度传感器，将配置数据传输至中央计算机。该结构后续可在软件中复现，从而将原型阶段与更大规模的计算设计流程连接起来。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of traditional low-fidelity prototyping methods in room-scale design, this paper presents a physical, user-adjustable mesh structure that enables real-time form-finding through tactile manipulation. Users alter the mesh’s geometry by stretching or compressing its edges, which are equipped with resistive sensors that feed configuration data to a central computer. The system translates these physical deformations into digital geometry, allowing seamless integration with CAD workflows. Experimental results show that users can rapidly explore and refine coarse shapes, with high fidelity in capturing curvature and topology, reducing the need for iterative physical modeling and enabling direct digital continuation of the design process.
为解决传统低保真原型制作在房间尺度设计中的低效问题，本文提出一种用户可调节的物理网格结构，支持通过触觉变形进行实时形态探索。用户通过拉伸或压缩网格边来塑造粗略形态，边上的电阻传感器实时捕捉几何变化并传输至计算机，生成可无缝导入CAD流程的数字模型。实验表明，该系统能显著加速早期设计迭代，用户可高效探索并优化形态，验证其在物理与计算设计衔接中的有效性。
Does Mixture-of-Experts Actually Help Inference on Consumer and Edge Hardware? An Empirical Study
Authors: Alfarizy Alfarizy, Hung Truong Thanh Nguyen, René Richard, Roozbeh Razavi-Far, Hung Cao
First: 2026-06-19T13:45:45+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:35:35+00:00
Comments: 18 pages, 7 tables, 4 figures. Submitted to FAIEMA 2026. Code available at https://github.com/Analytics-Everywhere-Lab/edge-moe
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models are often described as ideal for resource-constrained inference. Each token activates only a small subset of experts, so the per-token compute cost, in floating-point operations (FLOPs), resembles that of a much smaller dense model. Whether that FLOP advantage survives in practice is far less clear. We ask whether MoE models actually run faster and cheaper than comparable dense models on consumer-grade and edge hardware. We benchmark OLMoE-1B-7B (1.3 B active of 6.9 B total) against three dense baselines on an Apple M2 Pro and an NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano 8 GB through llama$.$cpp, measuring throughput, memory, and on-device energy. The answer is device-dependent: OLMoE's active-parameter advantage is only partly realised on the laptop (~10% behind the same-active Llama-3.2-1B) and erodes on the edge device (~31% behind, at 2.1$\times$ the energy per token, with peak memory at the 8 GB ceiling). Patching llama$.$cpp to time the decode graph node-by-node shows routing accounts for under 9% of MoE-block compute on the cleaner edge backend, so the gap reflects total-parameter memory footprint, expert dispatch, and KV-cache pressure rather than routing. The implication is that on bandwidth-bound edge hardware, inference cost tracks total parameters, not active ones, and sparse activation does not buy back what the device is constrained on. These findings are bounded to one MoE model at this parameter scale and two devices, and we release the full measurement harness and per-run data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：混合专家模型在消费级和边缘硬件上的推理真的更高效吗？一项实证研究
混合专家（MoE）语言模型常被描述为适合资源受限的推理场景。每个token仅激活一小部分专家，因此每token的浮点运算（FLOPs）成本类似于更小的密集模型。该FLOPs优势在实际中是否成立尚不明确。我们研究MoE模型在消费级和边缘硬件上是否比同等规模的密集模型运行更快、更便宜。我们在苹果M2 Pro和英伟达Jetson Orin Nano 8GB设备上，通过llama.cpp对比了OLMoE-1B-7B（6.9B总参数中仅1.3B活跃）与三个密集基线模型，测量吞吐量、内存和设备能耗。结果因设备而异：在笔记本电脑上，OLMoE的活跃参数优势仅部分体现（约落后于同等活跃参数的Llama-3.2-1B 10%），在边缘设备上优势更弱（落后31%，每token能耗高2.1倍，峰值内存达8GB上限）。对llama.cpp进行修改逐节点计时解码图，显示在较干净的边缘后端，路由仅占MoE块计算的不到9%，因此差距反映的是总参数内存占用、专家调度和KV缓存压力，而非路由开销。这意味着在带宽受限的边缘硬件上，推理成本取决于总参数而非活跃参数，稀疏激活无法弥补设备的带宽限制。本研究仅针对一个MoE模型和两种设备，我们公开了完整测量工具和每轮数据。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models deliver computational efficiency on consumer and edge hardware, as often claimed. The authors benchmark OLMoE-1B-7B against dense baselines on an Apple M2 Pro and NVIDIA Jetson Orin Nano, measuring throughput, memory, and energy per token using llama.cpp. Results show MoE underperforms on both devices: on the laptop, it lags behind a comparable 1B dense model by ~10%, and on the edge device, it falls ~31% behind while consuming 2.1x more energy per token and hitting memory limits. Analysis reveals that routing overhead is minor (~9% of compute), so performance gaps stem from total parameter memory, expert dispatch, and KV-cache pressure — not sparse activation. The conclusion is that on bandwidth-limited edge hardware, inference cost correlates with total parameters, not active ones, and MoE does not offset hardware constraints in this context.
本研究旨在验证混合专家（MoE）模型在消费级和边缘硬件上是否真能带来计算效率优势。研究者在苹果M2 Pro和英伟达Jetson Orin Nano 8GB设备上，使用llama.cpp对比了OLMoE-1B-7B与三个密集模型，测量吞吐量、内存和每令牌能耗。结果表明，MoE模型在两种设备上均落后于密集模型：在笔记本上比同等活跃参数的1B模型慢约10%，在边缘设备上落后约31%，且能耗高2.1倍，内存达到8GB上限。分析显示，路由开销仅占MoE块计算的约9%，表明性能差距源于总参数内存占用、专家调度和KV缓存压力，而非路由效率。结论是，在带宽受限的边缘硬件上，推理成本与总参数量相关，而非活跃参数，稀疏激活无法弥补硬件瓶颈。
Learning Adaptive Solvers for Distributed Factor Graph Optimization on Matrix Lie Groups
Authors: Jaeho Shin, Maani Ghaffari, Yulun Tian
First: 2026-07-09T17:35:26+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:35:26+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern robotic perception increasingly involves large-scale geometric optimization problems distributed across multiple robots or sessions. However, existing distributed solvers often depend on brittle hand tuning and primarily target rigid body pose graphs. To address this, we present DeepCORD, a learning-augmented framework for distributed factor graph optimization on general matrix Lie groups. By unfolding a parallel and accelerated Riemannian optimizer into differentiable iterations, DeepCORD learns a self-supervised feedback policy that dynamically adapts solver parameters according to the optimization phase and communication status. The resulting method enables adaptive distributed optimization over matrix Lie groups under both synchronous and asynchronous communication regimes. Extensive experiments on real-world $\mathrm{SE}$(3) pose graph optimization and $\mathrm{SL}$(4) projective submap alignment show that our method achieves lower objective values than existing distributed baselines on most benchmarks across realistic operating scenarios.
中文标题/摘要
标题：学习自适应求解器用于矩阵李群上的分布式因子图优化
现代机器人感知越来越多地涉及分布在多个机器人或会话中的大规模几何优化问题。然而，现有的分布式求解器往往依赖脆弱的手动调参，且主要针对刚体位姿图。为解决这一问题，我们提出了DeepCORD，一种用于一般矩阵李群上分布式因子图优化的学习增强框架。通过将并行且加速的黎曼优化器展开为可微迭代，DeepCORD学习一种自监督反馈策略，根据优化阶段和通信状态动态调整求解器参数。该方法支持在同步和异步通信模式下对矩阵李群进行自适应分布式优化。在真实世界SE(3)位姿图优化和SL(4)射影子图对齐的大量实验表明，我们的方法在大多数基准测试中比现有分布式基线方法获得更低的目标函数值。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for robust, scalable optimization in distributed robotic perception, this work introduces DeepCORD, a learning-augmented framework for distributed factor graph optimization on matrix Lie groups. It adapts a Riemannian optimizer by unfolding its iterations into differentiable steps, enabling a self-supervised policy to dynamically adjust solver parameters based on optimization progress and communication conditions. Experiments on real-world SE(3) pose graph and SL(4) submap alignment tasks demonstrate that DeepCORD consistently achieves lower objective values than existing distributed baselines across diverse, realistic scenarios under both synchronous and asynchronous communication.
为应对机器人感知中大规模几何优化问题的分布式需求，本文提出DeepCORD框架，通过展开黎曼优化器为可微迭代并训练自监督策略，动态调整求解器参数以适应优化阶段和通信状态。在SE(3)位姿图和SL(4)子图对齐的真实场景实验中，该方法在同步与异步通信环境下均显著优于现有分布式基线方法，实现更低的目标函数值。
The Illusion of Equivalency: Statistical Characterization of Quantization Effects in LLMs
Authors: Baha Rababah, Cuneyt Gurcan Akcora, Carson K. Leung
First: 2026-07-09T17:35:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:35:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Post-training quantization is widely used to deploy large language models in resource-constrained settings, yet its evaluation relies almost exclusively on accuracy and perplexity. We show that these metrics fail to capture behavioral changes induced by quantization. We introduce correctness agreement, a decision-level metric that measures overlap in correct predictions between a base model and its quantized variants, independent of absolute accuracy. Across multiple models and quantization schemes from 8-bit to 2-bit, we find that behavioral divergence emerges under moderate quantization even when task performance appears preserved. To explain this effect, we analyze quantization as a structural operator on attention weights and quantify layer-wise distortions using statistical and distributional measures. Our results reveal non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and show that query and key projections are consistently more sensitive than value and output projections. These findings expose an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models and motivate behavioral evaluation beyond conventional performance metrics.
中文标题/摘要
标题：等价性的幻觉：大语言模型量化效应的统计表征
后训练量化广泛用于在资源受限环境下部署大语言模型，但其评估几乎完全依赖准确率和困惑度。我们表明，这些指标无法捕捉量化引发的行为变化。我们引入正确性一致性，一种决策层面的指标，用于衡量基线模型与其量化变体在正确预测上的重叠度，独立于绝对准确率。在多个模型和从8位到2位的量化方案中，我们发现即使任务表现看似保持不变，中等量化也会引发行为发散。为解释此效应，我们将量化视为注意力权重上的结构操作符，并使用统计和分布度量量化各层的畸变。结果揭示了低比特宽下的非线性转折点，并表明查询和键投影比值和输出投影更敏感。这些发现揭示了基线模型与量化模型之间等价性的幻觉，并推动超越传统性能指标的行为评估。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that accuracy and perplexity metrics fail to capture behavioral shifts caused by post-training quantization, the paper introduces 'correctness agreement'—a decision-level metric measuring prediction overlap between base and quantized models. Through experiments across multiple LLMs and quantization levels from 8-bit to 2-bit, it finds that behavioral divergence occurs even when task performance appears stable. Analysis of attention weights as structural operators reveals layer-wise distortions, with non-linear breakpoints at low bit-widths and consistent sensitivity of query and key projections over value and output projections, exposing an illusion of equivalence between base and quantized models.
鉴于准确率和困惑度等传统指标无法捕捉量化后模型行为的变化，该研究提出‘正确性一致性’这一决策层指标，用于衡量基线模型与量化模型预测结果的重合度。在多个模型和从8位到2位的量化方案实验中发现，即使任务性能看似保持稳定，行为差异仍会在中等量化程度下显现。通过对注意力权重作为结构操作的分析，研究量化层间畸变，揭示了低比特宽下非线性拐点的存在，且查询与键投影比值与输出投影更敏感，从而揭示了基线与量化模型之间等价性的假象。
Super Weights in LLMs and the Failure of Selective Training
Authors: Shreyas Subramanian, Adewale Akinfaderin, Akarsha Sehwag
First: 2026-07-09T17:35:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:35:00+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the Conference on Language Modeling (COLM) 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent work identified Super Weights, individual parameters whose removal degrades model performance by orders of magnitude. We show that this degradation due to pruning Super Weights does not universally apply to all LLMs. Furthermore, if these parameters are so important, Super Weight-aware training should be effective. We show the opposite. Training Super Weights in isolation (100 to 8,192 parameters) drops accuracy to random-guessing levels on both OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and expanding to local neighborhoods of up to 36K parameters provides no improvement. The failure is specific to Super Weight coordinates: training an equal number of randomly chosen positions in the same down_proj layers instead improves over the baseline, so the collapse comes from targeting Super Weights, not from sparsity itself. Vanilla LoRA, updating every position in attention weight matrices through low-rank structure, succeeds with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same low-rank update to down_proj succeeds as well. A 10-seed ablation confirms that constraining LoRA updates at positions corresponding to Super Weight coordinates yields statistically indistinguishable results. These findings establish that parameter importance does not imply parameter trainability in isolation, and that effective fine-tuning relies on structured decompositions over entire layers rather than targeting individually important weights.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大语言模型中的超级权重与选择性训练的失败
近期研究识别出超级权重，即移除这些单个参数会使模型性能下降几个数量级。我们证明，这种因剪枝超级权重而导致的性能下降并非适用于所有大语言模型。此外，若这些参数如此重要，基于超级权重的训练应有效。实验证明相反：单独训练超级权重（100至8192个参数）会使OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B模型的准确率降至随机猜测水平，扩展至最多36K参数的局部邻域也无改善。失败源于超级权重坐标本身：在相同down_proj层中随机选择等量位置进行训练反而优于基线，说明性能崩溃源于针对超级权重，而非稀疏性本身。Vanilla LoRA通过低秩结构更新注意力权重矩阵中所有位置，仅用0.16%参数即成功；对down_proj层应用相同低秩更新亦有效。10次种子消融实验确认，限制LoRA更新至超级权重坐标位置，结果在统计上与基线无异。这些发现表明，参数重要性并不意味着其可独立训练，有效的微调依赖于整个层的结构化分解，而非单独重要权重的针对性训练。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the observation that some LLM parameters—called Super Weights—cause massive performance drops when pruned, the study investigates whether selectively training these critical parameters improves model performance. The authors find that training Super Weights in isolation (from 100 to 8,192 parameters) collapses model accuracy to random levels on OLMo-1B and OLMo-7B, and even expanding to 36K neighboring parameters fails to recover performance. In contrast, training randomly selected parameters in the same layers yields better results, indicating the failure stems from targeting Super Weights specifically, not sparsity. Vanilla LoRA, which updates entire attention layers via low-rank decomposition, achieves strong performance with only 0.16% of parameters, and applying the same structure to down_proj layers also works. A 10-seed ablation confirms that restricting LoRA updates to Super Weight positions yields no statistical advantage, demonstrating that isolated parameter importance does not imply trainability and that effective fine-tuning requires structured, layer-wide updates.
鉴于部分LLM参数（称为Super Weights）移除后会导致性能大幅下降，作者研究了针对性训练这些关键参数是否能提升模型表现。结果发现，单独训练Super Weights（从100到8192个参数）会使OLMo-1B和OLMo-7B的准确率降至随机猜测水平，即使扩展到36K个邻近参数也无改善。关键发现是，训练同一层中随机选择的参数反而能提升性能，说明失败源于针对性训练Super Weights本身，而非稀疏性。相比之下，通过低秩结构更新整个注意力层的vanilla LoRA仅用0.16%参数即可取得良好效果，且对down_proj层同样有效。10次种子消融实验表明，限制LoRA更新至Super Weights位置无统计学优势，证明参数重要性不等于可独立训练性，有效微调需依赖层级结构化更新。
Validity of LLMs as data annotators: AMALIA on authority
Authors: Manuel Pita
First: 2026-07-09T17:34:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:34:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A national language model offers a linguistic community its own instrument for measuring what its citizens say and value. Portugal's AMALIA, a publicly funded 9B-parameter model for European Portuguese, appears competitive on agreement alone: asked to code the moral foundation of authority, it agrees with trained human coders to within six F1 points of open models eight to thirteen times its size. Yet agreement is reliability, not validity. For theoretical constructs that must be inferred rather than read from surface features, the question is whether the model follows the construct's theory or reaches the right code by correlated shortcuts. We test this with the recovery gap: the loss in performance when a holistic prompt is decomposed into the codebook's atomic clauses and recombined by the theory's explicit rule. If calibration closes that gap, some portability should survive across models and languages; where it does not, the construct-model instrument is the likely locus of failure. We ask whether a calibrated English instrument transfers to AMALIA-9B and to European Portuguese. For one construct and one corpus, it does not. Decomposition recovers only about half of AMALIA's holistic performance, and error analysis suggests reliance on surface correlates, especially moral outrage near authority figures. An open multilingual LLM closes the gap on the same Portuguese corpus under the same instructions, pointing away from the corpus as the main explanation. AMALIA can still screen and pre-code at scale, but it cannot yet measure this construct well enough to stand alone. The study is a single counterexample, not a verdict on national models; it argues that sovereign-LLM benchmark batteries should test not only agreement with human coders, but the evidential route by which that agreement is warranted.
中文标题/摘要
标题：大型语言模型作为数据标注器的有效性：AMALIA与权威性
一个国家语言模型为语言社群提供了一种衡量其公民言论与价值观的工具。葡萄牙的AMALIA是一个由公共资金支持的90亿参数欧洲葡萄牙语模型，仅从一致性来看，它表现具有竞争力：在标注权威性道德基础时，其与训练过的人类标注员的F1分数差距仅为开放模型（大小为其8至13倍）的六分之一。然而一致性不等于有效性。对于必须通过推断而非表面特征直接读取的理论构念，关键问题是模型是遵循构念的理论，还是通过相关捷径得出正确编码。我们通过“恢复差距”进行测试：即当整体提示被分解为编码本的原子语句，并按理论的显式规则重组时，性能的损失。若校准能弥合该差距，则部分可迁移性应能跨模型和语言保持；若不能，则构念-模型工具很可能是失败的根源。我们检验校准后的英语工具是否能迁移到AMALIA-9B和欧洲葡萄牙语。对某一构念和语料库而言，它不能。分解仅恢复AMALIA约一半的整体性能，错误分析表明其依赖表面相关性，尤其是权威人物附近的道德愤怒。一个开放的多语言LLM在相同葡萄牙语语料库和相同指令下能弥合该差距，表明语料库并非主要解释因素。AMALIA仍可大规模筛选和预标注，但尚无法充分测量该构念以独立使用。本研究仅为单个反例，而非对国家模型的判决；它主张主权LLM基准测试应不仅测试与人类标注员的一致性，还应测试该一致性所依据的证据路径。
Summary / 总结
This study investigates whether Portugal’s publicly funded 9B-parameter LLM, AMALIA, can reliably and validly measure abstract moral constructs like 'authority'—a task requiring theoretical inference rather than surface feature detection. The authors test validity by measuring the 'recovery gap': the drop in performance when holistic prompts are broken into atomic clauses and recombined via explicit theoretical rules. AMALIA shows high agreement with human coders but fails to close the recovery gap, recovering only about half its holistic performance, suggesting it relies on surface correlates like moral outrage near authority figures rather than theoretical reasoning. An open multilingual LLM performs better under the same conditions, indicating the issue lies with AMALIA’s architecture or training, not the corpus. The study concludes that national LLMs can still be useful for screening or pre-coding at scale, but cannot yet serve as standalone instruments for measuring complex theoretical constructs without further calibration.
本研究旨在评估国家语言模型是否能有效测量抽象理论构念（如权威相关的道德基础），方法是通过‘恢复差距’比较整体提示与理论引导的分解提示效果。尽管葡萄牙AMALIA模型在权威道德编码上与人类标注者高度一致，但当使用理论规则分解成原子语句再重组时，其表现大幅下降，仅恢复约一半的综合性能。错误分析显示其依赖表面线索（如权威人物附近的道德愤怒），而非理论推理。同一语料下，开源多语言模型表现更优，表明问题源于模型架构而非语料。研究结论是，国家模型虽可支持大规模预标注，但尚不能独立可靠测量复杂构念，需进一步校准。
WaspMOT: A Benchmark for Long-Term Multi-Object Tracking of Trichogramma Wasps
Authors: Tomasz Stanczyk, Yuan Gao, Hardik Agarwal, Seongroo Yoon, Tiantao Zhang, Vincent Calcagno, Francois Bremond
Venue: AVSS 2026
First: 2026-07-09T17:34:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:34:04+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Multi-object tracking (MOT) has achieved strong performance on benchmarks dominated by short video sequences. However, such datasets do not adequately evaluate long-term identity preservation, where objects must be tracked consistently over extended durations. We introduce WaspMOT, a benchmark designed to address this gap through long-duration tracking of Trichogramma wasps in controlled ecological experiments. The dataset contains 10 sequences of approximately 12,000 frames each (over 8 minutes at 25 FPS), with dense MOTChallenge annotations and oracle detections to isolate association performance. Unlike existing benchmarks, WaspMOT forms a closed-set tracking scenario where all individuals remain present throughout the sequence, requiring consistent identity assignment across thousands of frames despite abrupt jumps, occlusions, and highly similar appearance. We establish a benchmark by evaluating five tracking-by-detection methods, including ByteTrack, BoT-SORT, C-BIoU, OC-SORT, and McByte, under a unified protocol. Results show that all methods suffer from significant trajectory fragmentation, highlighting the difficulty of long-term identity preservation even with perfect detections. A simple spatial tracklet stitching baseline consistently improves performance, indicating that substantial gains remain possible. WaspMOT provides a new benchmark for studying long-term association and reveals limitations of current tracking approaches that are not observable on conventional datasets. The benchmark will be made publicly available at the project repository: https://github.com/tstanczyk95/WaspMOT/ .
中文标题/摘要
标题：WaspMOT：一种针对三化螟茧蜂长期多目标跟踪的基准
多目标跟踪（MOT）在以短时视频序列为主的基准上表现优异，但此类数据集无法充分评估长期身份保持能力，即物体需在长时间内保持一致跟踪。我们引入WaspMOT基准，通过在受控生态实验中对三化螟茧蜂进行长时跟踪，以填补这一空白。该数据集包含10个序列，每个约12,000帧（25 FPS下总时长约8分钟），配有密集的MOTChallenge标注和oracle检测，以隔离关联性能。 与现有基准不同，WaspMOT构成一个封闭集跟踪场景，所有个体在整个序列中持续存在，要求在数千帧内保持一致身份分配，即使面临突然跳跃、遮挡和高度相似外观。我们通过统一协议评估五种检测跟踪方法（包括ByteTrack、BoT-SORT、C-BIoU、OC-SORT和McByte），结果表明所有方法均存在显著轨迹碎片化问题，凸显即使在完美检测条件下长期身份保持的难度。一个简单的空间轨迹片段拼接基线可持续提升性能，表明仍有大量改进空间。 WaspMOT为研究长期关联提供了新基准，揭示了当前跟踪方法在传统数据集上不可见的局限性。该基准将在项目仓库公开：https://github.com/tstanczyk95/WaspMOT/。

Evaluating five state-of-the-art tracking-by-detection methods under a unified protocol, the study finds that all methods suffer from severe trajectory fragmentation, even with perfect detections, underscoring the difficulty of long-term tracking. A simple spatial tracklet stitching baseline consistently improves performance, suggesting substantial room for improvement in current methods.
为解决现有多目标跟踪基准无法评估长期身份保持的问题，作者提出了WaspMOT数据集，包含10个约12,000帧的长时序序列，记录了在受控生态实验中持续存在的Trichogramma寄生蜂，标注包含密集MOTChallenge标签和oracle检测以隔离关联性能。与传统短时序基准不同，WaspMOT构建了封闭式跟踪场景，要求在所有个体全程可见的情况下，即使面对突然跳跃、遮挡和外观高度相似，仍需保持身份一致性。在统一评估协议下对五种主流检测跟踪方法进行测试，结果表明所有方法在长期跟踪中均出现严重轨迹断裂，即使检测完美也难以维持身份连续性。一个简单的空间轨迹片段拼接基线显著提升性能，表明当前方法仍有较大改进空间。
Echoes: A semantically-aligned music deepfake detection dataset
Authors: Octavian Pascu, Dan Oneata, Horia Cucu, Nicolas M. Muller
First: 2026-03-24T19:10:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:32:39+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We introduce Echoes, a new dataset for music deepfake detection designed for training and benchmarking detectors under realistic and provider-diverse conditions. Echoes comprises 4,468 tracks (131 hours of audio) spanning multiple genres (pop, rock, electronic), and includes content generated by ten popular AI music generation systems. To prevent shortcut learning and promote robust generalization, the dataset is deliberately constructed to be challenging, enforcing semantic-level alignment between spoofed audio and bona fide references. This alignment is achieved by conditioning generated audio samples directly on bona-fide waveforms or song descriptors. We evaluate Echoes in a cross-dataset setting against three existing AI-generated music datasets using state-of-the-art Wav2Vec2 XLS-R 2B representations. Results show that (i) Echoes is the hardest in-domain dataset; (ii) detectors trained on existing datasets transfer poorly to Echoes; (iii) training on Echoes yields the strongest generalization performance. These findings suggest that provider diversity and semantic alignment help learn more transferable detection cues.
中文标题/摘要
标题：回声：一个语义对齐的音乐深度伪造检测数据集
我们引入了Echoes，这是一个专为在真实且提供者多样化的条件下训练和基准测试音乐深度伪造检测器而设计的新数据集。Echoes包含4,468首曲目（131小时音频），涵盖多种音乐类型（流行、摇滚、电子），并包含由十种流行AI音乐生成系统生成的内容。为防止捷径学习并促进稳健泛化，该数据集被刻意设计为具有挑战性，强制伪造音频与真实参考音频在语义层面保持对齐。这种对齐通过直接以真实波形或歌曲描述符为条件生成音频样本实现。我们在跨数据集设置下，使用最先进的Wav2Vec2 XLS-R 2B表示方法，将Echoes与三个现有AI生成音乐数据集进行评估。结果表明：(i) Echoes是难度最高的同域数据集；(ii) 在现有数据集上训练的检测器难以迁移到Echoes；(iii) 在Echoes上训练可获得最强的泛化性能。这些发现表明，提供者多样性与语义对齐有助于学习更具迁移性的检测特征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for realistic and provider-diverse training data to improve music deepfake detection, the authors introduce Echoes, a dataset of 4,468 tracks generated by ten popular AI music systems and aligned semantically with real audio references. To prevent shortcut learning, the dataset enforces alignment by conditioning generated audio on bona fide waveforms or descriptors. Evaluated against three existing datasets using Wav2Vec2 XLS-R 2B features, Echoes proved to be the most challenging in-domain dataset, with detectors trained on other datasets showing poor transfer performance, while those trained on Echoes achieved the strongest generalization.
为提升AI生成音乐检测模型的泛化能力，作者构建了Echoes数据集，包含10个主流AI音乐系统生成的4,468首曲目（131小时），通过将生成音频与真实音频在语义层面对齐，避免模型依赖捷径学习。实验表明，Echoes是当前最难的同类数据集，基于其他数据集训练的检测器在迁移至Echoes时表现不佳，而使用Echoes训练的模型在跨数据集泛化能力上表现最优，说明提供者多样性与语义对齐有助于提升检测模型的迁移能力。
Pose-to-Biomechanics: Bridging 3D Human Pose Estimation and Biomechanical Attribute Prediction
Authors: Ayda Eghbalian, Kevin Desai
First: 2026-07-09T17:31:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:31:16+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent progress in 3D human pose estimation has made markerless recovery of skeletal motion increasingly accurate and scalable. However, most pose estimators remain optimized for geometric keypoint accuracy, while many real-world applications in rehabilitation, sports science, ergonomics, and clinical movement analysis require biomechanical quantities that describe how the body moves, loads, and activates. In this work, we propose BioModule, a lightweight plug-in temporal transformer that attaches downstream of any 3D pose estimator and predicts biomechanical attributes from standard 17-joint 3D skeletons. BioModule is estimator-agnostic and requires no modification of the upstream pose model, enabling existing pose estimators to be extended toward physically interpretable motion analysis. To train and evaluate BioModule, we construct a large-scale aligned dataset pairing Human3.6M video and 3D keypoints with the biomechanical label space of Human3.6Mplus. We establish and verify anatomical correspondence between coordinate systems of the two datasets, enabling frame-accurate cross-modal supervision. Using this aligned supervision, BioModule predicts biomechanical quantities. We further benchmark BioModule across seven state-of-the-art 3D pose estimators, providing the first systematic analysis of how upstream pose estimation quality propagates to downstream biomechanical prediction fidelity. The results position BioModule as a compact, modular bridge between vision-based pose estimation and biomechanically meaningful human motion analysis.
中文标题/摘要
标题：从姿态到生物力学：连接3D人体姿态估计与生物力学属性预测
近年来3D人体姿态估计取得显著进展，使无标记骨骼运动恢复越来越精确且可扩展。然而，大多数姿态估计器仍仅优化几何关键点精度，而康复、运动科学、人体工学和临床运动分析等实际应用则需要描述身体运动、负荷和激活的生物力学量。本文提出BioModule，一种轻量级插件式时序变换器，可附加于任意3D姿态估计器后端，从标准17关节3D骨架预测生物力学属性。BioModule与姿态估计器无关，无需修改上游姿态模型，使现有姿态估计器可扩展至物理可解释的运动分析。 为训练和评估BioModule，我们构建了一个大规模对齐数据集，将Human3.6M视频与3D关键点与Human3.6Mplus的生物力学标签空间配对。我们建立了两个数据集坐标系间的解剖对应关系，实现帧级跨模态监督。利用该对齐监督，BioModule预测生物力学量。我们进一步在七种最先进的3D姿态估计器上对BioModule进行基准测试，首次系统分析了上游姿态估计质量如何影响下游生物力学预测精度。结果表明，BioModule是连接基于视觉的姿态估计与具有生物力学意义的人体运动分析的紧凑型模块化桥梁。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to translate 3D human pose estimates into biomechanically meaningful quantities for applications in rehabilitation and sports science, this work introduces BioModule, a lightweight temporal transformer that plugs into any 3D pose estimator to predict biomechanical attributes from standard 17-joint skeletons. The method is designed to be estimator-agnostic and requires no changes to existing pose models. Trained and evaluated on a large-scale aligned dataset combining Human3.6M video and keypoints with Human3.6Mplus biomechanical labels, BioModule leverages anatomical correspondence for frame-accurate cross-modal supervision. Experimental results across seven state-of-the-art pose estimators demonstrate that BioModule consistently improves biomechanical prediction fidelity without altering the upstream pose model, establishing it as a modular bridge between pose estimation and physically interpretable motion analysis.
为满足康复、运动科学等领域对生物力学运动指标的需求，本文提出BioModule，一种轻量级时间变换器，可插接到任意3D姿态估计器后端，从标准17关节骨架预测生物力学属性。该方法无需修改上游姿态模型，具有估计器无关性。基于将Human3.6M视频与关键点与Human3.6Mplus生物力学标签对齐的大规模数据集，BioModule利用解剖学对应关系实现帧级跨模态监督。在七种主流3D姿态估计器上的实验表明，BioModule能稳定提升生物力学预测精度，且不改变上游模型，成为连接视觉姿态估计与生物力学意义运动分析的模块化桥梁。
Latent Memory Palace: Reasoning for Control as Autoregressive Variational Inference
Authors: Chuning Zhu, Eva Xu, Jose Barreiros, Krishnan Srinivasan, Paarth Shah, Abhishek Gupta
First: 2026-07-09T17:30:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:30:49+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Human decision-making is highly flexible -- some actions are taken immediately; others require longer deliberation. Language models have exhibited a similar capacity for adaptive "reasoning." However, transferring this capability to continuous control policies has been challenging, as directly reasoning in language space may lack the granularity for spatial understanding and precise motions. In this work, we show that reasoning for control policies can emerge by organizing information in an autoregressive latent space reminiscent of a memory palace, where retrieval is iterative and adaptive. Our method, Latent Memory Palace (LMP), formulates reasoning as variational inference with an autoregressive latent distribution. We derive a latent-space reinforcement learning technique to tractably optimize its variational lower bound. The resulting policy, LMP-$π$, achieves strong empirical performance in simulation and real-world domains while exhibiting interpretable, adaptive allocation of test-time compute. We further show that the same framework yields a variable-length action tokenizer, LMP-$\texttt{tok}$, which significantly improves the performance of downstream autoregressive policies. Together, these results present a new perspective on latent reasoning for control through the lens of variational inference.
中文标题/摘要
标题：潜在记忆宫殿：作为自回归变分推断的控制推理
人类决策具有高度灵活性——某些行为立即执行，其他则需长时间思考。语言模型也表现出类似的自适应“推理”能力。然而，将此能力迁移到连续控制策略中颇具挑战，因为直接在语言空间推理可能缺乏空间理解与精确动作所需的粒度。本文表明，通过在类似记忆宫殿的自回归潜在空间中组织信息，可使控制策略的推理自然涌现，其中检索过程迭代且自适应。我们的方法“潜在记忆宫殿”（LMP）将推理形式化为具有自回归潜在分布的变分推断。我们推导出一种潜在空间强化学习技术，以可计算方式优化其变分下界。所得策略LMP-π在仿真与真实世界领域均表现优异，且在测试时展现出可解释、自适应的计算分配能力。此外，同一框架还可生成可变长度动作分词器LMP-tok，显著提升下游自回归策略的性能。这些结果共同提出了一种通过变分推断视角理解控制领域潜在推理的新思路。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to enable adaptive reasoning in continuous control policies, this work proposes Latent Memory Palace (LMP), a method that frames control reasoning as autoregressive variational inference in a latent space structured like a memory palace. The approach uses a latent-space reinforcement learning algorithm to optimize a variational lower bound, allowing the policy to iteratively retrieve and adapt information during execution. Experiments show that LMP-π achieves strong performance in both simulation and real-world tasks while dynamically allocating compute at test time. Additionally, the framework produces LMP-tok, a variable-length action tokenizer that boosts downstream autoregressive policy performance.
为实现连续控制任务中的自适应推理，本文提出Latent Memory Palace（LMP）方法，将控制策略的推理建模为在类似记忆宫殿的自回归潜在空间中的变分推理。通过将策略优化表述为潜在分布上的变分推断，LMP支持推理时迭代、自适应地检索动作，实现灵活的计算分配。实验表明，LMP-π在仿真与真实世界控制任务中表现优异且具备可解释性，其衍生的可变长度动作分词器LMP-tok显著提升了下游自回归策略的性能。
Deep Learning for Joint Narrowband Interference Cancellation and Soft Demodulation in OFDM Systems
Authors: Emmanouil Kavvousanos, Francky Catthoor, Vassilis Paliouras
First: 2026-07-09T17:26:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:26:43+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 11 figures, 6 tables. Preprint under review
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Narrowband interference (NBI) severely degrades orthogonal frequency-division multiplexing (OFDM) systems by corrupting subcarriers and rendering classical soft demodulation ineffective. Conventional compressed-sensing (CS) mitigation exhibits high sequential latency and leaves structured, non-Gaussian residuals that cause log-likelihood ratio (LLR) unreliability, decoder saturation, and severe error floors when employing classical Gaussian demappers. We resolve this pipeline mismatch using a unified deep learning framework for joint NBI cancellation and robust soft demodulation. First, NBI-CNet employs a physics-informed convolutional architecture to estimate NBI parameters and remove multi-tone interference in a single forward pass. Without requiring prior knowledge of the active interferer count, NBI-CNet reduces computational complexity by up to 60% ($N{=}2048, Q{=}64$) compared to the state-of-the-art EOMP-IDS algorithm. Second, LLR-CNet acts as a structural whitener by mapping non-Gaussian post-mitigation residuals onto well-calibrated soft metrics. Simulations demonstrate that this joint framework eliminates the error floors inherent to traditional baselines across dense grids. Under severe interference ($\text{SIR}{=}{-}10$ dB), the pipeline operates within a $0.2$ to $0.5$ dB SNR margin of the optimal iterative baseline at a target block error rate (BLER) of $10^{-4}$. Under mild interference ($\text{SIR}{=}10$ dB) with heavy spectral overlap ($Q{=}12$), where classical greedy algorithms erroneously subtract valid data components and corrupt the payload, NBI-CNet avoids signal-peak confusion to deliver a coding gain exceeding $3$ dB. Finally, the architecture circumvents the $2{\times}10^{-4}$ error floor triggered by interferer-estimation errors, while its scale-invariant design enables robust generalization across arbitrary FFT sizes without retraining.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于OFDM系统中窄带干扰抑制与软解调联合处理的深度学习方法
窄带干扰（NBI）严重破坏正交频分复用（OFDM）系统，破坏子载波并使经典软解调失效。传统压缩感知（CS）抑制方法存在高时序延迟，且残留结构化、非高斯噪声，导致对数似然比（LLR）不可靠、解码器饱和及严重误码地板，尤其在使用经典高斯解映射器时。本文采用统一深度学习框架解决该处理流程不匹配问题，实现NBI抑制与鲁棒软解调联合处理。首先，NBI-CNet采用物理信息引导的卷积架构，在单次前向传播中估计NBI参数并消除多音干扰，无需已知激活干扰器数量，在N=2048、Q=64条件下较当前最优EOMP-IDS算法降低60%计算复杂度。其次，LLR-CNet作为结构白化器，将抑制后非高斯残差映射为校准良好的软度量。仿真表明，该联合框架在密集网格下消除传统基线固有的误码地板。在严重干扰（SIR=-10 dB）下，系统在目标块误码率（BLER）为10^{-4}时，性能仅比最优迭代基线低0.2至0.5 dB SNR。在轻度干扰（SIR=10 dB）且频谱重叠严重（Q=12）时，经典贪婪算法错误减去有效数据分量并破坏载荷，NBI-CNet避免信号峰值混淆，提供超过3 dB的编码增益。最后，该架构规避由干扰器估计误差引发的2×10^{-4}误码地板，其尺度不变设计支持在任意FFT尺寸下无需再训练实现鲁棒泛化。
Summary / 总结
To address the degradation of OFDM systems caused by narrowband interference (NBI), which corrupts subcarriers and undermines classical soft demodulation, this paper proposes a unified deep learning framework combining NBI cancellation and robust soft demodulation. The NBI-CNet module uses a physics-informed convolutional architecture to estimate and remove multi-tone interference in a single forward pass, reducing computational complexity by up to 60% compared to state-of-the-art methods. The LLR-CNet module then transforms non-Gaussian residuals into reliable soft metrics, eliminating error floors seen in traditional approaches. Experiments show that under severe interference (SIR = -10 dB), the system operates within 0.2–0.5 dB of the optimal iterative baseline at 10^{-4} BLER, and under heavy spectral overlap (Q=12), it achieves over 3 dB coding gain by avoiding signal-peak confusion. The architecture also avoids a 2×10^{-4} error floor from interferer estimation errors and generalizes across arbitrary FFT sizes without retraining.

Remember When It Matters: Proactive Memory Agent for Long-Horizon Agents
Authors: Yifan Wu, Lizhu Zhang, Yuhang Zhou, Mingyi Wang, Bo Peng, Serena Li, Xiangjun Fan, Zhuokai Zhao
First: 2026-07-09T17:26:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:26:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In long-horizon tasks, decision-relevant state is often scattered across an expanding trajectory, while the action agent must surface it and act. As trajectories grow, task requirements, environment facts, prior attempts, diagnoses, and open subgoals can be buried in the context window or pushed beyond it, failing to influence decisions when needed. We call this failure mode "behavioral state decay". We study memory as an active intervention mechanism rather than passive retrieval. A separate memory agent runs alongside an unmodified action agent, updating a structured memory bank from the recent trajectory and deciding whether to inject a memory-grounded reminder or remain silent. The module is plug-and-play with frontier action agents and existing agent harnesses. Across Terminal-Bench 2.0 and $τ^2$-Bench, it improves pass@1 for both weaker and stronger action agents, with gains of +8.3 pp on Terminal-Bench and +6.8 pp on $τ^2$-Bench. Ablations show that selective intervention outperforms passive bank exposure, always-on injection, advisor-only guidance, and general retrieval. As an early step toward open-weight memory policies, we train Qwen3.5-27B on SETA using SFT and GRPO, improving validation reward and achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关键时刻才记起：面向长时程代理的主动记忆代理
在长时程任务中，决策相关的状态常分散于不断扩展的轨迹中，而行动代理必须将其浮现并采取行动。随着轨迹增长，任务要求、环境事实、先前尝试、诊断结果和未完成子目标可能被埋入上下文窗口或超出其范围，导致在需要时无法影响决策。我们称此失效模式为“行为状态衰减”。我们将记忆视为一种主动干预机制，而非被动检索。一个独立的记忆代理与未经修改的行动代理并行运行，从近期轨迹中更新结构化记忆库，并决定是否注入基于记忆的提醒或保持沉默。该模块可与前沿行动代理及现有代理框架即插即用。在Terminal-Bench 2.0和$τ^2$-Bench上，它提升了两类行动代理的pass@1表现，分别在Terminal-Bench上提升+8.3 pp，在$τ^2$-Bench上提升+6.8 pp。消融实验表明，选择性干预优于被动记忆库暴露、持续注入、仅顾问引导和通用检索。作为迈向开放权重记忆策略的初步步骤，我们使用SFT和GRPO在SETA上训练Qwen3.5-27B，提升了验证奖励，并实现了部分向Terminal-Bench的迁移。
Summary / 总结
To address the issue of 'behavioral state decay'—where critical information decays from memory in long-horizon tasks—the paper introduces a proactive memory agent that operates alongside an unmodified action agent. This agent selectively updates a structured memory bank from recent trajectory data and injects memory-grounded reminders only when necessary, avoiding passive retrieval or constant intervention. Experiments on Terminal-Bench 2.0 and τ²-Bench show consistent performance gains: +8.3 pp in pass@1 for weaker agents and +6.8 pp for stronger ones, outperforming alternatives like always-on injection or advisor-only guidance. The memory agent is designed to be plug-and-play with existing frameworks and was trained on SETA using SFT and GRPO, achieving partial transfer to Terminal-Bench.
为解决长时任务中‘行为状态衰减’问题——即关键任务信息在轨迹扩展后难以被访问——论文提出一种主动记忆代理，与未修改的动作代理并行运行。该代理从近期轨迹中主动更新结构化记忆库，并在必要时选择性注入记忆引导的提醒，而非被动暴露记忆。在Terminal-Bench 2.0和τ²-Bench上的实验表明，该方法对弱代理和强代理均提升pass@1指标：分别提高8.3pp和6.8pp，证明选择性干预优于被动记忆访问、持续注入或仅顾问引导。该记忆代理设计为可插拔式，与现有代理兼容；Qwen3.5-27B模型在SETA上通过SFT和GRPO训练后，验证奖励提升并实现部分迁移至Terminal-Bench。
LTM: Large-scale Terrain Model for Wildfire-prone Landscapes
Authors: Xiao Fu, Yue Hu, Meida Chen, Peter Anthony Beerel, Barath Raghavan
First: 2026-07-09T17:22:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:22:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Accurate 3D terrain maps are essential for emergency response when assessing wildfire hazards. However, wildfire-prone regions often span vast areas where conventional reconstruction methods underperform. Airborne LiDAR systems provide high-resolution terrain data, but they are expensive and infrequently updated. Image-based methods offer a lower-cost alternative, but struggle due to sparse visual features and limited image overlap. We propose a multi-modal reconstruction framework leveraging outdated Digital Elevation Models (DEMs) as geometric priors for image-based 3D reconstruction. Our key innovation is physics-based pixel-pixel alignment between images and DEM data, dramatically reducing computational complexity by eliminating expensive feature matching procedures. To validate our approach, we developed a large-terrain simulator based on a real wildfire-prone area, generating realistic images enabling a comprehensive evaluation. Given posed images and legacy DEMs, our method produces high-fidelity depth maps while maintaining real-time performance. We find significant improvements in reconstruction accuracy and computational efficiency over existing techniques, offering a scalable solution for wildfire response.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LTM：面向易发生野火景观的大规模地形模型
在评估野火危害时，精确的三维地形图对应急响应至关重要。然而，易发生野火的区域往往覆盖广阔，传统重建方法表现不佳。机载激光雷达系统可提供高分辨率地形数据，但成本高昂且更新频率低。基于图像的方法成本较低，但受限于视觉特征稀疏和图像重叠度不足。我们提出一种多模态重建框架，利用过时的数字高程模型（DEM）作为图像三维重建的几何先验。我们的关键创新是基于物理原理的图像与DEM数据像素级对齐，通过消除昂贵的特征匹配过程，大幅降低计算复杂度。为验证该方法，我们基于真实易发生野火区域开发了大规模地形模拟器，生成逼真图像以实现全面评估。给定输入图像和历史DEM，我们的方法可生成高保真深度图并保持实时性能。与现有技术相比，该方法在重建精度和计算效率上均有显著提升，为野火应急响应提供可扩展的解决方案。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for accurate 3D terrain models in wildfire-prone regions where conventional methods fail due to cost or data sparsity, this work proposes a multi-modal reconstruction framework that uses outdated Digital Elevation Models as geometric priors to guide image-based 3D reconstruction. The method introduces a physics-based pixel-pixel alignment technique that bypasses expensive feature matching, drastically reducing computational load while preserving reconstruction fidelity. Evaluated on a large-terrain simulator based on a real wildfire-prone area, the approach generates high-fidelity depth maps in real time, demonstrating significant gains in both accuracy and efficiency compared to existing methods, making it a scalable solution for emergency response.
为解决大范围野火易发区因成本高或数据稀疏导致传统方法失效的问题，作者提出一种多模态重建框架，利用过时的数字高程模型作为几何先验引导基于图像的三维重建。其核心创新是采用基于物理的像素级对齐技术，绕过昂贵的特征匹配步骤，大幅降低计算开销同时保持重建精度。在基于真实野火易发区构建的大型地形模拟器上评估时，该方法能实时生成高保真深度图，显著优于现有方法，在精度和效率上均有明显提升，适用于大规模应急响应场景。
HumanForge: A Human-Centric Deepfake Video Benchmark with Multi-Agent Forgery Rationales
Authors: Wenbo Xu, Zhimin Chen, Xiaojie Liang, Hengrui Liu, Wei Lu
First: 2026-07-09T17:12:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:12:41+00:00
Comments: 6 pages, 2 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Rapid advancements in video diffusion models and temporal editing tools have enabled the generation of highly realistic human-centric videos, posing unprecedented challenges to digital content forensics. Existing benchmarks primarily focus on either face-swapping or global text-to-video synthesis, overlooking the crucial dimensions of human-object or human-human interactions and multi-modal alignment. To address these limitations, we introduce HumanForge, a unified, large-scale, and multi-paradigm human-centric video forgery dataset. To construct and annotate this dataset without labor-intensive manual labeling or hallucinated monolithic prompts, we propose Gen2Anno, a modular active multi-agent pipeline built on LangGraph. Gen2Anno coordinates six specialized agents-ranging from source profiling to MoE-based reference analysis and closed-loop forensic verification-to generate over 18K high-fidelity video segments and produce structured, contrastive omni-annotations containing binary decisions, fine-grained artifact categories, and spatio-temporal localization. Extensive benchmarks using state-of-the-art traditional detectors and Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) demonstrate the significant challenges of zero-shot generalization and fine-grained reasoning on HumanForge. Code and dataset will be publicly released.
中文标题/摘要
标题：HumanForge：面向以人为中心的深度伪造视频的多智能体伪造理由统一基准
视频扩散模型与时间编辑工具的快速发展，使得生成高度逼真的人类中心视频成为可能，对数字内容取证提出了前所未有的挑战。现有基准主要关注人脸交换或全局文本到视频合成，忽视了人与物体或人与人交互以及多模态对齐等关键维度。为解决这些局限，我们提出了HumanForge，一个统一、大规模、多范式的以人为中心的视频伪造数据集。为构建和标注该数据集，避免繁重的人工标注或虚构的单体提示，我们提出Gen2Anno，一个基于LangGraph的模块化主动多智能体流水线。Gen2Anno协调六个专业智能体——从源特征分析到基于MoE的参考分析及闭环取证验证——生成超过18,000个高保真视频片段，并产出结构化、对比式的全标注，包含二元决策、细粒度伪影类别及时空定位信息。使用最先进的传统检测器和大型多模态模型（LMMs）进行的广泛基准测试表明，HumanForge对零样本泛化和细粒度推理提出了重大挑战。代码与数据集将公开发布。

Driven by the growing realism of deepfake videos and the need for robust forensic tools, this work introduces HumanForge, a large-scale benchmark designed to evaluate human-centric video forgery across diverse interaction scenarios. 为应对生成式视频模型带来的高仿真伪造挑战，本文提出HumanForge数据集，覆盖人与物、人与人交互等多模态对齐场景，弥补现有基准的不足。通过基于LangGraph的多智能体系统Gen2Anno，自动协调六个专业代理完成18K高质量视频片段的标注，生成包含二元判断、细粒度伪影类别和时空定位的结构化对比标注。实验表明，传统检测器与大模型在该数据集上均难以实现零样本泛化与细粒度推理，凸显其对数字取证模型评估的挑战性与价值。
MPFlow: Learning Budgeted Max-Flow Optimization on the Lightning Network with Deep Graph Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Harrison Rush, Vincent Davis, Simone Antonelli, Vikash Singh, Jesse Shrader, Emanuele Rossi
First: 2026-07-09T17:09:20+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:09:20+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We address liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network (LN): given a fixed budget, which channels should a node open to maximize its routing capacity? We cast this as a budget-constrained combinatorial optimization problem on graphs, selecting $k$ edge additions that maximize $s$--$t$ max-flow, a theory-grounded measure of routing capacity, and solve it with graph reinforcement learning. Our lightweight agent combines a message-passing policy network with proximal policy optimization (PPO) and action masking, and is trained under a hub-exclusion curriculum: the network's top hubs are removed from training subgraphs, forcing the policy to learn capacity-aware placement rather than hub attachment. In extensive experiments on real Lightning Network snapshots, our method consistently outperforms strong heuristic baselines on the max-flow objective across multiple seeds and unseen graphs. The agent has been deployed in production for peer recommendations, executing 4640 channel-open decisions that cumulatively allocate 267.3 BTC over $16 million across 30 managed nodes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：MPFlow：使用深度图强化学习在闪电网络上学习预算受限的最大流优化
我们解决比特币闪电网络（LN）中的流动性布局问题：在预算固定的情况下，节点应打开哪些通道以最大化其路由容量？我们将此问题建模为图上的预算约束组合优化问题，选择k条边的添加以最大化s--t最大流（一种基于理论的路由容量度量），并使用图强化学习求解。我们的轻量级代理结合了消息传递策略网络、近端策略优化（PPO）和动作掩码，并在排除枢纽的课程中训练：训练子图中移除网络的顶级枢纽，迫使策略学习容量感知的布局而非枢纽连接。在真实闪电网络快照的广泛实验中，我们的方法在多个种子和未见过的图上，始终优于强启发式基线的最大流目标。该代理已在生产环境中部署用于同行推荐，执行了4640次通道开通决策，累计在30个管理节点上分配了267.3 BTC，总预算达1600万美元。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to optimize liquidity placement in the Bitcoin Lightning Network under budget constraints, the authors frame the problem as selecting k edge additions to maximize s-t max-flow in a graph. They solve this via a lightweight graph reinforcement learning agent combining message-passing networks, PPO, and action masking, trained with a hub-exclusion curriculum to avoid hub-centric biases. Experiments on real LN snapshots show consistent outperformance over heuristic baselines on max-flow, and the agent has been deployed to make 4,640 channel-opening decisions allocating 267.3 BTC across 30 nodes.
为在预算约束下优化比特币闪电网络中的流动性布局，作者将问题建模为在图中选择k条边以最大化s-t最大流。他们采用深度图强化学习求解，训练了一个结合消息传递网络、PPO和动作掩码的轻量级代理，并通过排除枢纽节点的课程学习机制，促使代理学习容量感知的布局策略。在真实闪电网络快照上的实验表明，该方法在最大流目标上持续优于多种启发式基线，且已在生产环境中部署，完成4640次通道开通决策，累计分配267.3 BTC至30个节点。
Do You Need a Frontier Model as a Citation Verifier? Benchmarking Rubric LLMs for Deep-Research Source Attribution
Authors: Ethan Leung, Elias Lumer, Corey Feld, Austin Huber, Vamse Kumar Subbiah, Kevin Paul
First: 2026-07-09T17:01:40+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:01:40+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Reinforcement learning increasingly relies on an LLM judge to score each rubric criterion, and that judge acts as the reward model during training. Before such a signal can be trusted, we need to know how capable the judge must be and how biased it is. We study this calibration question for citation quality in deep-research systems, where a search-grounded LLM must support each claim it writes with a cited source. Citation quality is a structured rubric task in which each attribution-citation pair is judged along two dimensions that require an LLM, source relevance and factual support. On an adversarial long-form benchmark, we score 8 off-the-shelf LLM judges from 3 model families against gold labels over 1,248 rubric decisions, all of which were human-reviewed and 378 of which were hard cases adjudicated from judge disagreements. Cheaper judges remain competitive across both dimensions, with GPT-5-mini attaining the strongest source-relevance pass-class F1 at 0.908 ($κ$=0.636), while on factual support the judges are statistically indistinguishable (overlapping confidence intervals), so no single model dominates. At comparable F1, the judges still differ substantially in pass-rate drift, false positive rate, and false negative rate. Scalar F1 obscures this directional bias, yet it is exactly what a downstream reinforcement learning loop would reinforce. Calibrating the judge is therefore a prerequisite for using citation rubrics as reward signals, and our results show that this calibration does not require the most expensive available model.
中文标题/摘要
标题：你需要前沿模型作为引文验证器吗？深度研究源引用的LLM基准评估
强化学习日益依赖LLM裁判对每个评分标准进行打分，该裁判在训练中充当奖励模型。在信任此类信号之前，我们需要了解裁判的能力水平及其偏倚程度。我们针对深度研究系统中的引文质量校准问题展开研究，其中搜索引导的LLM必须为其撰写的每个主张提供引用来源。引文质量是一个结构化评分任务，每对引用-归属关系需从两个维度进行评判：来源相关性和事实支持，这两者均需LLM判断。在对抗性长文本基准上，我们对8个来自3个模型家族的现成LLM裁判，基于1248个评分决策与黄金标签进行评分，所有决策均经人工审核，其中378个为裁判分歧的复杂案例。低成本裁判在两个维度上仍具竞争力，GPT-5-mini在来源相关性通过率F1上表现最佳（0.908，κ=0.636）；而在事实支持维度，各裁判统计上无显著差异（置信区间重叠），故无单一模型占优。在F1相近的情况下，裁判在通过率漂移、假阳性率和假阴性率上仍存在显著差异。标量F1掩盖了这种方向性偏倚，而这正是下游强化学习循环所强化的内容。因此，校准裁判是将引文评分作为奖励信号的前提，我们的结果表明，该校准无需最昂贵的可用模型。
Summary / 总结
This study addresses the need to calibrate LLM judges used in reinforcement learning for citation verification in deep-research systems, where models must attribute claims to sources. The authors evaluate eight off-the-shelf LLMs across two rubric dimensions—source relevance and factual support—on a 1,248-item adversarial benchmark, with 378 hard cases resolved via human adjudication. Results show that cheaper models like GPT-5-mini perform competitively, achieving the highest source-relevance F1 (0.908, κ=0.636), while all models perform similarly on factual support, indicating no clear dominance. Despite similar overall F1 scores, judges vary significantly in directional bias metrics such as pass-rate drift, false positives, and false negatives, which scalar F1 fails to capture. These biases could misguide reinforcement learning, underscoring that judge calibration is essential and can be achieved without resorting to the most expensive models.
为评估用于强化学习的LLM裁判在引文验证中的校准效果，本研究在包含1248个经人工审核的引文决策（含378个争议案例）的基准测试上，对八款现成LLM在两个维度——来源相关性和事实支持——的表现进行评估。结果表明，低成本模型如GPT-5-mini在来源相关性上表现最优（F1=0.908，κ=0.636），而各模型在事实支持维度上统计表现无显著差异。尽管F1分数相近，各模型在通过率漂移、假阳性与假阴性等方向性偏差指标上差异显著，而F1分数无法反映这些偏差，可能误导强化学习训练。因此，裁判校准是将引文评分作为奖励信号前的必要步骤，且无需依赖最昂贵模型即可实现有效校准。
XFACTORS: Disentangled Information Bottleneck via Contrastive Supervision
Authors: Alexandre Myara, Nicolas Bourriez, Thomas Boyer, Thomas Lemercier, Ihab Bendidi, Auguste Genovesio
First: 2026-01-29T13:20:48+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T17:00:50+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Disentangled representation learning aims to map independent factors of variation to independent representation components. On one hand, purely unsupervised approaches have proven successful on fully disentangled synthetic data, but fail to recover semantic factors from real data without strong inductive biases. On the other hand, supervised approaches are unstable and hard to scale to large attribute sets because they rely on adversarial objectives or auxiliary classifiers. We introduce \textsc{XFactors}, a weakly-supervised VAE framework that disentangles and provides explicit control over a chosen set of factors. Building on the Disentangled Information Bottleneck perspective, we decompose the representation into a residual subspace $\mathcal{S}$ and factor-specific subspaces $\mathcal{T}_1,\ldots,\mathcal{T}_K$ and a residual subspace $\mathcal{S}$. Each target factor is encoded in its assigned $\mathcal{T}_i$ through contrastive supervision: an InfoNCE loss pulls together latents sharing the same factor value and pushes apart mismatched pairs. In parallel, KL regularization imposes a Gaussian structure on both $\mathcal{S}$ and the aggregated factor subspaces, organizing the geometry without additional supervision for non-targeted factors and avoiding adversarial training and classifiers. Across multiple datasets, with constant hyperparameters, \textsc{XFactors} achieves state-of-the-art disentanglement scores and yields consistent qualitative factor alignment in the corresponding subspaces, enabling controlled factor swapping via latent replacement. We further demonstrate that our method scales correctly with increasing latent capacity and evaluate it on the real-world dataset CelebA. Our code is available at \href{https://github.com/ICML26-anon/XFactors}{github.com/ICML26-anon/XFactors}.
中文标题/摘要
标题：XFACTORS：通过对比监督实现解耦的信息瓶颈
解耦表征学习旨在将变化的独立因素映射到独立的表征组件。一方面，纯无监督方法在完全解耦的合成数据上表现成功，但在缺乏强归纳偏置的情况下无法从真实数据中恢复语义因素；另一方面，监督方法不稳定，难以扩展到大型属性集，因为它们依赖对抗性目标或辅助分类器。 我们提出了\textsc{XFactors}，一种弱监督的VAE框架，可解耦并提供对选定因素的显式控制。基于解耦信息瓶颈视角，我们将表征分解为残差子空间$\mathcal{S}$和因素特定子空间$\mathcal{T}_1,\ldots,\mathcal{T}_K$及残差子空间$\mathcal{S}$。每个目标因素通过对比监督编码在其分配的$\mathcal{T}_i$中：InfoNCE损失将具有相同因素值的潜在变量拉近，将不匹配的对推远。同时，KL正则化在$\mathcal{S}$和聚合因素子空间上施加高斯结构，无需额外监督即可组织非目标因素的几何结构，避免对抗训练和分类器。 在多个数据集上，\textsc{XFactors}在恒定超参数下实现最先进的解耦得分，并在相应子空间中保持一致的定性因素对齐，支持通过潜在变量替换实现可控因素交换。我们进一步证明该方法随潜在容量增加而正确扩展，并在真实世界数据集CelebA上进行评估。代码可在\href{https://github.com/ICML26-anon/XFactors}{github.com/ICML26-anon/XFactors}获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of purely unsupervised methods on real-world data and the instability of supervised approaches, the paper proposes XFactors, a weakly-supervised VAE framework that disentangles representations using contrastive supervision. It decomposes latent space into a residual subspace and factor-specific subspaces, where each factor is encoded via InfoNCE loss to pull similar factor values together and push dissimilar ones apart. KL regularization enforces Gaussian structure on both residual and factor subspaces, enabling disentanglement without adversarial training or auxiliary classifiers. Experiments across multiple datasets show XFactors achieves state-of-the-art disentanglement scores and consistent qualitative factor alignment, supporting controlled latent swapping, and scales effectively with latent capacity, including on CelebA.
为了解决纯无监督方法在真实数据上难以恢复语义因素、监督方法不稳定且难以扩展的问题，XFactors 提出一种弱监督 VAE 框架，通过对比监督实现表示解耦。该框架将潜在空间分解为残差子空间与因子特异性子空间，每个因子通过 InfoNCE 损失编码：拉近相同因子值的潜在表示，推远不同因子值的对。KL 正则化在残差与因子子空间上强制高斯结构，无需对抗训练或辅助分类器即可组织非目标因子的几何结构。实验表明，XFactors 在多个数据集上以固定超参取得最优解耦得分，具备稳定因子对齐与可控潜在替换能力，并随潜在容量增长而正确扩展，已在 CelebA 数据集上验证。
How YouTube Frames ChatGPT Use in Education: An Epistemic Network Analysis with Supporting Multimodal Metadata
Authors: Shayla Sharmin, Mohammad Al-Ratrout, Mohammad Fahim Abrar, Roghayeh Leila Barmaki
Venue: In Proceedings of the 28th ACM International Conference on Multimodal Interaction (ICMI '26), Napoli, Italy, October 2026
First: 2026-07-09T16:55:46+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:55:46+00:00
Comments: This paper has been accepted in ICMI 2026 and will be presented in October
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We examine educational YouTube videos through multimodal metadata, such as transcripts, titles, thumbnails, and viewer comments, to investigate how ChatGPT is framed across creator groups and how those framings relate to audience response and platform reach. Little is known about how large language models are presented to learners in informal, creator-driven public discourse. Following PRISMA, we selected 52 videos for analysis. We identified three structurally distinct discourse groups: (G1) videos that positioned ChatGPT as a conceptual scaffold for thinking, (G2) videos oriented toward retrieval practice and skill-building, and (G3) videos that framed ChatGPT as a tool for output generation. Epistemic Network Analysis revealed statistically significant group differences with large effect sizes. Multimodal metadata consistently reflected these distinctions across transcript discourse, titles, and thumbnails. Viewers of learning-oriented content described ChatGPT as a thinking partner or tutor, whereas viewers of output-oriented content raised concerns about over-reliance, surface-level learning, and cognitive offloading. G3 achieved comparable platform reach to G2, yet with substantially weaker learning-oriented framing. This may suggest that output-oriented content competes for visibility despite lower pedagogical depth. These findings reveal a structural tension in self-directed AI learning: content that prioritizes quick outputs reaches far more learners than content that promotes deep engagement. This gap raises critical questions about whose vision of AI literacy scales and what learners are actually left with.
中文标题/摘要
标题：YouTube如何呈现ChatGPT在教育中的应用：基于多模态元数据的支持性知识网络分析
我们通过转录文本、标题、缩略图和观众评论等多模态元数据，分析教育类YouTube视频，探讨不同创作者群体如何呈现ChatGPT，以及这些呈现方式如何与观众反应和平台影响力相关。目前对大型语言模型在非正式、创作者主导的公共话语中如何向学习者呈现知之甚少。遵循PRISMA方法，我们选取了52个视频进行分析。我们识别出三个结构上不同的话语群体：(G1) 将ChatGPT定位为思维概念支架的视频；(G2) 以检索练习和技能构建为导向的视频；(G3) 将ChatGPT呈现为输出生成工具的视频。知识网络分析揭示了各群体间具有统计学显著差异且效应量较大的区别。多模态元数据在转录话语、标题和缩略图中一致反映了这些差异。学习导向内容的观众将ChatGPT描述为思维伙伴或导师，而输出导向内容的观众则表达了对过度依赖、浅层学习和认知卸载的担忧。G3的平台影响力与G2相当，但其学习导向的呈现显著较弱。这可能表明，尽管教学深度较低，输出导向内容仍能竞争可见性。这些发现揭示了自主式AI学习中的结构性张力：优先快速输出的内容比促进深度参与的内容触及更多学习者。这一差距引发了关键问题：谁的AI素养观得以规模化，学习者最终真正获得的是什么？
Summary / 总结
This study investigates how ChatGPT is portrayed in educational YouTube videos using multimodal metadata including transcripts, titles, thumbnails, and comments, aiming to understand framing differences across creator groups and their impact on audience perception and platform reach. Following PRISMA guidelines, 52 videos were analyzed and grouped into three distinct discourse categories: G1 frames ChatGPT as a conceptual scaffold for thinking, G2 as a tool for retrieval and skill-building, and G3 as an output-generation tool.
