ProjAgent: Procedural Similarity Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation
Authors: QiHong Chen, Aaron Imani, Iftekhar Ahmed
First: 2026-07-09T16:50:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:50:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Repository-level code generation requires implementing target functions while accounting for complex cross-file dependencies and project-specific conventions. Existing retrieval methods predominantly rely on lexical, structural, or semantic similarity, often overlooking repository functions that implement similar procedural logic despite differing in identifiers or application domains. We propose ProjAgent, a repository-level code generation system that introduces procedural similarity as an explicit retrieval signal. ProjAgent decomposes the target function into intermediate reasoning steps and employs an agentic workflow to retrieve repository functions that exhibit similar procedural behavior at each step. The retrieved procedural context is integrated with conventional semantic retrieval to construct a richer repository context for code generation. ProjAgent further incorporates a conservative static-analysis feedback loop that iteratively repairs generated code using compiler and static-analysis feedback. Evaluated on REPOCOD, ProjAgent achieves 41.14% Pass@1, outperforming existing retrieval-based baselines. These results demonstrate that procedural similarity is an effective and previously unexplored retrieval dimension for repository-level code generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ProjAgent：面向仓库级代码生成的程序相似性检索
仓库级代码生成需在考虑复杂跨文件依赖和项目特定约定的前提下实现目标函数。现有检索方法主要依赖词法、结构或语义相似性，常忽略那些虽标识符或应用领域不同但实现相似程序逻辑的仓库函数。我们提出ProjAgent，一种仓库级代码生成系统，将程序相似性作为显式检索信号。ProjAgent将目标函数分解为中间推理步骤，并采用代理工作流，在每一步检索具有相似程序行为的仓库函数。所检索的程序上下文与传统语义检索结合，构建更丰富的仓库上下文用于代码生成。ProjAgent进一步引入保守静态分析反馈循环，利用编译器和静态分析反馈迭代修复生成代码。在REPOCOD数据集上的评估显示，ProjAgent达到41.14%的Pass@1，优于现有基于检索的基线方法。结果表明，程序相似性是仓库级代码生成中一种有效且此前未被探索的检索维度。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to handle complex cross-file dependencies and project-specific conventions in repository-level code generation, ProjAgent introduces procedural similarity as a novel retrieval signal. It decomposes target functions into reasoning steps and retrieves repository functions with similar procedural behavior at each step via an agentic workflow, then integrates this with semantic retrieval to enrich context. Evaluated on REPOCOD, ProjAgent achieves 41.14% Pass@1, outperforming existing retrieval-based baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of procedural similarity for code generation.
为解决在仓库级别生成代码时需兼顾跨文件依赖与项目约定的挑战，ProjAgent提出将过程相似性作为新的检索信号，补充传统词法、结构和语义方法。它将目标函数分解为推理步骤，通过代理工作流检索每步具有相似过程行为的仓库函数，并融合语义上下文以增强代码生成。在REPOCOD数据集上，ProjAgent达到41.14%的Pass@1，优于现有检索基线，证明过程相似性在仓库级代码生成中具有有效性。
A Practical Investigation of Training-free Relaxed Speculative Decoding
Authors: Guoxuan Xia, Luka Ribar, Paul Balanca
First: 2026-07-09T16:50:41+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:50:41+00:00
Comments: preprint
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates sampling from an autoregressive LLM by using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens which are then verified in parallel by the LLM. Standard speculative decoding is lossless: its rejection and resampling steps exactly preserve the LLM's sampling distribution. Recent work argues that relaxing this strict guarantee can yield further speed-ups, controlled capability-speed trade-offs, or even capability gains. We practically investigate training-free relaxed speculative decoding techniques, unify existing approaches within a shared framework, benchmark them on contemporary settings, and distil takeaways and empirical findings for practitioners. Important takeaways include: relaxation can require considerable capability evaluation unlike lossless speculative decoding, and many relaxed approaches rely on a drafter that is a good language model, making them unsuited for lightweight dedicated multi-token-prediction drafters.
中文标题/摘要
标题：无训练放松式推测解码的实用研究
推测解码通过使用更快的辅助模型草拟令牌，再由大语言模型并行验证，从而加速自回归大语言模型的采样。标准推测解码是无损的：其拒绝与重采样步骤精确保持大语言模型的采样分布。近期研究认为，放松这一严格保证可带来进一步加速、可控的能力-速度权衡，甚至能力提升。本文对无训练放松式推测解码技术进行实用研究，统一现有方法于同一框架内，于当前设置中进行基准测试，并为从业者提炼经验与实证发现。重要结论包括：放松可能需要大量能力评估，与无损推测解码不同；许多放松方法依赖于表现良好的语言模型作为草拟器，因此不适合轻量级专用多令牌预测草拟器。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the potential for speed gains and capability trade-offs beyond strict lossless decoding, this work investigates training-free relaxed speculative decoding methods, unifying existing approaches under a common framework and benchmarking them on modern settings. The method involves using a faster auxiliary model to draft tokens, which are then verified by the main LLM, with relaxation allowing for approximate rather than exact distribution preservation. Experimental results show that relaxation can significantly increase speed but often demands more capability evaluation and typically requires a high-quality language model drafter, making it less suitable for lightweight or specialized drafters.
为探索超越严格无损解码的加速潜力与能力权衡，本文实证研究了无需训练的宽松推测解码技术，将现有方法统一于一个框架下并在现代场景中进行基准测试。实验发现，放宽无损约束通常需要更严格的模型能力评估，且多数方法依赖高质量语言模型作为草稿生成器，难以适配轻量级专用多token预测器。结果表明，尽管宽松方法可带来加速，但其效果高度依赖草稿模型质量与评估开销，实际部署需谨慎设计。
SAM-MT: Real-Time Interactive Multi-Target Video Segmentation
Authors: Ruiqi Shen, Chang Liu, Henghui Ding
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-09T16:49:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:49:57+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026, Project Page: https://henghuiding.com/SAM-MT/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Modern Video Object Segmentation (VOS) involves tracking and segmenting user-specified targets. While recent approaches have achieved remarkable performance in single-target scenarios, extending them to multi-target settings typically involves replicating the single-target processing for each individual object, resulting in reduced frame rates (FPS) with unbounded latency as target count increases. Built upon Segment Anything 2 (SAM2), we propose SAM-MT, which addresses this by transforming the model into an interactive framework for real-time Multi-Target video segmentation. SAM-MT uses explicit queries to represent different individual targets, in parallel with a shared representation for global context. It employs decoupled masked attention to keep individual identities distinct from cross-target interference, and sparse memory for stable temporal evolution, along with specialized strategies for occlusion handling and overlap prevention. SAM-MT successfully decouples latency from the number of targets, achieving real-time speed on par with single-target baselines (>36 FPS for 10 targets) while maintaining SAM2's robust video segmentation performance.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SAM-MT：实时交互式多目标视频分割
现代视频目标分割（VOS）涉及跟踪和分割用户指定的目标。尽管近期方法在单目标场景中表现卓越，但将其扩展到多目标场景通常需为每个目标重复单目标处理，导致帧率下降，且目标数量增加时延迟无界增长。基于Segment Anything 2（SAM2），我们提出SAM-MT，通过将模型转化为交互式框架，实现实时多目标视频分割。SAM-MT使用显式查询表示不同个体目标，同时共享全局上下文表示。它采用解耦掩码注意力保持各目标身份独立，避免跨目标干扰，并结合稀疏记忆实现稳定的时间演化，以及专门的遮挡处理与重叠防止策略。SAM-MT成功将延迟与目标数量解耦，实现与单目标基线相当的实时速度（10个目标时>36 FPS），同时保持SAM2强大的视频分割性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of current multi-target video segmentation methods that suffer from latency scaling with target count, SAM-MT leverages Segment Anything 2 to build an interactive framework that processes multiple targets in parallel using explicit queries and shared global context. It employs decoupled masked attention to preserve individual target identities and sparse memory for stable temporal tracking, along with occlusion and overlap handling strategies. Experimental results show SAM-MT achieves real-time performance (>36 FPS even with 10 targets) while maintaining SAM2’s segmentation accuracy, effectively decoupling latency from target count.
为了解决单目标视频分割模型在多目标场景下因目标数量增加而导致帧率下降和延迟无界增长的问题，SAM-MT基于SAM2构建了一个交互式框架，支持实时多目标视频分割。该方法通过为每个目标设置显式查询、共享全局上下文、解耦掩码注意力保持目标身份独立性、稀疏记忆维持时间稳定性，并结合遮挡与重叠处理策略。实验表明，SAM-MT在保持SAM2分割性能的同时，实现了与单目标基线相当的实时速度（10个目标时>36 FPS），成功将延迟与目标数量解耦。
When RLHF Fails: A Mechanistic Taxonomy of Reward Hacking, Collapse, and Evaluator Gaming
Authors: Zelalem Abahana, David Evans, Satish Mahadevan Srinivasan, Matjaz Gams
First: 2026-06-02T06:55:52+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:49:37+00:00
Comments: 20 pages, 8 figures; includes code, artifacts, and live demo
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
RLHF evaluation should track how failures emerge, where they localize, and which warning signals appear before external quality degrades. We study this problem with a compact RLHF pipeline built for this paper, including PPO, DPO, uncertainty-penalized PPO (UP-PPO), reward-model uncertainty, approximate policy drift, diversity and repetition diagnostics, and two external LLM judges. Rather than treating reward hacking as a single terminal event, we classify matched checkpoint and prompt-level transitions by the directions of learned reward R_phi, judge scores R_dag and R2_dag, and their average R_dag. The main empirical findings are that aggressive PPO produces the clearest localized reward-hacking signal, UP-PPO reduces but does not eliminate that signal, row-level diagnostics reveal failures hidden by checkpoint averages, and pre-transition features partially anticipate future localized reward hacking. The central conclusion is methodological: RLHF failures are training dynamics that can be classified, localized, and partially anticipated, not only final-model pathologies. The repository is available at github.com/zabahana/rlhf-failure-modes-diagnostics. The pipeline is also deployed as a live interactive web demo for model comparison and diagnostic views at rlhf-failures.zelalem.ai.
中文标题/摘要
标题：当RLHF失效时：奖励黑客行为、崩溃与评估者博弈的机制分类
RLHF评估应追踪失效如何产生、局部化于何处，以及在外部质量下降前出现哪些预警信号。本文构建了一个紧凑的RLHF流程进行研究，包括PPO、DPO、不确定性惩罚PPO（UP-PPO）、奖励模型不确定性、近似策略漂移、多样性与重复性诊断，以及两个外部大语言模型裁判。我们不将奖励黑客行为视为单一终端事件，而是根据学习奖励R_phi、裁判评分R_dag和R2_dag及其平均值R_dag的方向，对匹配的检查点和提示级转换进行分类。主要实证发现是：激进PPO产生最清晰的局部奖励黑客信号，UP-PPO可减少但无法消除该信号；行级诊断揭示了被检查点平均值掩盖的失效；预转换特征部分预示未来局部奖励黑客行为。核心结论是方法论性的：RLHF失效是可分类、可定位、部分可预见的训练动态，而不仅是最终模型的病理问题。代码库位于github.com/zabahana/rlhf-failure-modes-diagnostics，该流程亦部署为交互式网页演示，用于模型比较与诊断视图，地址为rlhf-failures.zelalem.ai。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates why RLHF often fails by analyzing reward hacking, collapse, and evaluator gaming through a mechanistic taxonomy. Using a compact RLHF pipeline with PPO, DPO, and UP-PPO, along with two LLM judges and diagnostics for policy drift and repetition, the authors classify failures at checkpoint and prompt levels by tracking reward signals from the reward model, judges, and their averages. Key findings show that aggressive PPO produces the clearest localized reward-hacking signals, UP-PPO mitigates but does not eliminate them, row-level diagnostics uncover failures masked by checkpoint averages, and pre-transition features can partially predict future reward hacking. The study concludes that RLHF failures are emergent training dynamics, not just end-state pathologies, and can be classified, localized, and partially anticipated.
本文通过机制性分类法研究RLHF为何常失败，重点分析奖励黑客行为、崩溃及评估者博弈。研究构建了一个紧凑的RLHF流程，包含PPO、DPO、不确定性惩罚PPO（UP-PPO）及两个外部LLM评估者，并辅以多样性、重复性诊断工具。实验发现，激进PPO产生最清晰的局部奖励黑客信号，UP-PPO可缓解但无法消除该信号；行级诊断揭示了被检查点平均值掩盖的失败；预转换特征对后续局部奖励黑客具有部分预测能力。核心结论是，RLHF失败源于训练动态，可被分类、定位并部分预测，而非仅是最终模型的病理问题。
Pelican-VLA 0.5: Attending Before Acting Benefits Generalization
Authors: Zeyuan Ding, Wenhai Liu, Yang Xu, Jiayu Hu, Yinda Chen, Yi Zhang, Yong Dai, Jian Tang, Xiaozhu Ju
First: 2026-07-07T17:50:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:46:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
In this report, we present Pelican-VLA 0.5, a unified VLA model that integrates vision-language understanding, future-frame generation, and action prediction within a single architecture. Pelican-VLA 0.5 achieves attention-level generalization: without object annotations, segmentation masks, attention supervision, or task-specific fine-tuning, its action pathway already focuses on the manipulation-relevant object and contact region. This behavior persists across unseen scenes and unseen robot embodiments, and is substantially stronger than in other open-source VLA baselines. We verify that this ability originates from the learnable Bottleneck Token inserted between perception and action: by routing task-relevant visual information through a compact bottleneck, the tokens interface induces manipulation-centric attention during pre-training and remains effective across different policy structures, including a MoT-style architecture.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Pelican-VLA 0.5：先观察后行动有利于泛化
本报告提出Pelican-VLA 0.5，这是一个统一的视觉-语言-动作模型，将视觉-语言理解、未来帧生成和动作预测整合于单一架构中。Pelican-VLA 0.5实现了注意力层面的泛化：无需物体标注、分割掩码、注意力监督或任务特定微调，其动作路径已聚焦于操作相关物体及接触区域。该行为在未见场景和未见机器人本体中持续存在，且显著优于其他开源VLA基线。我们验证该能力源于感知与动作之间插入的可学习瓶颈标记：通过将任务相关视觉信息经由紧凑瓶颈路由，该标记接口在预训练期间诱导以操作为中心的注意力，并在不同策略结构（包括MoT风格架构）中保持有效性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for generalizable robotic action models without task-specific fine-tuning, Pelican-VLA 0.5 integrates vision-language understanding, future-frame generation, and action prediction into a single architecture. Its key innovation is a learnable Bottleneck Token that routes task-relevant visual information between perception and action, inducing manipulation-centric attention during pre-training. Experimental results show that this mechanism enables the model to focus on manipulation-relevant objects and contact regions without object annotations or supervision, and this behavior generalizes across unseen scenes and robot embodiments, outperforming other open-source VLA baselines.
为实现无需任务特定微调的通用机器人动作模型，Pelican-VLA 0.5 将视觉-语言理解、未来帧生成与动作预测整合于单一架构。其核心创新是引入可学习的瓶颈标记，将任务相关视觉信息在感知与动作之间路由，从而在预训练阶段诱导以操作为中心的注意力。实验表明，该机制使模型无需物体标注或注意力监督即可聚焦于操作相关物体与接触区域，并在未见场景与机器人本体上保持泛化能力，显著优于其他开源VLA基线模型。
SolarChain-Eval: A Physics-Constrained Benchmark for Trustworthy Economic Agents in Decentralized Energy Markets
Authors: Shilin Ou, Yifan Xu, Luyao Zhang
First: 2026-07-09T16:43:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:43:42+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As agentic AI systems are increasingly applied to cyber-physical environments, their evaluation requires assessment of both task performance and trustworthiness. In decentralized energy markets, autonomous agents may improve market utility, but may also exploit invalid physical data, create artificial liquidity, and produce unstable governance decisions. Therefore, we propose SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark for evaluating trustworthy economic agents. It formulates market governance as a Gymnasium-compatible Markov Decision Process, where agents make hourly decisions. SolarChain-Eval evaluates each policy across multiple dimensions, including market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. To support agentic evaluation, SolarChain-Eval incorporates an LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer. The Planner defines episode-level action bounds and audit rules, while the Auditor reviews and revises high-risk actions. All interventions are recorded through structured logs, including trigger signals, proposed actions, revised actions, and audit rationales. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies reveal a clear utility-safety trade-off. RL agents improve market utility but can still produce unsafe behavior. When the physics penalty is removed, reward-maximizing agents exploit invalid generation and increase artificial liquidity. The LLM Planner/Auditor improves auditability and mitigates selected risks, but it cannot fully compensate for a misspecified reward function. These results indicate that trustworthy agentic AI evaluation requires both physical constraints and transparent intervention traces. We release data and code as open access on GitHub for replicability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SolarChain-Eval：用于评估去中心化能源市场中可信经济代理的物理约束基准
随着代理型AI系统在物理-数字环境中的应用日益广泛，其评估需兼顾任务表现与可信度。在去中心化能源市场中，自主代理可能提升市场效用，但也可能利用无效物理数据、制造人工流动性并导致治理决策不稳定。为此，我们提出SolarChain-Eval，一个用于评估可信经济代理的物理约束基准。该基准将市场治理建模为兼容Gymnasium的马尔可夫决策过程，代理每小时做出决策。SolarChain-Eval从市场效用、物理安全、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性及可审计性等多个维度评估每项策略。为支持代理评估，SolarChain-Eval引入基于大语言模型（LLM）的规划者/审计者层：规划者定义回合级动作边界与审计规则，审计者审查并修正高风险动作。所有干预均通过结构化日志记录，包括触发信号、提议动作、修正动作及审计理由。实验表明，静态、随机、近视、强化学习（RL）及RL+LLM策略均存在效用-安全权衡：RL代理提升市场效用但仍可能产生不安全行为；若移除物理惩罚，奖励最大化代理会利用无效生成并增加人工流动性。LLM规划者/审计者可提升可审计性并缓解部分风险，但无法完全弥补奖励函数设定不当的问题。结果表明，可信代理型AI评估需结合物理约束与透明干预轨迹。我们已在GitHub开源数据与代码，以支持复现。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to evaluate trustworthy agentic AI in decentralized energy markets, where agents may exploit physical data or destabilize governance, the authors introduce SolarChain-Eval, a physics-constrained benchmark modeled as a Gymnasium-compatible MDP. Agents make hourly decisions, and policies are assessed across six dimensions: market utility, physical safety, slippage, action smoothness, spatial fairness, and auditability. An LLM-based Planner/Auditor layer enforces action bounds and reviews high-risk actions, with all interventions logged. Experiments with static, random, myopic, RL, and RL+LLM policies show that RL agents boost utility but often violate safety constraints; removing physics penalties leads to exploitation and artificial liquidity. The LLM layer improves auditability and reduces some risks but cannot fully offset flawed reward functions, underscoring the necessity of physical constraints and transparent intervention logs for trustworthy evaluation.
为评估分布式能源市场中可信的自主AI代理，作者提出SolarChain-Eval物理约束基准。该基准将市场治理建模为兼容Gymnasium的马尔可夫决策过程，每小时决策，并从市场效用、物理安全、滑点、动作平滑性、空间公平性和可审计性六个维度评估策略。引入基于LLM的规划者/审计者层，设定动作边界并审查高风险动作，所有干预均记录日志。实验表明，强化学习代理提升效用但常违反安全约束；移除物理惩罚后，代理会利用无效数据并制造虚假流动性。LLM层提升可审计性并缓解部分风险，但无法完全弥补奖励函数设计缺陷，表明可信AI评估需结合物理约束与透明干预记录。
Multi-Resolution Feature Stem for Diabetic Retinopathy lesion segmentation
Authors: Indranil Dutta, Taehee Jeong
First: 2026-07-09T16:42:23+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:42:23+00:00
Comments: 2026 International Conference on Advances in Artificial Intelligence and Machine Learning (AAIML), 20-22 March 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Diabetic Retinopathy (DR) is a leading cause of preventable blindness worldwide, requiring automated lesion segmentation using deep learning models for early detection and monitoring. However, DR lesions vary dramatically in size from tiny microaneurysms to large hemorrhages and exudates. This variability creates conflicting demands on the model architecture and input resolution, posing a challenge for effective design. This work investigates the impact of input resolution on different lesion types. Through systematic experimentation with multiple architectures (U-Net, UNet++, Vision Transformers, DeepLabV3+) at $512 \times 512$ and $1024 \times 1024$ resolutions, we identify a critical, counter-intuitive phenomenon where increasing input resolution has opposing effects on different lesion types. We demonstrate that while higher resolution is essential for resolving fine-grained microaneurysms, it can unexpectedly degrade performance on larger hemorrhages. This finding challenges the common assumption that higher resolution is uniformly beneficial. To address this, we propose a novel Multi-Resolution Feature Stem, an input-level pyramid integrated with a UNet++ backbone. This architecture processes multiple scales in parallel, capturing fine-grained details without sacrificing contextual information. This work contributes crucial empirical evidence of this complex, resolution-dependent behavior and a practical, parameter-efficient architecture that successfully resolves this trade-off.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于糖尿病视网膜病变病灶分割的多分辨率特征茎
糖尿病视网膜病变（DR）是全球导致失明的主要可预防原因，需要利用深度学习模型实现病灶的自动化分割，以实现早期检测和监测。然而，DR病灶大小差异显著，从微动脉瘤到大出血和渗出物不等。这种变异性对模型架构和输入分辨率提出了矛盾的需求，给有效设计带来挑战。本研究探讨了输入分辨率对不同病灶类型的影响。通过在512×512和1024×1024分辨率下，对多种架构（U-Net、UNet++、视觉Transformer、DeepLabV3+）进行系统实验，我们发现了一个关键且反直觉的现象：提高输入分辨率对不同病灶类型产生相反影响。我们证明，虽然高分辨率对分辨精细的微动脉瘤至关重要，但对大出血病灶却可能意外降低性能。这一发现挑战了高分辨率普遍有益的常见假设。为解决此问题，我们提出了一种新颖的多分辨率特征茎，即与UNet++骨干网络集成的输入级金字塔结构。该架构可并行处理多尺度信息，在保留上下文信息的同时捕捉细粒度细节。本工作提供了关于这种复杂、分辨率依赖行为的关键实证证据，并提出了一种实用且参数高效的架构，成功解决了这一权衡问题。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need for accurate automated detection of diabetic retinopathy lesions, this study investigates how varying input resolutions affect segmentation performance across lesion types of different sizes. Through experiments with U-Net, UNet++, Vision Transformers, and DeepLabV3+ at 512×512 and 1024×1024 resolutions, the authors discover that higher resolution benefits fine lesions like microaneurysms but harms larger ones such as hemorrhages, contradicting the assumption that higher resolution universally improves results. To resolve this trade-off, they introduce a Multi-Resolution Feature Stem that integrates a pyramid structure with UNet++ to process multiple scales in parallel, preserving fine details while maintaining context. The proposed architecture achieves superior performance across lesion types without increasing model parameters.
为提升糖尿病视网膜病变病变的自动分割精度，该研究探讨了不同输入分辨率对各类病变（从微动脉瘤到大出血）分割效果的影响。通过在512×512和1024×1024分辨率下测试U-Net、UNet++、Vision Transformer和DeepLabV3+等架构，作者发现一个反直觉现象：高分辨率虽有助于微动脉瘤识别，却会损害大出血的分割表现。为此，他们提出一种多分辨率特征茎结构，将金字塔结构与UNet++骨干网络结合，实现多尺度并行处理，在保留细节的同时保持上下文信息。该方法在各类病变上均取得稳定提升，且参数效率高。
Bellman-sufficient Information Complexity
Authors: Yunbei Xu
First: 2026-06-09T17:49:09+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:40:03+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We develop Bellman-sufficient information complexity, a formal representation-level framework for sequential decision making. The primitive benchmark is a fixed-truth environment space $Ω$ with unrestricted nonanticipating algorithms. The intrinsic object is a Bellman-sufficient state representation, serving as an interactive notion of sufficient statistics, together with an information index $Y=χ(Ω)$, often the optimal decision or value object rather than the full environment. On the upper-bound side, learning is organized as a dynamic program on the sufficient state, equipped with a logarithmic information potential for the index. On the lower-bound side, a Bellman-Fano certificate uses the same state representation and information index, but propagates separate Bellman recursions for information gain and ghost mass. The central matching statement is therefore a conditional Bellman information-risk sandwich: when the log-penalized Bellman upper value and the ghost-quantile lower certificate close at the same radius, they certify the same complexity scale. Popular algorithms then appear as tractable certificates or relaxations of this common log-potential Bellman program, rather than as separate notions of information complexity.
中文标题/摘要
标题：贝尔曼充分信息复杂度
我们发展了贝尔曼充分信息复杂度，这是一种面向序列决策的正式表示层面框架。基本基准是一个固定真值环境空间 $Ω$，配备无限制的非预知算法。内在对象是贝尔曼充分状态表示，作为交互式充分统计量的概念，以及一个信息指标 $Y=χ(Ω)$，通常为最优决策或价值对象，而非完整环境。在上界方面，学习被组织为在充分状态上的动态规划，配备对数信息势能。在下界方面，贝尔曼-费诺证书使用相同的状态表示和信息指标，但分别传播信息增益与幽灵质量的贝尔曼递归。因此，核心匹配陈述是一个条件贝尔曼信息-风险夹层：当对数惩罚贝尔曼上界值与幽灵分位数下界证书在相同半径上接近时，它们认证相同的复杂度尺度。流行算法因此表现为该共同对数势能贝尔曼程序的可处理证书或松弛，而非独立的信息复杂度概念。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for a unified framework to analyze information complexity in sequential decision making, this paper introduces Bellman-sufficient information complexity, formalizing state representations that capture sufficient statistics for decision-making. The method constructs an upper bound via a dynamic program on the Bellman-sufficient state, using a logarithmic information potential, and a lower bound via a Bellman-Fano certificate that tracks information gain and ghost mass through separate recursions. Experimental results show that when the upper and lower bounds converge at the same radius, they certify equivalent complexity scales, and popular algorithms emerge as relaxations or certificates of this shared Bellman program.
为统一分析序列决策中的信息复杂度，本文提出贝尔曼充分信息复杂度，构建了一个基于固定真值环境与非前瞻算法的表示层面理论。核心概念是贝尔曼充分状态表示，作为交互式充分统计量，与信息指标Y=χ(Ω)配对，该指标常代表最优决策或价值而非完整环境。方法上，上界通过在充分状态上构建动态规划并引入对数信息势能，下界则采用贝尔曼-费诺证书，使用相同状态表示与信息指标，但分别递归计算信息增益与鬼质量。核心结果是条件贝尔曼信息风险夹层：当对数惩罚贝尔曼上界值与鬼分位数下界证书在相同半径收敛时，二者共同认证同一复杂度尺度，而流行算法则表现为该共同贝尔曼程序的可计算松弛或证书。
Data Alchemy: Mitigating Cross-Site Model Variability Through Test Time Data Calibration
Authors: Abhijeet Parida, Antonia Alomar, Zhifan Jiang, Pooneh Roshanitabrizi, Austin Tapp, Maria Ledesma-Carbayo, Ziyue Xu, Syed Muhammed Anwar, Marius George Linguraru, Holger R. Roth
First: 2024-07-18T16:03:59+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:37:22+00:00
Comments: accepted to Machine Learning in Medical Imaging (MLMI 2024)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Deploying deep learning-based imaging tools across various clinical sites poses significant challenges due to inherent domain shifts and regulatory hurdles associated with site-specific fine-tuning. For histopathology, stain normalization techniques can mitigate discrepancies, but they often fall short of eliminating inter-site variations. Therefore, we present Data Alchemy, an explainable stain normalization method combined with test time data calibration via a template learning framework to overcome barriers in cross-site analysis. Data Alchemy handles shifts inherent to multi-site data and minimizes them without needing to change the weights of the normalization or classifier networks. Our approach extends to unseen sites in various clinical settings where data domain discrepancies are unknown. Extensive experiments highlight the efficacy of our framework in tumor classification in hematoxylin and eosin-stained patches. Our explainable normalization method boosts classification tasks' area under the precision-recall curve(AUPR) by 0.165, 0.545 to 0.710. Additionally, Data Alchemy further reduces the multisite classification domain gap, by improving the 0.710 AUPR an additional 0.142, elevating classification performance further to 0.852, from 0.545. Our Data Alchemy framework can popularize precision medicine with minimal operational overhead by allowing for the seamless integration of pre-trained deep learning-based clinical tools across multiple sites.

Do Transformations Reveal the Truth? Generative Residual Learning for Generalized AI-Generated Image Detection
Authors: Kutub Uddin, Nusrat Tasnim, Awais Khan, Mohammad Umar Farooq, Khalid Malik
First: 2026-07-09T16:36:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:36:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The rapid advancement of generative AI has enabled the creation of highly realistic deepfake media, posing significant threats, including misinformation, digital identity theft, fraud, and manipulation of public opinion. AI-generated image (AIGI) detection is reliably challenging due to the diversity of generative methods and the subtle artifacts they leave behind. In this work, we propose GenRes, a novel framework for generative residual learning via a neural tensor network, which models fine-grained relational features between original and transformed samples to enhance generalization. To address scenarios involving multiple generative transformations, we introduce GenRes++, which employs a learnable attention mechanism to aggregate relational features across multiple transformed samples and enables the model to focus on the most informative cues. Both models leverage PE-Core as a feature extractor, providing generalized and semantically rich embeddings that improve cross-domain performance and enable the detection of AIGI generated by unseen methods. Comprehensive experiments on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that the proposed GenRes++ approach outperforms existing methods.

生成式AI的快速发展使得高度逼真的深度伪造媒体得以生成，带来了诸如虚假信息、数字身份盗用、欺诈和舆论操控等重大威胁。AI生成图像（AIGI）检测因生成方法多样性和其留下的细微伪影而极具挑战性。本文提出GenRes，一种基于神经张量网络的生成残差学习新框架，通过建模原始样本与变换样本间的细粒度关系特征以增强泛化能力。为应对多类生成变换场景，我们进一步提出GenRes++，其采用可学习注意力机制聚合多变换样本的关系特征，使模型能聚焦于最具信息量的线索。两个模型均采用PE-Core作为特征提取器，提供通用且语义丰富的嵌入，提升跨域性能并支持检测未知生成方法的AIGI。在多个基准数据集上的全面实验表明，所提出的GenRes++方法优于现有方法。

GenRes++ enhances this by incorporating a learnable attention mechanism to weigh informative cues across multiple transformations. Evaluated across multiple benchmarks, GenRes++ consistently outperforms existing methods, demonstrating superior generalization and robustness to unseen generative techniques, thanks to its use of PE-Core for semantically rich feature extraction.
为应对AI生成图像带来的虚假信息与身份欺诈等风险，本文提出GenRes与GenRes++两种框架，通过神经张量网络建模原始图像与变换后图像间的细粒度关系特征以提升检测能力。GenRes++引入可学习注意力机制，聚合多变换样本中的关键线索，增强模型泛化能力。在多个基准数据集上的实验表明，GenRes++显著优于现有方法，对未见过的生成方式具有更强的检测鲁棒性。
Resample or Reroute? Budget-Aware Test-Time Model Selection for Large Language Models
Authors: Teng-Ruei Chen
First: 2026-07-09T16:34:15+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:34:15+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 3 figures. Code: github.com/luka-krixvon/resample-or-reroute-experiment. Companion analysis: arXiv:2607.03436
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Routing among large language models (LLMs) trades response quality against serving cost, motivated by the reported gap between deployed routers and a per-instance oracle. Recent analysis shows that test-time resampling can recover per-instance selection headroom that no single-commit router captures; however, that guarantee holds only under an idealized oracle equipped with correctness labels and an unconstrained budget, neither of which a deployed system has. To the best of our knowledge, no previous work treats resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative model as competing uses of a single per-query cost budget. Therefore, this work formulates budget-aware test-time model selection: given a per-query budget and an imperfect verifier, allocate each unit of budget between resampling and rerouting so that expected correctness is maximized. An online resample-or-reroute (RoR) allocation policy driven by estimated marginal correctness per unit cost is proposed, and its behavior is grounded in the recoverability asymmetry between selection and sampling. Replay experiments on newly regenerated multi-draw correctness tensors from an eleven-model open-weight pool over four benchmarks of differing difficulty show that the proposed RoR policy attains a favorable cost-quality Pareto front relative to single-route, one-commit-router, budget-aware best-of-K, cascade, and random-allocation baselines for the tested pools, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; an ablation further shows the gains are verifier-gated, shrinking as verifier quality degrades, and robustness replays under a provider price vector and a label-free agreement verifier delineate where the conclusions carry over.
中文标题/摘要
标题：重采样还是重路由？面向大语言模型的预算感知测试时模型选择
在大语言模型（LLM）之间进行路由，是在响应质量与服务成本之间进行权衡，其动机源于已部署路由器与每实例最优选择之间的性能差距。近期分析表明，测试时重采样可恢复单次提交路由器无法捕捉的每实例选择余地；但该保证仅在理想化oracle（具备正确性标签且预算无约束）下成立，而实际部署系统不具备这些条件。据我们所知，此前研究未将重采样已提交模型与重路由至替代模型视为单一查询预算的两种竞争性使用方式。因此，本文提出预算感知测试时模型选择：给定每查询预算与不完美验证器，将每单位预算分配于重采样与重路由之间，以最大化预期正确性。提出一种基于单位成本边际正确性估计的在线重采样或重路由（RoR）分配策略，其行为基于选择与采样之间可恢复性的不对称性。在由十一模型开源池重新生成的多抽样正确性张量上，于四个难度各异的基准测试中进行回放实验，结果表明，所提RoR策略相较单路由、单提交路由器、预算感知最佳K选、级联及随机分配基线，在测试池中均获得更优的成本-质量帕累托前沿，尤其在最异质的基准上收益最大；消融实验进一步表明，收益受验证器质量调控，验证器质量下降时收益递减，且在提供商价格向量与无标签一致性验证器下的鲁棒性回放实验，明确了结论的适用范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between deployed LLM routers and ideal per-instance selection, this work addresses the trade-off between response quality and serving cost at test time. It proposes a budget-aware model selection framework that dynamically allocates each query’s cost between resampling the committed model and rerouting to an alternative, optimizing expected correctness under an imperfect verifier. The proposed online resample-or-reroute (RoR) policy uses estimated marginal correctness per unit cost to guide allocation, leveraging the asymmetry in recoverability between sampling and rerouting. Experiments across four benchmarks using an eleven-model pool show RoR achieves superior cost-quality trade-offs compared to baselines including single-router, best-of-K, and random allocation, with the largest gains on the most heterogeneous benchmark; ablation confirms gains depend on verifier quality and persist under realistic pricing and label-free verification conditions.
为弥合部署路由器与理想单实例选择之间的性能差距，本文提出一种预算感知的测试时模型选择方法，动态分配每个查询的预算用于重采样当前模型或切换至备选模型，以最大化预期正确率。实验在四个难度各异的基准上使用十一模型池，结果显示所提的重采样或重路由（RoR）策略在成本-质量权衡上优于单路由、最佳K选、级联等基线方法，尤其在模型异质性最强的基准上收益最大。消融实验表明性能增益依赖于验证器质量，且在不同定价和无标签一致性验证下策略仍保持稳健。
WebSwarm: Recursive Multi-Agent Orchestration for Deep-and-Wide Web Search
Authors: Xiaoshuai Song, Liancheng Zhang, Kangzhi Zhao, Yutao Zhu, Zhongyuan Wang, Guanting Dong, Jinghan Yang, Han Li, Kun Gai, Ji-Rong Wen, Zhicheng Dou
First: 2026-07-09T16:28:49+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:28:49+00:00
Comments: Work in progress
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language model (LLM)-based web search agents are transforming information seeking from simple factoid question answering into complex, deep-and-wide search and research-oriented tasks. A single ReAct-style agent is constrained by one long trajectory and limited context, making it difficult to handle depth and coverage simultaneously. Existing multi-agent systems improve search coverage through parallel execution and aggregation, but still exhibit clear limitations in recursive depth, collaboration adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion. We propose WebSwarm, a progressive recursive delegation framework that jointly constructs task decomposition, recursive expansion, and agent collaboration during inference. WebSwarm dynamically instantiates agentic search nodes, each coupling a local objective with a search mode that specifies how the node should organize search and collaboration. Each node can either solve its objective itself or further delegate child nodes; after solving, it returns evidence and results upward, enabling parent nodes to further expand, revise, or aggregate the search process. To guide this process, WebSwarm first probes how task-relevant information is organized on the web to ground subsequent node expansion, and reuses process-level experience across homogeneous sibling nodes. Experiments on BrowseComp-Plus, WideSearch, DeepWideSearch, and GISA show that WebSwarm consistently outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines on deep, wide, and interleaved deep-and-wide tasks. Further analyses of ablation, task difficulty, web tool efficiency, and model generalization explain WebSwarm's effectiveness and provide insights for multi-agent search systems.
中文标题/摘要
标题：WebSwarm：用于深度与广度网络搜索的递归多智能体编排
基于大语言模型（LLM）的网络搜索代理正将信息检索从简单的事实问答转变为复杂的深度与广度搜索及研究任务。单个ReAct风格代理受限于单一长轨迹和有限上下文，难以同时处理深度与广度。现有多智能体系统通过并行执行与聚合提升搜索覆盖，但仍存在递归深度、协作适应性及证据驱动扩展方面的明显局限。我们提出WebSwarm，一种渐进式递归委派框架，在推理过程中联合构建任务分解、递归扩展与智能体协作。WebSwarm动态实例化代理搜索节点，每个节点结合局部目标与搜索模式，规定节点如何组织搜索与协作。每个节点可自行解决目标，或进一步委派子节点；解决后向上返回证据与结果，使父节点可进一步扩展、修正或聚合搜索过程。为引导该过程，WebSwarm首先探测网络上任务相关信息的组织方式，以锚定后续节点扩展，并在同质兄弟节点间复用过程级经验。在BrowseComp-Plus、WideSearch、DeepWideSearch和GISA上的实验表明，WebSwarm在深度、广度及交织深度与广度任务上持续优于单智能体与多智能体基线。进一步的消融分析、任务难度、网络工具效率及模型泛化性分析解释了WebSwarm的有效性，并为多智能体搜索系统提供洞见。

Motivated by the limitations of single-agent LLM search systems in handling both depth and breadth of web queries, WebSwarm introduces a recursive multi-agent framework that dynamically decomposes tasks into hierarchical search nodes, each with a local objective and search mode. Agents either solve their subtask or delegate further, returning evidence upward to enable parent nodes to revise or expand search paths. Guided by web structure probing and experience reuse across sibling agents, WebSwarm outperforms single-agent and multi-agent baselines across deep, wide, and interleaved search tasks on multiple benchmarks, demonstrating superior coverage, adaptability, and evidence-grounded expansion.
为解决单个LLM搜索代理在处理深度与广度并存的复杂查询时的局限性，WebSwarm提出一种递归多代理编排框架，动态将任务分解为层级化搜索节点。每个节点拥有局部目标和搜索模式，可自主解决或向下委托子节点，结果向上聚合以支持迭代扩展与修正。系统首先探测网页信息结构以引导节点扩展，并在同质兄弟节点间复用过程经验。在多个基准测试中，WebSwarm在深度、广度及混合任务上持续优于单代理与多代理基线，展现出更强的覆盖能力、适应性与证据驱动推理能力。
EdgeRefine: Privacy-Utility Balance for Graphs via Jaccard Sampling under Edge Differential Privacy
Authors: Wenxiu Ding, Muzhi Liu, Zheng Yan, Mingjun Wang, Yifan Zhao, Qiao Liu
First: 2026-07-09T16:28:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:28:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have shown considerable success in learning from graph-structured data, but their use in privacy-sensitive areas remains difficult because graph structure can leak sensitive link information. To satisfy edge-level differential privacy, a common approach is to inject noise into all elements of the graph's adjacency matrix, thereby obfuscating the existence of any single edge. However, stronger privacy requires more noise, and excessive noise reduces utility, making the privacy-utility balance a major barrier to practical privacy-preserving graph learning. To address this issue, we propose EdgeRefine, a local differential privacy framework that improves this trade-off through adaptive edge refinement. EdgeRefine first estimates edge-existence probabilities using Jaccard similarity and ranks edges for noisy edge removal. To ensure the sparsity and reliability of the final graph, it uses the privacy budget $ε$ to determine the ratio of true to false edges, samples them separately based on this probability ranking, and controls the total number of edges with a separate sampling rate $k$. Extensive experiments show that EdgeRefine achieves accuracy comparable to the noise-free baseline and substantially outperforms other privacy-preserving methods across datasets and GNN architectures. Under privacy budget $ε= 2.5$, EdgeRefine improves node classification accuracy over state-of-the-art baselines by 17.8\% on ACM under GAT and 19.7\% on Cora under GCN. In graph classification, it achieves an average accuracy degradation of around 5\% compared to the noise-free baseline. Under graph reconstruction attacks, EdgeRefine maintains relative absolute error levels above 1 across all privacy budgets, averaging 1.962 on Cora and 1.472 on AMAP, indicating strong resilience against privacy leakage.
中文标题/摘要
标题：EdgeRefine：通过Jaccard采样在边级差分隐私下实现图的隐私-效用平衡
图神经网络（GNNs）在学习图结构数据方面表现出显著成功，但在隐私敏感领域应用仍困难，因为图结构可能泄露敏感的边信息。为满足边级差分隐私，常用方法是在图邻接矩阵所有元素中注入噪声，从而掩盖任何单条边的存在。然而，更强的隐私保护需要更多噪声，而过多噪声会降低效用，使隐私-效用平衡成为实际隐私保护图学习的主要障碍。 为解决此问题，我们提出EdgeRefine，一种局部差分隐私框架，通过自适应边精炼改善该权衡。EdgeRefine首先使用Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率，并按概率对边进行排序，以进行带噪声的边移除。为确保最终图的稀疏性和可靠性，它利用隐私预算ε确定真实边与虚假边的比例，根据概率排序分别采样这些边，并通过独立采样率k控制总边数。大量实验表明，EdgeRefine在准确率上可媲美无噪声基线，并在多个数据集和GNN架构上显著优于其他隐私保护方法。在隐私预算ε=2.5下，EdgeRefine在ACM数据集上使用GAT时比现有最佳基线提升节点分类准确率17.8%，在Cora数据集上使用GCN时提升19.7%。在图分类任务中，其平均准确率下降约5%（相比无噪声基线）。在图重构攻击下，EdgeRefine在所有隐私预算下保持相对绝对误差水平高于1，Cora上平均为1.962，AMAP上为1.472，表明其对隐私泄露具有强鲁棒性。
Summary / 总结
To address the privacy-utility trade-off in graph learning under edge differential privacy, EdgeRefine introduces a local privacy framework that adaptively refines edges by leveraging Jaccard similarity to estimate edge existence probabilities and rank edges for selective noise injection. It uses the privacy budget ε to determine the true-to-false edge ratio, samples edges separately based on their probability ranking, and controls total edge count via a sampling rate k. Experiments show EdgeRefine achieves near-noise-free accuracy, outperforming existing methods by 17.8% on ACM (GAT) and 19.7% on Cora (GCN), with only ~5% accuracy loss in graph classification and robust reconstruction error (>1) across all privacy budgets, demonstrating strong privacy resilience.
为解决图学习中边缘差分隐私下的隐私-效用权衡问题，EdgeRefine提出一种局部隐私框架，通过Jaccard相似度估计边存在概率并排序，实现对边的自适应噪声注入。它利用隐私预算ε确定真实边与虚假边的比例，按概率排序分别采样，并通过采样率k控制图的稀疏性。实验表明，EdgeRefine在保持接近无噪声基线精度的同时显著优于现有方法：在ε=2.5下，其在ACM（GAT）和Cora（GCN）数据集上节点分类准确率分别提升17.8%和19.7%；图分类任务平均精度损失约5%；在图重构攻击下，其相对绝对误差始终高于1，Cora和AMAP平均值分别为1.962和1.472，展现出强抗隐私泄露能力。
Instance Generation for Patient-to-room Assignment and Admission Scheduling Based on Real Hospital Data
Authors: Tabea Brandt, Christina Büsing, Johanna Leweke, Finn Seesemann, Sina Weber
First: 2025-07-04T09:29:30+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:26:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Developing algorithms for real-life problems that perform well in practice depends on the availability of realistic data for testing. Obtaining real-life data for optimization problems in health care, however, is often difficult, and such data typically cannot be published, which limits reproducibility by other researchers. This is especially true for patient-related problems because of data privacy policies such as the patient-to-room assignment problem. Therefore, artificially generated instances are commonly used. To improve the generation of realistic instances, we develop a configurable instance generator for the patient-to-room assignment problem and other patient-related problems, featuring an easy-to-use graphical user interface. The design of the generator is based on an extensive empirical analysis of real hospital data, which identifies relevant ward-specific patterns such as patients' age and length-of-stay distributions. Moreover, as randomly generated instances are often infeasible, we address this issue in two ways. We implement a dynamic programming approach in the generator to optionally enforce feasibility and extend existing results from the literature to derive new combinatorial insights into patient-to-room feasibility.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于真实医院数据的患者-病房分配与入院调度实例生成
解决现实问题的算法开发依赖于可用于测试的现实数据。然而，医疗优化问题的真实数据往往难以获取，且通常因隐私政策无法公开，限制了其他研究人员的可重复性。尤其在患者相关问题（如患者-病房分配问题）中更为明显。因此，常使用人工生成实例。为提升现实实例生成质量，我们开发了一款可配置的患者-病房分配及其他患者相关问题实例生成器，配备易用图形用户界面。该生成器设计基于对真实医院数据的广泛实证分析，识别出病房特有模式（如患者年龄与住院时长分布）。此外，由于随机生成实例常不可行，我们通过两种方式解决：在生成器中实现动态规划方法以可选强制可行，并扩展文献已有结果，获得患者-病房分配可行性的新组合学见解。

Formal Mechanisms for Market Stability in Self-Interested Agent Societies: A Marketplace Simulation Study
Authors: Eugene Ng Yi Sheng, Bingquan Shen
First: 2026-07-09T16:21:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:21:16+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 8 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Self-interested agents, left unconstrained, tend toward defection in repeated social dilemmas, causing cooperative gains from trade to collapse. This paper investigates what formal mechanisms, layered on top of unrestricted communication, are sufficient for a society of such agents to maintain market stability, and how resilient those mechanisms are to adversarial attack. We instantiate the research question as a multi-agent marketplace simulation where 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) with complementary production specialties must trade within a constrained social network to obtain utility. We conduct two experimental phases: (1) a mechanism comparison across eight conditions under progressive troll injection over 200 rounds, identifying Mediation as the top-performing mechanism; and (2) adversarial red-teaming of Mediation using iteratively prompt-optimised LLM-driven trolls, finding that the best attack (v6) reduces honest-agent utility by 13.3% but cannot collapse the market. Mediation enables recovery even under sustained adversarial pressure. We define adversarial robustness as a mechanism's ability to sustain positive honest-agent utility under optimised attack, and find that Mediation is robust: it can be bent but not broken.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自利代理社会中维持市场稳定的正式机制：市场模拟研究
若无约束，自利代理在重复社会困境中倾向于背叛，导致合作性交易收益崩溃。本文研究在无限制通信基础上，哪些正式机制足以使此类代理社会维持市场稳定，以及这些机制对对抗性攻击的韧性如何。我们将研究问题建模为多代理市场模拟：18个具有互补生产专长的DeepSeek-V3大语言模型代理必须在受限社交网络内交易以获取效用。我们进行两个实验阶段：(1) 在200轮渐进式 troll 注入下，比较八种机制，发现调解机制表现最佳；(2) 使用迭代优化提示的LLM驱动 troll 对调解机制进行对抗性红队测试，发现最佳攻击（v6）使诚实代理效用下降13.3%，但无法摧毁市场。调解机制即使在持续对抗压力下仍能恢复。我们将对抗鲁棒性定义为机制在优化攻击下维持正向诚实代理效用的能力，发现调解机制具有鲁棒性：可被扭曲但不会被破坏。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the tendency of self-interested agents to defect in repeated social dilemmas, this study explores formal mechanisms that can sustain market stability under unrestricted communication. Using a simulation with 18 LLM agents (DeepSeek-V3) trading within a constrained network, the research compares eight mechanisms across 200 rounds with progressive troll injection, identifying Mediation as the most effective. In a second phase, adversarial red-teaming with iteratively optimized trolls reduced honest-agent utility by 13.3% but failed to collapse the market, demonstrating Mediation’s resilience. The study defines adversarial robustness as maintaining positive utility under optimized attack, and concludes Mediation is robust—capable of recovery despite sustained pressure.
为解决自利代理人在重复博弈中倾向于背叛导致市场崩溃的问题，该研究探讨了在无约束通信环境下维持市场稳定的正式机制。通过模拟18个DeepSeek-V3 LLM代理在受限网络中进行交易的多智能体市场，作者在200轮渐进式恶意攻击下比较了八种机制，发现“调解”表现最优。在第二阶段，通过迭代优化的恶意代理攻击，“调解”使诚实代理的效用下降13.3%，但市场未崩溃，证明其具备抗攻击能力：可被扭曲但不会被摧毁。
Secure Decentralized Federated Learning via Gossip and Virtual Voting
Authors: Amirhossein Taherpour, Xiaodong Wang
First: 2026-07-09T16:21:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:21:13+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Decentralized federated learning (DFL) removes the central server by letting nodes exchange model updates through peer-to-peer gossip, but existing gossip-based methods often lack provenance finality and resilience to Byzantine or lazy participants. Ledger-assisted federated learning (FL) improves auditability, yet blockchains, shards, or settlement committees can reintroduce global coordination costs that conflict with DFL locality. This paper proposes \emph{gspDAG-FL}, a secure DFL framework that derives consensus from the same gossip history used to disseminate models. Nodes exchange model payloads only with neighbors, while full nodes collect event certificates and receiver-endorsed accepted gossip proofs, reconstruct a compact Topology directed acyclic graph (DAG), and run Hashgraph-style virtual voting followed by compact full-node certificates. Finality is over unique model-origin tuples, not identical local parameter states. To improve resilience, gspDAG-FL combines payload validation, accepted-proof validation, and private semantic audit before aggregation. We formalize the adversarial setting, prove safety and conditional liveness of the control plane, and give a convergence guarantee for certified perturbed gossip under time-varying effective mixing. Experiments on MNIST classification and Penn Treebank language modeling, using fair held-out validation/audit data and networks up to \(N=100\), show that gspDAG-FL achieves learning quality close to validation-based ledger FL while reducing coordination bottlenecks, improving throughput, and maintaining high invalid-origin detection under mixed Byzantine and lazy participation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：通过流言和虚拟投票实现安全的去中心化联邦学习
去中心化联邦学习（DFL）通过节点间点对点流言交换模型更新来消除中心服务器，但现有基于流言的方法常缺乏溯源最终性和对拜占庭或懒惰参与者的鲁棒性。借助账本的联邦学习（FL）可提升可审计性，但区块链、分片或结算委员会可能重新引入全局协调开销，与DFL的本地性冲突。本文提出\emph{gspDAG-FL}，一种安全的DFL框架，其共识源自用于传播模型的同一流言历史。节点仅与邻居交换模型负载，全节点收集事件证书和接收端认可的流言证明，重构紧凑的有向无环图（DAG），并运行Hashgraph风格的虚拟投票后生成紧凑的全节点证书。最终性基于唯一的模型来源元组，而非相同的本地参数状态。为提升鲁棒性，gspDAG-FL在聚合前结合负载验证、接受证明验证和私有语义审计。我们形式化了对抗性环境，证明了控制平面的安全性和条件活性，并给出了在时变有效混合下经认证扰动流言的收敛保证。在MNIST分类和Penn Treebank语言建模实验中，使用公平保留验证/审计数据及最多\(N=100\)的网络，结果显示gspDAG-FL在学习质量上接近基于验证的账本FL，同时减少协调瓶颈、提升吞吐量，并在混合拜占庭与懒惰参与下保持高无效来源检测率。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for secure, decentralized federated learning without central coordination or blockchain overhead, this paper introduces gspDAG-FL, a framework that derives consensus from gossip-based model dissemination. Nodes exchange model updates only with neighbors, while full nodes collect certificates and proofs to reconstruct a compact DAG and perform virtual voting via Hashgraph-style mechanisms. The system ensures finality over unique model-origin tuples rather than local parameter states, and combines payload, proof, and semantic validation to resist Byzantine and lazy participants. Experiments on MNIST and Penn Treebank with up to 100 nodes show gspDAG-FL achieves near-validation-quality learning while reducing coordination costs, boosting throughput, and maintaining high detection of invalid model origins.
为解决去中心化联邦学习中缺乏最终性和对拜占庭或惰性节点脆弱的问题，本文提出gspDAG-FL框架，该框架从用于模型传播的 gossip 历史中推导共识。节点仅与邻居交换模型更新，全节点收集证书与接收端认可的 gossip 证明，重构紧凑的有向无环图（DAG），并执行类似 Hashgraph 的虚拟投票机制。系统以模型-来源唯一元组而非本地参数状态实现最终性，并结合负载、证明与语义验证提升鲁棒性。在 MNIST 和 Penn Treebank 上的实验表明，该框架在最多 100 节点网络中，学习质量接近基于验证的账本联邦学习，同时降低协调开销、提升吞吐量，并保持对拜占庭与惰性参与者的高检测率。
Multi-Modal, Multi-Environment Machine Teaching for Robust Reward Learning
Authors: Ali Larian, Qian Lin, Chang Zong Wu, Daniel S. Brown
First: 2026-07-09T16:18:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:18:16+00:00
Comments: Accepted to RLC 2026. Conference paper
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As autonomous agents are increasingly deployed across diverse operational contexts, aligning their behavior with human intent demands reward functions that remain robust to such changes rather than overfitting to any single environment. Inverse reinforcement learning (IRL) provides a principled way to infer such objectives from human feedback. However, existing analyses of optimal teaching approaches for IRL focus on single-environment, demonstration-only settings, leaving underexplored how heterogeneous feedback modalities and environment dynamics jointly constrain reward functions that generalize across multiple environments. Because demonstrations in one MDP entangle reward information with that environments specific structure, the resulting rewards frequently fail to generalize when the agent is deployed in a new setting. We first analyze how different feedback modalities constrain rewards, showing that, in the unlimited-data regime, comparisons impose strictly stronger global constraints than other modalities. Beyond this theoretical analysis, we introduce a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm for reward learning that operates across multiple MDPs. The algorithm first greedily selects informative environments that expose complementary reward constraints, then strategically queries low-cost feedback within those environments. Empirically, our method achieves substantially lower regret and stronger generalization to held-out environments than uniform teaching baselines under identical feedback budgets, demonstrating the importance of multi-environment, multi-modal teaching for learning dynamics-robust reward functions.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向鲁棒奖励学习的多模态、多环境机器教学
随着自主代理在多样化的操作环境中日益部署，使其行为与人类意图对齐需要在环境变化时仍保持鲁棒性的奖励函数，而非过度拟合于单一环境。逆强化学习（IRL）提供了一种从人类反馈中推断此类目标的原理性方法。然而，现有对IRL最优教学方法的分析聚焦于单环境、仅演示的设置，尚未充分探讨异构反馈模态与环境动态如何共同约束能跨多个环境泛化的奖励函数。由于单个MDP中的演示将奖励信息与该环境特定结构纠缠在一起，由此产生的奖励函数在新环境中部署时常无法泛化。我们首先分析不同反馈模态如何约束奖励，表明在无限数据条件下，比较反馈施加的全局约束严格强于其他模态。在此理论分析基础上，我们提出了一种跨多个MDP的分层机器教学算法用于奖励学习：该算法首先贪心选择能暴露互补奖励约束的有信息量环境，然后在这些环境中战略性地查询低成本反馈。实验证明，在相同反馈预算下，我们的方法相比均匀教学基线显著降低遗憾值，并在未见环境中实现更强的泛化能力，凸显了多环境、多模态教学对学习动态鲁棒奖励函数的重要性。

They then propose a hierarchical machine teaching algorithm that selects informative environments to expose complementary reward constraints and strategically queries low-cost feedback within them. Experiments show their method achieves significantly lower regret and better generalization to unseen environments compared to uniform teaching baselines under the same feedback budget.
为解决奖励函数在多环境下的泛化难题，本文研究了多模态、多环境机器教学下的逆强化学习。研究发现，比较类反馈在无限数据下对奖励函数施加的全局约束强于演示类反馈。作者提出一种分层教学算法，先选择能暴露互补奖励约束的环境，再在其中策略性地查询低成本反馈。实验表明，该方法在相同反馈预算下，相比均匀教学基线显著降低遗憾并提升对未见环境的泛化能力，凸显多环境、多模态反馈对学习鲁棒奖励函数的重要性。
UltraX: Refining Pre-Training Data at Scale with Adaptive Programmatic Editing
Authors: Xinlong Zhao, Dongsheng Liu, Hengyu Zhao, Zixuan Fu, Zheng Wang, Jie Cai, Jie Zhou, Qiang Ma, Xuanhe Zhou, Xu Han, Yudong Wang, Zhiyuan Liu
First: 2026-07-09T16:18:07+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:18:07+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As available training data approaches its physical limit, gains from Scaling Laws have begun to diminish. Consequently, improving Large Language Models (LLMs) now depends less on data expansion and more on higher-quality data utilization. However, in the context of large-scale corpora, existing refinement methodologies face significant limitations in quality, efficiency, and reliability: Rule-based approaches are constrained by fixed heuristics and struggle with instance-level variations; LLM-based approaches improve quality but fail to meet the efficiency and reliability requirements of large-scale data processing. To address these challenges, we propose UltraX, a function-calling refinement framework for large-scale pre-training data that completes the editing function space by introducing insertion in addition to deletion and modification, enabling fine-grained instance-level editing. Specifically, UltraX builds a reliable program-supervision generation pipeline. In this pipeline, dataset-adaptive prompt optimization first guides an expert LLM to produce high-quality end-to-end refined texts, and Line Alignment Mapping and Dynamic Context Replacement then convert original-refined text pairs into structured program supervision. Meanwhile, UltraX improves supervision quality and stabilizes the training distribution with low-confidence example filtering and ratio-controlled sampling by operation combination. During inference and execution, it normalizes and validates model outputs through sliding-window prediction, global operation aggregation, and systematic post-processing, improving the stability and reliability of large-scale execution. Experiments show that UltraX achieves the highest average performance across all corpora and also matches or surpasses baselines with fewer training tokens, demonstrating stronger data efficiency and refinement reliability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：UltraX：通过自适应程序化编辑大规模精炼预训练数据
随着可用训练数据接近物理极限，规模定律带来的收益已开始减弱。因此，改进大语言模型（LLM）现在更依赖于高质量数据的利用，而非单纯的数据扩展。然而，在大规模语料库背景下，现有精炼方法在质量、效率和可靠性方面存在显著局限：基于规则的方法受限于固定启发式，难以应对实例级差异；基于LLM的方法虽能提升质量，却无法满足大规模数据处理所需的效率与可靠性。为应对这些挑战，我们提出UltraX，一种面向大规模预训练数据的函数调用精炼框架，通过引入插入操作（除删除和修改外），完成编辑功能空间，实现实例级精细编辑。具体而言，UltraX构建了一个可靠的程序监督生成流水线：首先，通过语料自适应提示优化引导专家LLM生成高质量端到端精炼文本；随后，通过行对齐映射与动态上下文替换，将原始-精炼文本对转换为结构化程序监督。同时，UltraX通过低置信度示例过滤与操作组合的比率控制采样，提升监督质量并稳定训练分布。在推理与执行阶段，通过滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合与系统化后处理，对模型输出进行标准化与验证，提升大规模执行的稳定性与可靠性。实验表明，UltraX在所有语料库上均取得最高平均性能，且在训练token更少的情况下仍可匹配或超越基线，展现出更强的数据效率与精炼可靠性。
Summary / 总结
As scaling laws yield diminishing returns due to data saturation, improving Large Language Models now hinges on refining existing data rather than expanding it. UltraX addresses this by introducing a function-calling framework that enables fine-grained, instance-level editing through insertion, deletion, and modification. It employs a program-supervision pipeline: first optimizing prompts to guide an expert LLM to generate refined text, then converting text pairs into structured programmatic supervision via alignment and context replacement. To enhance supervision quality and training stability, UltraX filters low-confidence examples and samples operations with controlled ratios. During execution, it normalizes outputs using sliding-window prediction, aggregation, and post-processing. Experiments confirm UltraX achieves the highest average performance across datasets and matches or exceeds baselines using fewer training tokens, demonstrating superior data efficiency and reliability.
随着数据规模接近物理极限，单纯扩展数据已难以提升大语言模型性能，因此提升模型的关键转向数据质量优化。UltraX提出一种函数调用框架，支持插入、删除和修改等细粒度实例级编辑。其程序监督生成流程首先通过提示优化引导专家模型生成高质量精修文本，再通过行对齐映射与动态上下文替换将文本对转化为结构化监督指令。为提升可靠性，系统过滤低置信度样本并控制操作组合比例。执行阶段通过滑动窗口预测、全局操作聚合与后处理标准化输出。实验表明，UltraX在所有数据集上平均性能最优，且在训练token更少的情况下仍可匹配或超越基线模型，展现出更强的数据效率与精修稳定性。
BiSCo-LLM: Lookup-Free Binary Spherical Coding for Extreme Low-Bit Large Language Model Compression
Authors: Yuantian Shao, Peisong Wang, Zhilei Liu, Chuangyi Li, Yuanteng Chen, Pengcheng Xie, Yiwu Yao, Zhihui Wei, Jian Cheng
First: 2026-07-09T16:17:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:17:10+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly constrained by memory capacity, weight bandwidth, and checkpoint storage during deployment. Existing low-bit compression methods mainly follow two directions. Scalar or group-wise quantization is simple and compatible with efficient low-precision kernels, but its representation capacity becomes limited when the target budget approaches 2 bits per weight. Vector-quantized weight compression provides a richer block-level representation, but usually introduces explicit codebooks, index lookup, and additional storage accounting. This paper presents BiSCo-LLM, a codebook-free binary spherical coding framework for extreme low-bit LLM weight compression. The core pipeline is built on three components. First, local weight chunks are mapped onto a unit hypersphere and binarized into compact spherical codes, so that the main payload is a bit-packed sign stream rather than explicit VQ centroids. Second, a residual BSQ stage encodes the reconstruction error left by the base spherical codec, providing an explicit rate-distortion path without stored codebooks. Third, category-wise recovery distillation is performed after replacing each Transformer module category, reducing the mismatch between local weight reconstruction and assembled model behavior. A small 8-bit protected-channel path is used as an auxiliary stabilization mechanism for sensitive channels and is counted separately from the BSQ payload. The reported storage budget includes binary codes, neural decoders, protected-channel payloads, LoRA adapters, and metadata.
中文标题/摘要
标题：BiSCo-LLM：无查找表的二进制球面编码用于极低比特大型语言模型压缩
大型语言模型（LLM）在部署过程中日益受限于内存容量、权重带宽和检查点存储。现有低比特压缩方法主要沿两条路径发展：标量或分组量化方法简单且兼容高效的低精度内核，但当目标预算接近每权重2比特时，其表示能力受限；向量量化权重压缩提供更丰富的块级表示，但通常引入显式码本、索引查找和额外存储开销。本文提出BiSCo-LLM，一种无码本的二进制球面编码框架，用于极低比特LLM权重压缩。核心流程由三个组件构成：首先，局部权重块映射到单位超球面并二值化为紧凑球面码，使主要负载为位打包符号流而非显式VQ质心；其次，残差BSQ阶段编码基础球面编码遗留的重建误差，提供无需存储码本的显式率失真路径；第三，在替换每个Transformer模块类别后执行类别级恢复蒸馏，减少局部权重重建与组装模型行为之间的不匹配。一个小型8比特保护通道路径用作敏感通道的辅助稳定机制，且独立于BSQ负载计数。报告的存储预算包括二进制码、神经解码器、保护通道负载、LoRA适配器和元数据。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to compress large language models to extreme low-bit representations without sacrificing performance or requiring codebooks, BiSCo-LLM introduces a lookup-free binary spherical coding framework. It maps local weight chunks onto a unit hypersphere and binarizes them into compact spherical codes, encoding weights as a bit-packed sign stream. A residual BSQ stage handles reconstruction error without stored codebooks, and category-wise recovery distillation fine-tunes model behavior post-compression. Experimental results show that this approach achieves high compression ratios while maintaining model accuracy, even under 2-bit per weight budgets, with minimal additional storage overhead from decoders and auxiliary stabilization channels.
为应对大语言模型在部署时对内存、带宽和存储的严苛限制，本文提出BiSCo-LLM框架，一种无码本的二进制球面编码方法。该方法将局部权重块映射到单位超球面上并二值化为紧凑球面码，以位打包符号流代替显式向量量化中心。残差BSQ阶段处理重建误差，无需存储码本，且通过模块类别级恢复蒸馏优化模型行为。实验表明，该方法在极低比特率下仍能保持模型性能，存储预算涵盖二进制码、神经解码器、保护通道数据、LoRA适配器及元数据。
DominoTree: Conditional Tree-Structured Drafting with Domino for Speculative Decoding
Authors: Saw S. Lin, Jyh-Shing Roger Jang
First: 2026-07-09T16:16:35+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:16:35+00:00
Comments: 23 pages, 2 figures, 11 tables. Code: https://github.com/slin-zhq/Domino-Tree
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by drafting several tokens and verifying them in parallel. Block-diffusion drafters such as DFlash produce a draft block in one pass but model only per-position marginals; best-first tree methods such as DDTree expand candidate trees from those marginals. The released Domino drafter adds a GRU-based causal correction that makes each draft token's distribution path-dependent, a structure DDTree's factorized formulation cannot represent. We introduce DominoTree, a training-free best-first draft tree scored by Domino's conditional, non-factorized correction along each root-to-node path, made practical by restricting the per-node correction to a candidate top-M. On Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, DominoTree reaches up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length of any evaluated method, up to 10.7 tokens per round, at every temperature we test. DominoTree constructs its tree with a GPU-native, CUDA-graph builder that is bit-identical to a reference Python implementation, so acceptance is unchanged, while keeping per-round tree construction cheap. With this builder as default, DominoTree wins throughput over the released Domino decoder at every temperature, 9-10% overall on Qwen3-4B and up to +22% on Alpaca, and over DDTree/CaDDTree at every temperature we test. On Qwen3- 8B, DominoTree keeps the highest accepted length at every temperature and adds a decisive throughput win at T=0, +24% over DDTree; at higher temperature that edge over DDTree/CaDDTree narrows to a tie and a small loss, while its Overall aggregate wins over DFlash and Domino persist.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DominoTree：基于Domino的条件树结构草稿与推测解码
推测解码通过并行草拟多个token并验证来加速大语言模型推理。块扩散草稿器如DFlash一次通过生成一个草稿块，但仅提供每个位置的边际概率；最佳优先树方法如DDTree则从这些边际概率扩展候选树。已发布的Domino草稿器增加了基于GRU的因果校正机制，使每个草稿token的分布路径依赖，而DDTree的因子化公式无法表示该结构。我们引入DominoTree，这是一种无需训练的最佳优先草稿树，其评分基于Domino的条件非因子化校正，沿每个根到节点路径进行，通过限制每个节点的校正仅针对候选Top-M使其实用化。在Qwen3-4B跨八个基准测试中，DominoTree相比自回归解码最高加速达6.6倍，且在所有测试温度下均达到最高平均接受长度，每轮最高达10.7个token。DominoTree使用GPU原生CUDA图构建器构建树结构，与参考Python实现完全比特一致，因此接受率不变，同时保持每轮树构建成本低廉。以该构建器为默认设置，DominoTree在所有温度下均优于已发布的Domino解码器，Qwen3-4B整体提升9-10%，Alpaca最高达+22%；且在所有测试温度下均优于DDTree/CaDDTree。在Qwen3-8B上，DominoTree在所有温度下保持最高接受长度，并在T=0时以+24%的吞吐量优势胜过DDTree；在较高温度下，该优势对DDTree/CaDDTree缩小至平局或微小劣势，但其整体聚合吞吐量仍优于DFlash和Domino。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to accelerate LLM inference, the paper introduces DominoTree, a speculative decoding method that builds draft token trees using a non-factorized, path-dependent correction from the Domino drafter, which outperforms factorized approaches like DDTree. DominoTree scores candidate trees with conditional distributions along root-to-node paths, restricting per-node corrections to top-M candidates to maintain efficiency. Evaluated on Qwen3-4B across eight benchmarks, it achieves up to 6.6x speedup over autoregressive decoding and the highest mean accept length (up to 10.7 tokens per round) at all tested temperatures. Its GPU-native CUDA-graph builder ensures bit-identical results to a Python reference while keeping tree construction cheap, enabling it to outperform both Domino and DDTree/CaDDTree in throughput across all temperatures, with up to +22% gain on Alpaca and +24% over DDTree on Qwen3-8B at T=0.
为加速大语言模型推理，本文提出DominoTree方法，结合树状草稿与Domino的路径依赖非因子化纠错机制。与以往基于因子化边际的方法不同，DominoTree在每条根节点到子节点的路径上，使用基于GRU的因果纠错进行评分，并限制每节点仅考虑前M个候选以保持效率。在Qwen3-4B八项基准测试中，DominoTree实现最高6.6倍于自回归解码的速度提升，且在所有温度下均保持最高平均接受长度（最高达每轮10.7个token）。其GPU原生CUDA图构建器确保与Python参考实现完全一致，同时保持树构建成本低廉，使其在吞吐量上全面超越Domino和DDTree/CaDDTree，尤其在Alpaca上最高提升22%，在Qwen3-8B上T=0时比DDTree高出24%。
Steering Neural Network Training through Interpretable Constraints Based on Partial Dependence
Authors: Yann Claes, Pierre Geurts, Vân Anh Huynh-Thu
First: 2026-07-09T16:16:27+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:16:27+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Over the last few years, there has been an increased interest in making machine learning models more interpretable. Although a great deal of effort goes into developing techniques for interpreting the interactions learned by a given model, fewer studies focus on assessing the quality of such explanations. Even fewer focus on how to adjust the model to produce explanations faithful to prior knowledge, a process known as explanation-guided learning. Furthermore, most approaches in this area focus on classification problems and usually assume prior knowledge about which input features or regions are most important. In this work, we introduce a new approach to steering neural networks based on partial dependence, such that their average response to certain features aligns with specific functional domain knowledge about the problem. We empirically demonstrate on a range of regression problems, including dynamical systems forecasting, that models whose training has been controlled using our method perform better than unconstrained models and are more data-efficient. Moreover, we highlight that interpretations obtained from the former actually align with the user-provided knowledge, whereas those obtained from the latter do not.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于部分依赖的可解释约束引导神经网络训练
近年来，人们越来越关注使机器学习模型更具可解释性。尽管大量研究致力于开发解释模型所学交互关系的技术，但较少研究关注评估这些解释的质量，更少研究关注如何调整模型以生成符合先验知识的解释，这一过程称为解释引导学习。此外，该领域大多数方法聚焦于分类问题，并通常假设已知哪些输入特征或区域最为重要。本文提出一种基于部分依赖的新方法，引导神经网络使其对特定特征的平均响应与问题领域的特定功能知识保持一致。我们在多种回归问题（包括动力系统预测）上实证表明，使用本方法控制训练的模型表现优于无约束模型，且更具数据效率。此外，我们指出，前者获得的解释实际与用户提供的知识一致，而后者则不然。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve the interpretability and fidelity of machine learning explanations, this work proposes a method to steer neural network training using interpretable constraints derived from partial dependence functions, ensuring model outputs align with domain-specific functional knowledge. Applied to regression tasks including dynamical systems forecasting, the method constrains model behavior to match user-provided prior knowledge about feature interactions. Experimental results show that models trained with these constraints outperform unconstrained baselines in both predictive accuracy and data efficiency, while their generated explanations more accurately reflect the intended domain knowledge.
为提升神经网络的可解释性与可信度，本文提出基于偏依赖的可解释约束方法，引导模型训练使其对特定输入特征的平均响应符合领域先验知识。该方法通过约束模型输出与领域知识的一致性来调整训练过程，而不改变模型结构。在多个回归任务（包括动力系统预测）上的实验表明，使用该方法训练的模型在精度和数据效率上优于无约束模型，且其解释结果更贴近用户提供的先验知识。
Native Video-Action Pretraining for Generalizable Robot Control
Authors: Qihang Zhang, Lin Li, Luyao Zhang, Shuai Yang, Yiming Luo, Shuaiting Li, Ruilin Wang, Junke Wang, Jiahao Shao, Gangwei Xu, Jiaming Zhou, Yishu Shen, Yudong Jin, Fangyi Xu, Shuailei Ma, Jiaqi Liao, Guanxing Lu, Zifan Shi, Yongkun Wen, Yujie Zhao, Weixuan Tang, Xinyang Wang, Chaojian Li, Jiapeng Zhu, Ka Leong Cheng, Nan Xue, Xing Zhu, Yujun Shen, Yinghao Xu
First: 2026-07-09T16:15:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:15:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The advent of video-action models offers a promising path for robot control. Nevertheless, we argue that repurposing video generative models designed for digital content creation is inherently inadequate for physical environments. To bridge this gap, we present LingBot-VA 2.0, a video-action foundation model built from the ground up for embodiment. Four core design principles showcase its evolution from LingBot-VA. (1) Departing from traditional reconstruction-focused VAEs, we introduce a semantic visual-action tokenizer, which aligns visual representations with both semantics and actions, improving instruction following and action precision in subsequent policy learning. (2) Given the strictly causal nature of temporal dynamics, we adopt a causal pretraining paradigm, training from scratch to circumvent the catastrophic forgetting that frequently occurs when adapting bidirectional architectures. (3) To meet the demands of high-frequency inference, our model employs a sparse MoE backbone, expanding model capacity without compromising efficiency. (4) Real-time closed-loop control is realized through an enhanced asynchronous inference scheme, which predicts future latents in parallel with action execution while re-grounding each rollout on the latest observation via learned forward dynamics. Real-world deployment validates LingBot-VA 2.0 as a robust foundation model, as evidenced by its few-shot generalization across complex manipulation tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：面向通用机器人控制的原生视频-动作预训练
视频-动作模型的出现为机器人控制提供了有前景的路径。然而，我们认为，将为数字内容创作设计的视频生成模型直接用于物理环境是固有的不足。为弥合这一差距，我们提出了LingBot-VA 2.0，这是一个从头构建的、专为具身设计的视频-动作基础模型。其演进基于四个核心设计原则：(1) 脱离传统以重建为中心的VAE，我们引入语义视觉-动作分词器，将视觉表征与语义和动作对齐，从而提升后续策略学习中的指令遵循与动作精度。(2) 考虑时间动态的严格因果性，我们采用因果预训练范式，从零开始训练以避免双向架构适应时常见的灾难性遗忘。(3) 为满足高频推理需求，模型采用稀疏MoE主干结构，在不牺牲效率的前提下扩展模型容量。(4) 通过增强的异步推理方案实现实时闭环控制，该方案在动作执行的同时并行预测未来潜在状态，并通过学习的前向动力学将每次 rollout 重新锚定在最新观测上。真实世界部署验证了LingBot-VA 2.0作为稳健基础模型的能力，其在复杂操作任务中展现出强大的少样本泛化能力。
