Motivated by the need for robot control models that generalize across physical environments, this work introduces LingBot-VA 2.0, a video-action foundation model designed from scratch for embodied agents. It employs a semantic visual-action tokenizer to align visual and action representations, a causal pretraining approach to avoid catastrophic forgetting, a sparse MoE architecture for high-frequency inference, and an asynchronous inference scheme for real-time closed-loop control. Experiments demonstrate its robustness and few-shot generalization across complex manipulation tasks in real-world deployment.
为解决现有视频生成模型难以适配物理环境的问题，本文提出专为具身智能设计的视频-动作基础模型LingBot-VA 2.0。该模型采用语义视觉-动作分词器对视觉与动作进行联合表征，采用因果预训练避免灾难性遗忘，使用稀疏MoE架构实现高效高频推理，并通过异步推理机制支持实时闭环控制。实验证明其在真实环境中具备良好的泛化能力，可在少量样本下完成复杂操作任务。
On the Gradient Complexity of Private Optimization with Private Oracles
Authors: Michael Menart, Aleksandar Nikolov
First: 2025-11-17T23:58:11+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:14:12+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We study the running time, in terms of first order oracle queries, of differentially private empirical/population risk minimization of Lipschitz convex losses. We first consider the setting where the loss is non-smooth and the optimizer interacts with a private proxy oracle, which sends only private messages about a minibatch of gradients. In this setting, we show that expected running time $Ω(\min\{\frac{\sqrt{d}}{α^2}, \frac{d}{\log(1/α)}\})$ is necessary to achieve $α$ excess risk on problems of dimension $d$ when $d \geq 1/α^2$. Upper bounds via DP-SGD show these results are tight when $d>\tildeΩ(1/α^4)$. We further show our lower bound can be strengthened to $Ω(\min\{\frac{d}{\bar{m}α^2}, \frac{d}{\log(1/α)} \})$ for algorithms which use minibatches of size at most $\bar{m} < \sqrt{d}$. We next consider smooth losses, where we relax the private oracle assumption and give lower bounds under only the condition that the optimizer is private. Here, we lower bound the expected number of first order oracle calls by $\tildeΩ\big(\frac{\sqrt{d}}α + \min\{\frac{1}{α^2}, n\}\big)$, where $n$ is the size of the dataset. Modifications to existing algorithms show this bound is nearly tight. Compared to non-private lower bounds, our results show that differentially private optimizers pay a dimension dependent runtime penalty. Finally, as a natural extension of our proof technique, we show lower bounds in the non-smooth setting for optimizers interacting with information limited oracles. Specifically, if the proxy oracle transmits at most $Γ$-bits of information about the gradients in the minibatch, then $Ω\big(\min\{\frac{d}{α^2Γ}, \frac{d}{\log(1/α)}\}\big)$ oracle calls are needed. This result shows fundamental limitations of gradient quantization techniques in optimization.
中文标题/摘要
标题：关于使用私有算子的私有优化的梯度复杂度
我们研究了Lipschitz凸损失函数在差分隐私下的经验/总体风险最小化所需的首次阶算子查询运行时间。首先考虑损失非光滑的情形，优化器与私有代理算子交互，该算子仅发送关于小批量梯度的私有信息。在此设定下，我们证明在维度为d且d≥1/α²的问题中，实现α超额风险的期望运行时间为Ω(min{√d/α², d/log(1/α)})，而DP-SGD的上界表明当d>˜Ω(1/α⁴)时，该结果是紧的。进一步地，对于使用最大批量大小≤√d的算法，我们可将下界加强为Ω(min{d/(¯mα²), d/log(1/α)})。接着考虑光滑损失情形，我们放松私有算子假设，仅在优化器为私有的条件下给出下界，此时期望首次阶算子调用次数下界为˜Ω(√d/α + min{1/α², n})，其中n为数据集大小。对现有算法的改进表明该下界几乎紧致。与非私有下界相比，我们的结果表明差分隐私优化器需付出依赖维度的运行时间代价。最后，作为证明技术的自然延伸，我们在非光滑设定下，针对与信息受限算子交互的优化器给出下界：若代理算子关于小批量梯度最多传输Γ比特信息，则需Ω(min{d/(α²Γ), d/log(1/α)})次算子调用。该结果揭示了梯度量化技术在优化中的根本性局限。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the computational complexity of differentially private optimization using private oracles, motivated by the need to understand runtime trade-offs in private empirical risk minimization for Lipschitz convex losses. For non-smooth losses with private proxy oracles, it establishes a lower bound of Ω(min{√d/α², d/log(1/α)}) on expected first-order oracle calls to achieve α-excess risk in d-dimensional problems, showing tightness via DP-SGD when d > ̃Ω(1/α⁴). For algorithms using small minibatches, the bound tightens to Ω(min{d/(̄mα²), d/log(1/α)}). For smooth losses, under only privacy of the optimizer, the lower bound becomes ̃Ω(√d/α + min{1/α², n}), nearly matched by modified algorithms. The results demonstrate that differential privacy incurs a dimension-dependent runtime penalty compared to non-private settings. Finally, extending the technique to information-limited oracles, it shows that if gradients are quantized to at most Γ bits, Ω(min{d/(α²Γ), d/log(1/α)}) calls are necessary, revealing fundamental limits of gradient quantization in private optimization.
该文研究了使用私有代理梯度查询的差分隐私优化的计算复杂度，旨在理解在Lipschitz凸损失下的私有经验风险最小化中运行时间的权衡。对于非光滑损失，当维度d≥1/α²时，需Ω(min{√d/α², d/log(1/α)})次期望一阶查询以达到α超额风险，且当d>̃Ω(1/α⁴)时DP-SGD算法达到紧界；若使用小批量（≤̄m<√d），下界提升为Ω(min{d/(̄mα²), d/log(1/α)})。对于光滑损失，仅需优化器隐私条件，下界为̃Ω(√d/α + min{1/α², n})，经算法改进接近紧界。结果表明，差分隐私优化器相比非私有方法需承担维度依赖的运行时代价。最后，针对信息受限代理（每梯度最多传输Γ比特），下界为Ω(min{d/(α²Γ), d/log(1/α)})，揭示梯度量化技术的根本局限。
IMProofBench: Benchmarking AI on Research-Level Mathematical Proof Generation
Authors: Johannes Schmitt, Gergely Bérczi, Jasper Dekoninck, Jeremy Feusi, Tim Gehrunger, Raphael Appenzeller, Pieter Belmans, Alessio Bottini, Jim Bryan, João Camarneiro, Ana Cannas da Silva, Niklas Canova, Ana-Maria Castravet, Timo de Wolff, Claudio Fontanari, Filippo Gaia, Baran Hashemi, Daniel Holmes, David Holmes, Aitor Iribar Lopez, Victor Jaeck, Martina Jørgensen, Steven Kelk, Martijn Kool, Stefan Kuhlmann, Adam Kurpisz, Johannes Lengler, Chiara Meroni, Ingmar Metzler, Martin Möller, Samuel Muñoz-Echániz, David Muñoz-Lahoz, Robert Nowak, Georg Oberdieck, Daniel Platt, Dylan Possamaï, Gabriel Ribeiro, Aluna Rizzoli, Daria Sakhanda, Raúl Sánchez Galán, Zheming Sun, Diaaeldin Taha, Josef Teichmann, Richard P. Thomas, Henk van der Pol, Michel van Garrel, Charles Vial, Ignacio Barros, Benjamin Doerr, Peter Grünwald, Henry Liu, David Martins, Aleksandar Mijatović, Sergej Monavari, Marc Roth, Patrick Schnider, Yannik Schuler, Pim Spelier, Yuuji Tanaka, Ronald van Luijk
First: 2025-09-30T10:50:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:12:00+00:00
Comments: v2: benchmark expanded from 39 to 77 problems; evaluation extended to 14 models including GPT-5.4, Gemini 3.1 Pro, and Claude Opus 4.6; new analyses (IRT-based score aggregation, inter-rater reliability, tool/token usage, non-agentic ablation); contributor author list updated
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As the mathematical capabilities of large language models (LLMs) improve, it becomes increasingly important to evaluate their performance on research-level tasks at the frontier of mathematical knowledge. However, existing benchmarks are limited, as they focus solely on final-answer questions or high-school competition problems. To address this gap, we introduce IMProofBench, a private benchmark consisting of 77 peer-reviewed problems developed by expert mathematicians. Each problem requires a detailed proof and is paired with subproblems that have final answers, supporting both an evaluation by human experts and a large-scale quantitative analysis through automated grading. Furthermore, unlike prior benchmarks, the evaluation setup simulates a realistic research environment: models operate in an agentic framework with tools like web search for literature review and mathematical software such as SageMath. Our results show that current LLMs can already solve a significant percentage of research-level questions. IMProofBench will continue to evolve as a dynamic benchmark in collaboration with the mathematical community, ensuring its relevance for evaluating the next generation of LLMs.
中文标题/摘要
标题：IMProofBench：评估AI在研究级数学证明生成中的表现
随着大型语言模型（LLMs）在数学能力上的提升，评估其在数学知识前沿的研究级任务中的表现变得愈发重要。然而，现有基准测试存在局限，仅关注最终答案问题或高中竞赛题。为填补这一空白，我们推出了IMProofBench，这是一个由77个经同行评审、由专家数学家开发的私有基准测试集。每个问题均需详细证明，并配以具有最终答案的子问题，既支持专家人工评估，也支持通过自动化评分进行大规模量化分析。此外，与以往基准不同，该评估设置模拟了现实研究环境：模型在代理框架中运行，可使用网络搜索进行文献回顾，以及使用SageMath等数学软件。我们的结果显示，当前LLMs已能解决相当比例的研究级问题。IMProofBench将在与数学界合作下持续演进，确保其作为评估下一代LLMs的相关性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to assess large language models' ability to handle research-level mathematical proofs, the authors introduce IMProofBench, a new benchmark comprising 77 peer-reviewed problems crafted by expert mathematicians. Each problem demands a full proof and includes subproblems with final answers, enabling both expert evaluation and automated scoring. The benchmark evaluates models in an agentic framework that incorporates tools like web search and SageMath, simulating real research workflows. Experimental results show that current LLMs can solve a substantial portion of these advanced problems, with performance varying across models; additional analyses including IRT-based scoring and tool usage metrics further validate the benchmark’s rigor and scalability.
为评估大语言模型在真实研究级数学证明生成中的能力，作者构建了IMProofBench，包含77道由数学专家设计的同行评审题目，每题需完整证明并配以子题支持自动化评分。评估在包含网络搜索与SageMath等工具的代理框架下进行，模拟真实研究环境。实验表明当前LLM已能解决相当比例的问题，不同模型表现各异；新增IRT评分、工具使用等分析进一步验证了该基准的严谨性与可扩展性。
LESV: Language Embedded Sparse Voxel Fusion for Open-Vocabulary 3D Scene Understanding
Authors: Fusang Wang, Nathan Piasco, Moussab Bennehar, Luis Roldão, Dzmitry Tsishkou, Fabien Moutarde
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-04-01T20:48:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T16:05:00+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Recent advancements in open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding heavily rely on 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to register vision-language features into 3D space. However, we identify two critical limitations in these approaches: the spatial ambiguity arising from unstructured, overlapping Gaussians which necessitates probabilistic feature registration, and the multi-level semantic ambiguity caused by pooling features over object-level masks, which dilutes fine-grained details. To address these challenges, we present a novel framework that leverages Sparse Voxel Rasterization (SVRaster) as a structured, disjoint geometry representation. By regularizing SVRaster with monocular depth and normal priors, we establish a stable geometric foundation. This enables a deterministic, confidence-aware feature registration process and suppresses the semantic bleeding artifact common in 3DGS. Furthermore, we resolve multi-level ambiguity by exploiting the emerging dense alignment properties of the AM-RADIO foundation model, avoiding the computational overhead of hierarchical training methods. Our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance on Open Vocabulary Point Cloud Understanding, and highly competitive results on 3D and 2D Object Retrieval benchmarks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：LESV：用于开放词汇3D场景理解的语言嵌入稀疏体素融合
近期开放词汇3D场景理解的进展高度依赖3D高斯点绘（3DGS）将视觉-语言特征注册到3D空间。然而，我们发现这些方法存在两个关键局限：由无结构、重叠高斯引起的空域模糊，需要概率性特征注册；以及在对象级掩码上池化特征导致的多层级语义模糊，削弱了细粒度细节。为解决这些问题，我们提出一种新框架，利用稀疏体素光栅化（SVRaster）作为结构化、不相交的几何表示。通过引入单目深度和法线先验正则化SVRaster，我们建立了稳定的几何基础，从而实现确定性、置信度感知的特征注册，并抑制3DGS中常见的语义渗漏伪影。此外，我们通过利用AM-RADIO基础模型新兴的密集对齐特性，解决多层级模糊问题，避免了分层训练方法的计算开销。我们的方法在开放词汇点云理解任务上达到最先进性能，并在3D和2D物体检索基准上取得极具竞争力的结果。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the limitations of 3D Gaussian Splatting in open-vocabulary 3D scene understanding—namely spatial ambiguity from overlapping Gaussians and semantic dilution from object-level pooling—the paper proposes LESV, a framework using Sparse Voxel Rasterization to provide structured, disjoint geometry. By incorporating monocular depth and normal priors, LESV establishes a stable geometric base enabling deterministic, confidence-aware feature registration and suppressing semantic bleeding. It further resolves multi-level semantic ambiguity by leveraging the dense alignment capabilities of AM-RADIO, avoiding hierarchical training. Experimental results show LESV achieves state-of-the-art performance on Open Vocabulary Point Cloud Understanding and competitive results on 3D and 2D Object Retrieval benchmarks.
为解决3D高斯点云在开放词汇3D场景理解中因无序重叠高斯导致的空间模糊和因对象级池化造成的语义稀释问题，本文提出LESV框架，采用稀疏体素光栅化（SVRaster）构建结构化、不重叠的几何表示。通过引入单目深度与法线先验，LESV建立稳定几何基础，实现确定性、置信度感知的特征注册并抑制语义渗漏。同时，利用AM-RADIO基础模型的密集对齐特性解决多层级语义模糊，避免分层训练开销。实验表明，LESV在开放词汇点云理解任务上达到最先进性能，在3D和2D物体检索任务上也取得极具竞争力的结果。
The complexities of patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence
Authors: João Matos, Olivia Buege, Donny Cheung, Gary S. Collins, Paula Dhiman, Nan Li, Bingyu Mao, Benjamin W. Nelson, Michail Ouroutzoglou, Paul Varghese, Jonathan Amar
First: 2026-07-09T15:56:55+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:56:55+00:00
Comments: 36 pages (main text), 129 pages (supplementary materials)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Consumer-facing health chatbots powered by large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used for symptom assessment. However, chatbot development and evaluation often rely on cooperative, articulate, simulated patients. We analysed 2,053 real patient-chatbot conversations and found that communication patterns and expression of emotions vary widely across users. We developed a patient simulator that separately models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation of realism with 15 human graders, simulated conversations were nearly indistinguishable from real ones, with human graders achieving an accuracy of 55%. We used five distinct patient personae, across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, to evaluate the performance of four LLMs in urgency assessment. We found that communication style can significantly alter triage outcomes. Patient-centred conversational artificial intelligence must accommodate communication diversity: systems designed for idealised, rather than realistic, interactions risk underperforming and amplifying health disparities when deployed in the real world.
中文标题/摘要
标题：以患者为中心的对话式人工智能的复杂性
基于大语言模型（LLMs）的面向消费者的健康聊天机器人正越来越多地用于症状评估。然而，聊天机器人的开发与评估往往依赖于合作、表达清晰的模拟患者。我们分析了2053次真实患者与聊天机器人的对话，发现用户间的沟通模式和情绪表达差异显著。我们开发了一种患者模拟器，分别建模临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略和沟通风格。在受图灵测试启发的现实性评估中，15名人类评估者难以区分模拟对话与真实对话，准确率仅为55%。我们使用五种不同患者角色，在1164个临床评估案例中，评估了四种LLMs在紧急程度评估中的表现。结果表明，沟通风格可显著影响分诊结果。以患者为中心的对话式人工智能必须包容沟通多样性：若系统仅针对理想化而非现实化的交互设计，部署于真实世界时可能表现不佳，并加剧健康不平等。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve the realism and fairness of health chatbots powered by large language models, this study analyzed 2,053 real patient-chatbot interactions to uncover diverse communication patterns and emotional expressions. To address this, the researchers developed a patient simulator that independently models clinical content, emotional state, conversational strategy, and communication style. In a Turing-inspired evaluation, human graders could distinguish real from simulated conversations only 55% of the time, indicating high realism. Using five distinct patient personae across 1,164 clinician-graded cases, they tested four LLMs for urgency assessment and found that communication style significantly impacts triage outcomes, highlighting the necessity for patient-centred AI to account for real-world communication diversity to avoid performance gaps and health disparities.
鉴于当前健康聊天机器人开发中普遍依赖理想化模拟患者，本研究分析了2053条真实患者对话，发现用户在沟通风格与情绪表达上差异显著。为此，作者构建了一个可分别建模临床内容、情绪状态、对话策略和沟通风格的患者模拟器。在15名人类评判者参与的图灵测试中，模拟对话与真实对话几乎难以区分，准确率仅为55%。进一步在五类患者角色、1164例临床评分案例中测试四类大语言模型，发现沟通风格显著影响分诊结果，表明面向真实用户的对话式AI必须包容沟通多样性，否则在实际部署中可能因忽略现实差异而性能不足并加剧健康不平等。
A New Human-Likeness and Comfort Index for Robot Movements Along Prescribed Paths
Authors: Rosanna Coccaro, Enrico Ferrentino, Antonio Parziale, Angelo Marcelli, Pasquale Chiacchio
First: 2026-07-09T15:53:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:53:51+00:00
Comments: 14 pages, 5 figures, accepted for IEEE Transactions on Cybernetics
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
As human-robot interaction rapidly spreads in numerous fields, the subject of robot acceptance gains increasing importance. Visual similarity to the human body, as occurs for humanoids, is generally not enough to ensure acceptance in physical interaction, as acceptance directly links to comfort and ergonomics, which are measured in terms of the quality of the robot movement perceived by the human. This paper discusses the connection between comfort and similarity of the robot movement to the human one. By considering the kinematic characterization of human movement, this paper focuses on the time laws of such movements, wherein the end-effector path is prescribed. Based on the lognormality principle for modeling human movements, a human-likeness index is defined and used to provide an a priori characterization of trajectories. Such an index can be used to evaluate the performance of trajectory generation algorithms in producing human-like movements before they are actually executed. For validation purposes, 68 subjects are required to judge their comfort. The results of three experimental campaigns involving a physical interaction with a robot demonstrate a globally consistent trend between the preference in terms of perceived comfort and the distribution of the suggested human-likeness index.
中文标题/摘要
标题：一种用于沿预定路径机器人运动的人类相似性与舒适度新指标
随着人机交互在众多领域迅速普及，机器人接受度的重要性日益增加。视觉上与人体相似（如人形机器人）通常不足以确保物理交互中的接受度，因为接受度直接关联于舒适度和人体工学，而这些因素通过人类感知的机器人运动质量来衡量。本文探讨了舒适度与机器人运动与人类运动相似性之间的联系。基于人类运动的运动学特征，本文聚焦于末端执行器路径已给定情况下的运动时间规律。依据对人类运动建模的对数正态性原理，本文定义了一种人类相似性指标，并用于对轨迹进行先验表征。该指标可用于在轨迹实际执行前评估轨迹生成算法生成类人运动的性能。为验证目的，需68名受试者评估其舒适度。三个涉及物理交互的实验表明，感知舒适度偏好与所建议的人类相似性指标分布之间存在全局一致的趋势。
Summary / 总结
As human-robot interaction expands across domains, acceptance hinges on perceived comfort and ergonomics rather than mere visual similarity. This study defines a human-likeness index based on the lognormality principle of human kinematics, focusing on time laws of prescribed end-effector paths to characterize movement similarity. The index enables pre-execution evaluation of trajectory generation algorithms. In three experiments with 68 human subjects interacting physically with a robot, results consistently showed that higher human-likeness scores correlated with greater perceived comfort, validating the index’s predictive power.
随着人机交互在多个领域普及，机器人被接受的程度取决于舒适性和人体工学，而非外观相似性。本文基于人类运动的对数正态特性，提出一种针对预设路径末端执行器运动时间规律的人类相似性指数，用于在实际执行前评估轨迹生成算法的人类化运动质量。通过三组涉及68名受试者的物理交互实验验证，结果表明该指数与受试者感知舒适度高度一致：指数值越高，舒适度评价越优，证明其预测能力有效。
Generative Action Tell-Tales: Assessing Human Motion in Synthesized Videos
Authors: Xavier Thomas, Youngsun Lim, Ananya Srinivasan, Audrey Zheng, Deepti Ghadiyaram
First: 2025-12-01T15:36:33+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:41:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Despite rapid advances in video generative models, robust metrics for evaluating visual and temporal correctness of complex human actions remain elusive. Critically, existing pure-vision encoders and Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) are strongly appearance-biased, lack temporal understanding, and thus struggle to discern intricate motion dynamics and anatomical implausibilities in generated videos. We tackle this gap by introducing a novel evaluation metric derived from a learned latent space of real-world human actions. Our method first captures the nuances, constraints, and temporal smoothness of real-world motion by fusing appearance-agnostic human skeletal geometry features with appearance-based features. We posit that this combined feature space provides a robust representation of action plausibility. Given a generated video, our metric quantifies its action quality by measuring the distance between its underlying representations and this learned real-world action distribution. For rigorous validation, we develop a new multi-faceted benchmark specifically designed to probe temporally challenging aspects of human action fidelity. Through extensive experiments, we show that our metric achieves substantial improvement of more than 68% compared to existing state-of-the-art methods on our benchmark, performs competitively on established external benchmarks, and has a stronger correlation with human perception. Our in-depth analysis reveals critical limitations in current video generative models and establishes a new standard for advanced research in video generation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：生成式动作告密者：评估合成视频中的人类运动
尽管视频生成模型发展迅速，但评估复杂人类动作视觉与时间正确性的稳健指标仍难以获得。现有纯视觉编码器和多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）高度依赖外观，缺乏时间理解能力，因而难以辨别生成视频中的复杂运动动态和解剖学不合理性。我们通过引入一种基于真实人类动作学习潜在空间的新型评估指标来解决这一问题。该方法首先融合外观无关的人体骨骼几何特征与基于外观的特征，以捕捉真实运动的细微之处、约束条件和时间平滑性。我们认为，这种结合特征空间能提供动作合理性的稳健表示。对于生成视频，我们的指标通过测量其潜在表示与所学真实动作分布之间的距离，量化其动作质量。为严谨验证，我们开发了一个专门用于探测人类动作时间挑战性的多维度基准。大量实验表明，我们的指标在本基准上相较现有最先进方法提升超过68%，在主流外部基准上表现竞争力，并与人类感知具有更强相关性。深入分析揭示了当前视频生成模型的关键局限，确立了视频生成领域先进研究的新标准。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the lack of reliable metrics to evaluate complex human motion in synthesized videos, this work introduces a novel evaluation method that combines appearance-agnostic skeletal geometry with appearance-based features to capture real-world motion constraints and temporal smoothness. The method quantifies action plausibility by measuring the distance between generated video representations and a learned distribution of real human actions in a latent space. Experiments on a newly designed benchmark and external datasets show the metric outperforms state-of-the-art methods by over 68%, correlates more strongly with human perception, and highlights critical shortcomings in current video generation models.
为解决现有视频生成模型缺乏有效评估复杂人体动作的方法，该研究提出一种基于真实人体动作学习潜在空间的新评估指标。该方法融合了无外观依赖的骨骼几何特征与外观特征，以捕捉动作约束和时序平滑性，从而实现对动作合理性的鲁棒评估。在生成视频上，该指标通过测量其潜在表示与真实动作分布的距离来量化动作质量。实验表明，该方法在新构建的基准上比现有最先进方法提升68%以上，在外部基准上表现优异，且与人类感知的相关性更强，同时揭示了当前视频生成模型的关键局限。
Theoria: Rewrite-Acceptability Verification over Informal Reasoning States
Authors: Michael Saldivar, Ben Slivinski
First: 2026-07-01T17:56:42+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:39:53+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
When should an AI system's answer be trusted? Formal proof assistants offer certainty but cannot reach most of the problem distribution; scalar LLM judges offer coverage but produce opaque scores that cannot be audited after the fact and are subject to the same coherence issues as any LLM. We present Theoria, a verification architecture that closes this gap. A candidate solution is rewritten into a sequence of typed state transitions, each licensed by an explicit justification, whether that be a citation, computation, or problem-given fact, and every transition is independently auditable. The foundational invariant is completeness of change: every difference between consecutive proof states must be accounted for, so hidden premises surface as unlicensed mutations rather than passing silently. On HLE-Verified Gold (185 text-only expert problems), Theoria certifies 105 at 91.4% strict precision (Wilson 95% CI [84.5%, 95.4%]). Every certification produces a human readable proof trace in which each step can be independently challenged. Holistic LLM judges achieve comparable precision at matched coverage but fail on different problems (Jaccard 0.14-0.36), making the approaches complementary. On 95 adversarial poisoned proofs across 15 domains, structured judges catch 94.7% versus 83.2% for holistic judging (p= 0.0017). The overall 11.5 pp gap concentrates in hidden premises (90.6% vs. 62.5%, a 28 pp difference) and fabricated citations (100% vs. 90%), the error classes where the formal analysis predicts an advantage; performance is identical on arithmetic and theorem-misapplication errors, where no advantage is predicted. On GPQA Diamond (n= 65), certified precision is 97.1% (Wilson CI [85.1%, 99.5%]).
中文标题/摘要
标题：Theoria：基于非正式推理状态的重写可接受性验证
何时应信任AI系统的答案？形式化证明助手提供确定性，但无法覆盖大部分问题分布；标量LLM评判器具有广泛覆盖性，但其评分不透明，事后无法审计，且与任何LLM一样存在连贯性问题。我们提出Theoria，一种弥合此差距的验证架构。候选解被重写为一系列带类型的状态转换序列，每个转换由明确的正当化（如引用、计算或题目给定事实）授权，且每个转换均可独立审计。其基础不变式是变化的完备性：连续证明状态间的每项差异都必须被解释，隐含前提将表现为无授权突变而非悄然通过。在HLE-Verified Gold（185个纯文本专家问题）上，Theoria以91.4%严格精度（Wilson 95%置信区间[84.5%，95.4%]）认证了105个问题。每项认证均生成可读证明轨迹，每一步均可独立质疑。整体LLM评判器在匹配覆盖度下精度相当，但失败于不同问题（Jaccard 0.14-0.36），使两种方法互补。在15个领域95个对抗性污染证明中，结构化评判器捕获94.7%，而整体评判仅83.2%（p=0.0017）。整体11.5个百分点差距集中于隐含前提（90.6% vs. 62.5%，差28个百分点）和伪造引用（100% vs. 90%），即形式化分析预测优势的错误类别；在算术和定理误用错误上表现相同，因形式化分析未预测优势。在GPQA Diamond（n=65）上，认证精度达97.1%（Wilson置信区间[85.1%，99.5%]）。
Summary / 总结
Theoria addresses the challenge of trusting AI answers by bridging the gap between formal proof assistants and LLM-based judges. It verifies informal reasoning by rewriting candidate solutions into sequences of typed state transitions, each justified by explicit citations, computations, or given facts, enabling independent auditability. The system enforces completeness of change, forcing hidden premises to surface as unlicensed mutations. On HLE-Verified Gold, it certifies 105 out of 185 problems with 91.4% strict precision, producing human-readable proof traces. Compared to holistic LLM judges, Theoria achieves higher precision on adversarial poisoned proofs (94.7% vs. 83.2%) and excels at detecting hidden premises and fabricated citations, while matching performance on arithmetic and theorem-misapplication errors. On GPQA Diamond, it achieves 97.1% certified precision.
Theoria旨在解决AI答案可信度问题，通过将候选答案重写为带明确依据的类型化状态转换序列，实现可审计的验证。其核心机制是强制‘变化完整性’，使隐藏前提必须显式声明，否则视为非法突变。在HLE-Verified Gold数据集上，其严格精度达91.4%，并生成可挑战的可读证明轨迹。相比整体LLM评判器，Theoria在对抗性污染证明上表现更优（94.7% vs 83.2%），尤其在检测隐藏前提和伪造引用方面优势显著（分别提升28pp和10pp），而在算术和定理误用错误上表现持平，符合预期。在GPQA Diamond上，其认证精度高达97.1%。
TNODEV: Toolbox for Neural ODE Verification
Authors: Abdelrahman Sayed Sayed, Pierre-Jean Meyer, Mohamed Ghazel
First: 2026-06-15T11:09:06+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:36:14+00:00
Comments: 30 pages, 7 figures, Under review in TMLR
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Neural ordinary differential equations (neural ODE) gained attention in safety critical settings such as continuous-time controllers for cyber-physical systems and classifiers integrated into automated decision pipelines, raising the question whether their behavior can be formally verified. Existing tools dedicated to neural ODE provide only a single reachability call without iterative input-set refinement, limiting the precision of their verdicts to whatever one reachability call can deliver. We present TNODEV, the first formal verifier for neural ODE that integrates a falsification checker, a fast interval-based reachability backend based on continuous-time mixed monotonicity, a verification and refinement loop with three input-set splitting heuristics, and a parallel scheduler in a single end-to-end pipeline. TNODEV supports safe-set inclusion verification on pure neural ODE, neural ODE in closed loop with a neural network controller and general neural ODE (GNODE), with the safe set specified either as an interval or as the half-space intersection induced by a target classification label. We evaluate TNODEV on a range of benchmarks across safe-set inclusion and classification-robustness properties, including a direct reachability comparison against NNV 2.0 and CORA and a verification comparison against NNV 2.0 on MNIST general neural ODE classifiers.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TNODEV：神经常微分方程验证工具箱
神经常微分方程（神经ODE）在如物理信息系统中的连续时间控制器和集成于自动化决策流程中的分类器等安全关键场景中受到关注，由此引发对其行为是否可形式化验证的疑问。现有针对神经ODE的工具仅提供单次可达性调用，且无迭代输入集细化，其验证精度受限于单次可达性调用所能提供的结果。我们提出TNODEV，这是首个集成虚假性检测器、基于连续时间混合单调性的快速区间可达性后端、包含三种输入集分割启发式方法的验证与细化循环，以及并行调度器的端到端验证工具。TNODEV支持纯神经ODE、闭环神经网络控制器下的神经ODE及通用神经ODE（GNODE）的安全集包含验证，其中安全集可指定为区间或由目标分类标签诱导的半空间交集。我们在涵盖安全集包含与分类鲁棒性属性的多个基准测试中评估了TNODEV，包括与NNV 2.0和CORA的直接可达性对比，以及与NNV 2.0在MNIST通用神经ODE分类器上的验证对比。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to formally verify neural ODEs in safety-critical applications, TNODEV introduces an end-to-end verification pipeline that combines falsification checking, interval-based reachability via continuous-time mixed monotonicity, and iterative input-set refinement through three splitting heuristics, all orchestrated by a parallel scheduler. Evaluated on benchmarks for safe-set inclusion and classification robustness, TNODEV outperforms existing tools like NNV 2.0 and CORA in reachability precision and achieves stronger verification results on MNIST-based GNODE classifiers, demonstrating its effectiveness in handling both pure neural ODEs and closed-loop systems with neural controllers.
为满足在安全关键场景中对神经常微分方程（神经ODE）进行形式化验证的需求，TNODEV提出了一种端到端验证框架，结合了反例检测、基于区间和连续时间混合单调性的可达性分析、以及通过三种分割启发式方法实现的输入集迭代细化，并由并行调度器协调。实验表明，TNODEV在可达性任务中比NNV 2.0和CORA更精确，在MNIST数据集上对神经ODE分类器的分类鲁棒性验证中也优于NNV 2.0，验证了其在纯神经ODE、闭环控制器及一般神经ODE中安全集包含性验证方面的有效性。
When Structured Sparse Autoencoders Learn Consistent Concepts Across Modalities
Authors: Weiduo Liao, Yunqiao Yang, Ying Wei
First: 2026-07-09T15:35:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:35:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparse autoencoders (SAEs) have emerged as a promising technique for mechanistic interpretability by learning a set of sparse latent features in large models, each of which encodes a distinct concept. However, in vision-language models (VLMs), vanilla SAEs struggle to learn modality-consistent concepts, with concepts often exhibiting fragmented coverage (i.e., disjoint regions) in the visual modality. To address this challenge, we propose a Structured Sparse AutoEncoder ($S^2AE$) that enforces concept consistency from both semantic and spatial perspectives in the visual modality. Specifically, we group image patches based on Transformer attention similarity and spatial proximity, and introduce a structured sparsity regularization when training the vanilla SAE. The regularization consists of exclusive sparsity for inter-group concept disentanglement and group sparsity for intra-group concept consistency, which drives the latent neurons by SAEs to specialize in distinct, semantically grounded concepts. Evaluated on the \texttt{Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct} model, the method achieves 6.06% average improvement in semantic alignment (mIoU) and 60.81 in representational efficiency (lower l0 norm) while maintaining near-perfect reconstruction fidelity with an Explained Variance above 99%. Cross-modal analysis further demonstrates that $S^2AE$ enhances neuronal monosemanticity by this visual structural prior, achieving a 3.08% average gain in semantic consistency and a 2.37% average gain in monosemanticity scores for both modalities of multimodal features, thereby fostering more coherent and disentangled representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：结构化稀疏自编码器如何在多模态间学习一致的概念
稀疏自编码器（SAEs）作为一种机制可解释性技术，通过在大型模型中学习一组稀疏潜在特征，每个特征编码一个独特概念。然而，在视觉-语言模型（VLMs）中，基础SAEs难以学习模态一致的概念，视觉模态中的概念常呈现碎片化覆盖（即不连续区域）。为解决此问题，我们提出结构化稀疏自编码器（$S^2AE$），从语义和空间两个角度强制视觉模态中的概念一致性。具体而言，我们根据Transformer注意力相似性和空间邻近性对图像块进行分组，并在训练基础SAE时引入结构化稀疏正则化。该正则化包含组间概念解耦的排他性稀疏性和组内概念一致性的组稀疏性，促使SAE的潜在神经元专注于语义基础的差异化概念。在\texttt{Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct}模型上评估，该方法在语义对齐（mIoU）上平均提升6.06%，在表征效率（更低l0范数）上提升60.81%，同时保持近完美的重建保真度（解释方差超过99%）。跨模态分析进一步表明，$S^2AE$通过视觉结构先验增强神经元的单语义性，在多模态特征的两个模态中分别实现语义一致性平均提升3.08%和单语义性评分平均提升2.37%，从而促进更连贯且解耦的表征。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the fragmentation of visual concepts in vision-language models using vanilla sparse autoencoders, this work introduces a Structured Sparse AutoEncoder ($S^2AE$) that enforces semantic and spatial consistency in visual representations. The method groups image patches via Transformer attention and spatial proximity, then applies structured sparsity regularization—combining exclusive sparsity for inter-group disentanglement and group sparsity for intra-group consistency—to guide latent neurons toward semantically grounded, distinct concepts. Evaluated on Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct, $S^2AE$ improved semantic alignment (mIoU) by 6.06%, reduced l0 norm (representational efficiency) by 60.81, maintained >99% reconstruction fidelity, and enhanced cross-modal monosemanticity by 2.37% and semantic consistency by 3.08%.
为解决视觉语言模型中视觉概念碎片化的问题，作者提出结构化稀疏自编码器（S²AE），通过Transformer注意力和空间邻近性对图像块进行分组，并在训练中引入结构化稀疏正则化，以促进组间概念解耦与组内概念一致性。在Qwen2.5-VL-7B-Instruct模型上，该方法使语义对齐提升6.06%（mIoU），l0范数降低60.81%（提升表示效率），并保持近完美重建（解释方差>99%）。跨模态分析显示，语义一致性提升3.08%，单语义性得分提升2.37%，表明多模态表示更连贯且解耦。
Towards Precision Therapy in Hepatocellular Carcinoma: A Clinical-Reasoning LLM for Risk Stratification and Treatment Guidance
Authors: Peng Cui, Jitao Wang, Siyan Xue, Yao Huang, Haoming Xia, Dong Li, Dengxiang Liu, Weilin Wang, Liping Liu, Leida Zhang, Yunfu Cui, Tao Peng, Daolin Ji, Haitao Zhao, Wei Zhang, Xiaojuan Wang, Weijie Ma, Zongren Ding, Jinlong Li, Yuan Ding, Jiajing Zhao, Zhiyu Chen, Chengkun Yang, Ziyue Huang, Jiaqi Liu, Fusheng Liu, Yang Zhou, Xiaojuan Wang, Zhongquan Sun, Shiyun Bao, Xiaojun Wang, Ming Yang, Guangxin Li, Bin Shu, Yong Liao, Hongxuan Li, Yao Tang, Shizhong Yang, Yongyi Zeng, Yufeng Yuan, Yinpeng Dong, Jihui Hao, Jun Zhu, Jiahong Dong
First: 2026-07-09T15:33:08+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:33:08+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hepatocellular carcinoma (HCC) is a common malignancy and a leading cause of cancer-related mortality. Current guidelines and staging systems provide coarse categories, but often miss within-stage heterogeneity and the clinical context in electronic medical records (EMRs). We present HCC-STAR (Hepatocellular Carcinoma Staging, Treatment And pRognosis), a clinically aligned large language model that reads routine EMR narratives and jointly outputs risk score-based staging, ranked guideline-consistent treatments with evidence-based rationales, and individualized survival estimates. We curated about 30,000 HCC cases from SEER and expanded them into EMR-style narrative training data using a clinician-validated, prompt-based augmentation workflow. On this corpus, we developed a knowledge-aligned reasoning framework optimized with a step-verifiable composite reward, moving beyond text-level memorization of clinical guidelines. In a multi-center cohort of 6,668 patients from 12 hospitals in China, HCC-STAR achieved state-of-the-art performance in treatment recommendation and risk stratification compared with clinical guidelines and competitive models, including GPT-5 and Gemini-2.5 Pro. Hypothetical overall-survival analysis showed a median survival of 51 months under adherence to HCC-STAR recommendations, compared with 29 and 32 months under BCLC and CNLC. In clinician-centric evaluations, blinded hepatobiliary specialists rated HCC-STAR's reasoning and evidence-based justifications as trustworthy. The model surpassed resident and attending physicians in treatment accuracy and helped physicians make more accurate decisions faster when used as an assistant. These findings support HCC-STAR as a reliable and verifiable decision-support system for risk stratification and precision therapy in HCC.
