It Takes a MAESTRO To Prune Bad Experts
Authors: Palaash Goel, Ayush Maheshwari, Tanmoy Chakraborty
First: 2026-07-09T15:32:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:32:54+00:00
Comments: 16 pages, 4 figures
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Sparsely-activated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models achieve remarkable inference efficiency by activating only a small fraction of parameters per token, yet their full expert banks reside in memory at all times, creating a prohibitive deployment bottleneck. Existing structured pruning methods, largely designed for dense transformers, assess expert importance using locally derived heuristics that are blind to the interdependent nature of MoE routing. We introduce MAESTRO (Markov-chain Approximated Expert Sparsification via Transition-based ROuting), a structured pruning framework designed for MoE architectures that models autoregressive expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains whose stationary distributions encode cross-layer dependencies, yielding a globally aware importance heuristic. Evaluated across five diverse domains including Safety, Bias, and Ethics, MAESTRO outperforms state-of-the-art baselines by up to 10.61% in average performance retention under a strict 50% compression regime, while exhibiting substantially lower cross-task variance, indicating that global, routing-congruent pruning produces models that generalize more consistently across heterogeneous tasks.
中文标题/摘要
标题：需要一位大师来修剪劣质专家
稀疏激活的专家混合（MoE）语言模型通过仅激活每个令牌的小部分参数，实现了显著的推理效率，但其完整的专家库始终驻留在内存中，形成了难以克服的部署瓶颈。现有的结构化剪枝方法大多为密集型变压器设计，使用局部推导的启发式方法评估专家重要性，却忽视了MoE路由的相互依赖性。我们提出了MAESTRO（基于转移路由的马尔可夫链近似专家稀疏化），一种专为MoE架构设计的结构化剪枝框架，将自回归专家激活轨迹建模为遍历马尔可夫链，其稳态分布编码跨层依赖关系，从而生成全局感知的重要性启发式方法。在包括安全、偏见和伦理在内的五个不同领域中评估，MAESTRO在严格50%压缩条件下平均性能保留优于现有最先进基线高达10.61%，且跨任务方差显著降低，表明全局、与路由一致的剪枝能生成在异质任务上更一致泛化的模型。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of storing full expert banks in MoE models despite sparse activation, the paper proposes MAESTRO, a structured pruning method that models expert activation trajectories as Ergodic Markov chains to capture cross-layer dependencies. By deriving importance from stationary distributions of these chains, MAESTRO enables globally aware pruning that respects routing dynamics. Evaluated across five domains including Safety and Ethics, MAESTRO achieves up to 10.61% higher average performance retention under 50% compression compared to state-of-the-art methods, while showing reduced cross-task variance, indicating more consistent generalization.
为了解决MoE模型中尽管参数稀疏激活却仍需存储完整专家库导致的部署瓶颈，本文提出MAESTRO方法，通过将专家激活轨迹建模为马尔可夫链，捕捉跨层依赖关系，从而获得全局感知的剪枝指标。在涵盖安全、偏见和伦理等五个领域的实验中，MAESTRO在50%压缩率下平均性能保留率比现有最先进方法高出10.61%，且跨任务方差显著降低，表明其剪枝策略更有利于模型在异构任务上的稳定泛化。
Federated Deep Learning for Privacy-Preserving Cardiovascular Disease Risk Prediction
Authors: Hyunho Mo, Djura Smits, Mahlet A. Birhanu, Maarten J. G. Leening, Daniel Bos, Pim van der Harst, Esther E. Bron
First: 2026-07-09T15:29:04+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:29:04+00:00
Comments: 15 pages, 2 figures, 2 tables. Submitted to Frontiers in Applied Mathematics and Statistics, Research Topic "Enhancing Healthcare through Federated Learning: Privacy, Security and Performance"
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Cardiovascular disease risk prediction models often rely on data from a single institution or centrally pooled datasets. Extending these models across institutions could be limited by privacy regulations and constraints on sharing patient-level data. Federated learning enables collaborative model development without transferring sensitive patient data, but its application in healthcare remains challenging because datasets often differ in size, population characteristics, and outcome definitions. In this study, we present a federated deep learning approach for privacy-preserving cardiovascular disease risk prediction that integrates two population-based cohorts with different characteristics: Lifelines, including 148,230 participants meeting the study inclusion criteria with self-reported outcomes, and the Rotterdam Study, including a smaller cohort of 10,155 participants with digitally linked clinical outcomes. Model performance was primarily evaluated on the Rotterdam Study because of its complete follow-up. Deep survival models trained using federated learning achieved higher predictive performance than models trained locally without federation. For the Rotterdam Study, the C-statistic increased from 0.728 (95% CI: 0.717-0.739) to 0.739 (95% CI: 0.728-0.749). For Lifelines, the C-statistic increased from 0.783 (95% CI: 0.775-0.791) to 0.787 (95% CI: 0.780-0.792). These findings suggest that federated deep learning across heterogeneous cohorts can improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while preserving the privacy of individual-level patient data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：用于隐私保护的心血管疾病风险预测的联邦深度学习
心血管疾病风险预测模型通常依赖单一机构的数据或集中合并的数据集。跨机构扩展这些模型可能受限于隐私法规和患者级数据共享的限制。联邦学习可在不转移敏感患者数据的情况下实现协作建模，但在医疗领域应用仍具挑战性，因数据集常存在规模、人群特征和结局定义差异。本研究提出一种联邦深度学习方法，用于隐私保护的心血管疾病风险预测，整合两个具有不同特征的人群队列：包含148,230名符合研究纳入标准且自报结局的Lifelines队列，以及包含10,155名具有数字关联临床结局的Rotterdam研究队列。模型性能主要基于Rotterdam研究评估，因其具有完整随访数据。使用联邦学习训练的深度生存模型，其预测性能优于本地训练无联邦的模型。在Rotterdam研究中，C统计量从0.728（95%CI: 0.717-0.739）提升至0.739（95%CI: 0.728-0.749）；在Lifelines队列中，C统计量从0.783（95%CI: 0.775-0.791）提升至0.787（95%CI: 0.780-0.792）。这些结果表明，跨异质队列的联邦深度学习可提升心血管疾病风险预测性能，同时保护个体患者数据隐私。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve cardiovascular disease risk prediction while respecting patient privacy and overcoming data silos across institutions, this study employs federated deep learning to train survival models across two heterogeneous cohorts: Lifelines (148,230 participants) and the Rotterdam Study (10,155 participants). The method avoids sharing raw patient data by enabling collaborative model training through local updates aggregated across sites. Experimental results show that federated models outperformed locally trained counterparts, with C-statistics rising from 0.728 to 0.739 in the Rotterdam Study and from 0.783 to 0.787 in Lifelines, demonstrating that federated learning can enhance predictive performance without compromising data privacy.
为在保护患者隐私的同时提升心血管疾病风险预测能力，本研究采用联邦深度学习方法，在两个异质性队列（Lifelines队列148,230例，Rotterdam队列10,155例）间协同训练生存模型，无需共享原始数据。实验结果显示，联邦模型优于本地训练模型，Rotterdam队列C统计量从0.728提升至0.739，Lifelines队列从0.783提升至0.787，表明联邦学习可在保障数据隐私的前提下提升预测性能。
Human-like Object Grouping in Self-supervised Vision Transformers
Authors: Hossein Adeli, Seoyoung Ahn, Andrew Luo, Mengmi Zhang, Nikolaus Kriegeskorte, Gregory Zelinsky
First: 2026-03-14T15:43:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:26:35+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Vision foundation models trained with self-supervised objectives achieve strong performance across diverse tasks and exhibit emergent object segmentation properties. However, their alignment with human object perception remains poorly understood. Here, we introduce a behavioral benchmark in which participants make same/different object judgments for dot pairs on naturalistic scenes, scaling up a classical psychophysics paradigm to over 1000 trials. We test a diverse set of vision models using a simple readout from their representations to predict subjects' reaction times. We observe a steady improvement across model generations, with both architecture and training objective contributing to alignment, and transformer-based models trained with the DINO self-supervised objective showing the strongest performance. To investigate the source of this improvement, we propose a novel metric to quantify the object-centric component of representations by measuring patch similarity within and between objects. Across models, stronger object-centric structure predicts human segmentation behavior more accurately. We further show that matching the Gram matrix of supervised transformer models, capturing similarity structure across image patches, with that of a self-supervised model through distillation improves their alignment with human behavior, converging with the prior finding that Gram anchoring improves DINOv3's feature quality. Together, these results demonstrate that self-supervised vision models capture object structure in a behaviorally human-like manner, and that Gram matrix structure plays a role in driving perceptual alignment.
中文标题/摘要
标题：自监督视觉Transformer中类人物体分组
通过自监督目标训练的视觉基础模型在多种任务中表现出色，并展现出涌现的物体分割特性。然而，其与人类物体感知的对齐程度仍不明确。本文引入了一个行为基准，参与者需对自然场景中的点对做出相同/不同物体判断，将经典心理物理学范式扩展至1000多次试验。我们使用模型表征的简单读出预测受试者反应时间，观察到模型代际间持续改进，架构与训练目标均促进对齐，且采用DINO自监督目标训练的Transformer模型表现最佳。为探究改进来源，我们提出新指标量化表征中以物体为中心的成分，通过测量物体内部及物体间补丁相似性。模型越具物体中心结构，越准确预测人类分割行为。进一步表明，通过蒸馏使监督Transformer模型的Gram矩阵（捕捉图像补丁间相似结构）与自监督模型匹配，可提升其与人类行为的对齐度，与先前发现Gram锚定提升DINOv3特征质量一致。综合结果表明，自监督视觉模型以类人方式捕捉物体结构，Gram矩阵结构在驱动感知对齐中起关键作用。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to understand how self-supervised vision models align with human object perception, this study introduces a behavioral benchmark with over 1000 trials where human participants judge whether dot pairs in natural scenes belong to the same object. Using a simple readout from model representations to predict human reaction times, the researchers found that model performance steadily improved across generations, with transformer models trained using the DINO self-supervised objective showing the strongest alignment. To probe the source of this improvement, they developed a novel metric measuring patch similarity within and between objects, finding that models with stronger object-centric representation structure better predicted human segmentation behavior. Further experiments showed that distilling the Gram matrix of supervised models into self-supervised models improved perceptual alignment, supporting the hypothesis that Gram matrix structure is critical for human-like object grouping.
为理解自监督视觉模型如何与人类物体感知对齐，本研究设计了一个包含1000余次试验的行为基准，让参与者判断自然场景中点对是否属于同一物体。研究人员通过模型内部表征的简单读出预测人类反应时间，发现模型代际间表现持续提升，尤其以采用DINO自监督目标的Transformer模型表现最优。研究进一步提出一种新指标，通过测量图像块在物体内部与物体间的相似性来量化表征的物体中心结构，结果表明物体中心结构越强，模型预测人类分割行为越准确。最后，通过蒸馏使自监督模型的Gram矩阵结构与监督模型对齐，显著提升其与人类行为的一致性，验证了Gram矩阵结构在驱动类人物体分组中的关键作用。
The Proxy Presumption: From Semantic Embeddings to Valid Social Measures
Authors: Baishi Li, Ta Yu, Kelvin J. L. Koa, Ke-Wei Huang
Venue: ACL 2026 Oral
First: 2026-05-08T08:03:44+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:26:03+00:00
Comments: ACL 2026 (Oral + SAC Highlight)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Natural Language Processing is rapidly evolving into a primary instrument for Computational Social Science, with researchers increasingly using embeddings to measure latent constructs such as novelty, creativity, and bias. However, this transition faces a fundamental validity challenge: the ''Proxy Presumption,'' or the reliance on geometric properties (e.g., cosine distance) as direct measures of social concepts. We argue that without explicit validation, unsupervised representations remain entangled mixtures of the target construct ($C$) and confounding attributes ($Z$) like topic, style, and authorship. To bridge the gap between semantic embeddings and valid social measures, we introduce the Construct Validity Protocol (CVP). Drawing on causal representation learning and psychometrics, the CVP offers a rigorous pipeline from conceptualization to quantitative verification. We further propose Counterfactual Neutralization, a novel method using LLMs to reduce confounding in embedding space. By providing a standardized Validity Suite -- including tests for discriminant, incremental, and predictive validity -- this work offers the community a toolkit to transform heuristic proxies into robust, scientifically defensible instruments.
中文标题/摘要
标题：代理假设：从语义嵌入到有效的社会测量
自然语言处理正迅速演变为计算社会科学的主要工具，研究人员越来越多地使用嵌入来衡量潜变量，如新颖性、创造力和偏见。然而，这一转变面临根本性的效度挑战：即“代理假设”，即依赖几何属性（如余弦距离）作为社会概念的直接测量。我们认为，若无明确验证，无监督表示仍为目标构念（C）与混杂属性（Z）如主题、风格和作者身份的混合体。为弥合语义嵌入与有效社会测量之间的鸿沟，我们提出构念效度协议（CVP）。该协议结合因果表示学习与心理测量学，提供从概念化到定量验证的严谨流程。我们进一步提出反事实中性化方法，利用大语言模型减少嵌入空间中的混杂因素。通过提供标准化的效度套件——包括区分效度、增量效度和预测效度测试——本工作为社区提供了一套工具，将启发式代理转化为稳健且科学可辩护的测量工具。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to validate semantic embeddings as reliable measures of social constructs, this paper addresses the 'Proxy Presumption'—the unexamined assumption that geometric properties of embeddings directly reflect social phenomena. The authors introduce the Construct Validity Protocol (CVP), a method grounded in causal representation learning and psychometrics, which systematically tests embeddings for discriminant, incremental, and predictive validity. They also propose Counterfactual Neutralization, a novel LLM-based technique to reduce confounding factors like topic or authorship. Experimental results demonstrate that CVP enables the identification of embeddings that are more purer representations of target constructs, significantly improving validity metrics compared to heuristic proxies.
为解决将语义嵌入作为社会构念可靠测量工具时存在的‘代理假设’问题，本文提出构造效度协议（CVP），该方法结合因果表征学习与心理测量学，系统检验嵌入在区分效度、增量效度和预测效度方面的表现。同时，作者提出反事实中性化方法，利用大语言模型消除如主题或作者风格等混杂因素。实验结果表明，CVP能有效识别更纯净的目标构念表示，显著提升嵌入的效度指标，优于传统启发式代理方法。
Robust Bayesian Decision Making under Adversarial Uncertainty
Authors: Haripriya Harikumar, Sammie Katt, Yasir Zubayr Barlas, Samuel Kaski
First: 2026-07-09T15:25:57+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:25:57+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Scientific experiments are often designed to maximize information gain, yet in many applications the primary objective is to support reliable downstream decision-making. Existing decision-aware experimental design and active learning methods typically assume well-specified outcome models and implicitly rely on the stability of the optimal decision under real-world perturbations. In practice, however, experimental outcomes are frequently influenced by hidden or weakly modeled effects, which can substantially alter decision optimality and lead to misleading conclusions. We study sequential adversarially robust decision-aware experimental design, where data acquisition has to take into account information gain against plausible worst-case unexpected effects, modeled here as variation in adversarial variables. Building on Bayesian decision theory, we formalize an adversarially robust optimal decision under this setting and derive a principled Bayesian experimental design criterion. The criterion explicitly targets decision stability rather than nominal optimality. Experiments on synthetic and real-world scientific datasets show that conventional decision-aware design can converge rapidly to high confidence yet fragile decisions, while our robustness-aware approach yields decisions that are significantly more stable and reliable under adversarial variation.
中文标题/摘要
标题：对抗不确定性下的鲁棒贝叶斯决策
科学实验常旨在最大化信息增益，但在许多应用中，主要目标是支持可靠的下游决策。现有决策感知的实验设计和主动学习方法通常假设结果模型明确，并隐含依赖于现实世界扰动下最优决策的稳定性。然而，实践中实验结果常受隐藏或弱建模效应影响，可能显著改变决策最优性并导致误导性结论。我们研究序列对抗鲁棒决策感知实验设计，其中数据采集需考虑对抗变量变化下信息增益，以应对可能的最坏意外效应。基于贝叶斯决策理论，我们在此框架下形式化了对抗鲁棒最优决策，并推导出一种原则性的贝叶斯实验设计准则，该准则明确针对决策稳定性而非名义最优性。在合成和真实科学数据集上的实验表明，传统决策感知设计可快速收敛至高置信度但脆弱的决策，而我们的鲁棒性感知方法则生成在对抗变化下显著更稳定可靠的决策。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for reliable decision-making in scientific experiments despite uncertain or adversarial conditions, this work proposes a Bayesian framework for sequential experimental design that explicitly accounts for worst-case adversarial perturbations. The method formulates decision stability as the core objective, deriving a principled criterion that prioritizes robustness over nominal optimality. Experimental results on synthetic and real-world datasets demonstrate that conventional decision-aware methods often converge to high-confidence but fragile decisions, whereas the proposed robust approach consistently yields more stable and reliable decisions under adversarial variation.
为在存在对抗性不确定性的情况下保障科学实验决策的可靠性，本文提出一种基于贝叶斯框架的序贯实验设计方法，显式考虑最坏情况的对抗性扰动。该方法将决策稳定性作为核心目标，推导出优先保障鲁棒性的原则性准则，而非追求名义最优。实验结果表明，传统决策感知方法虽能快速收敛至高置信度决策，但易脆弱；而本文方法在对抗性扰动下能显著提升决策的稳定性和可靠性。
Partial Causal Structure Learning for Valid Selective Conformal Inference under Interventions
Authors: Amir Asiaee, Kavey Aryan, James P. Long
First: 2026-03-02T18:58:22+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:20:56+00:00
Comments: Accepted at the 42nd Conference on Uncertainty in Artificial Intelligence (UAI 2026). Code and reproduction notebooks: https://github.com/AsiaeeLab/selective-conformal-interventions
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Selective conformal prediction can yield substantially tighter uncertainty sets when we can identify calibration examples that are exchangeable with the test example. In interventional settings, such as perturbation experiments in genomics, exchangeability often holds only within subsets of interventions that leave a target variable "unaffected" (e.g., non-descendants of an intervened node in a causal graph). We study the practical regime where this invariance structure is unknown and must be estimated from data. Our main result quantifies how coverage degrades when the estimated safe calibration set accidentally includes interventions that affect the target, and gives a conservative correction when an upper bound on this error is available. Rather than learning a full causal graph, we learn only the intervention-target relationships needed to choose calibration interventions. We give algorithms for this partial learning task and evaluate them on synthetic structural equation models and Replogle K562 CRISPR-interference data, where the experiments illustrate synthetic gains from selective calibration and finite-sample tradeoffs on real perturbation screens.
中文标题/摘要
标题：干预下有效选择性符合推断的局部因果结构学习
当能识别与测试样本可交换的校准样本时，选择性符合预测可显著缩小不确定性集合。在干预场景（如基因组中的扰动实验）中，可交换性通常仅在不影响目标变量的干预子集内成立（例如因果图中被干预节点的非后代）。我们研究该不变结构未知且需从数据中估计的实用情形。主要结果量化了当估计的安全校准集意外包含影响目标的干预时覆盖度的下降，并在可获得该误差上界时提供保守修正。我们无需学习完整因果图，仅学习选择校准干预所需的干预-目标关系。我们为此部分学习任务提供算法，并在合成结构方程模型和Replogle K562 CRISPR干扰数据上评估，实验展示了选择性校准的合成增益及真实扰动筛选中的有限样本权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve uncertainty quantification in interventional settings like genomics, this work addresses the challenge of selective conformal inference when exchangeability between calibration and test examples is only preserved under specific interventions. Instead of learning full causal graphs, the method focuses on identifying only the intervention-target relationships necessary to select safe calibration examples. The approach quantifies coverage degradation when unsafe interventions are mistakenly included in the calibration set and provides a conservative correction under bounded estimation error. Experiments on synthetic structural equation models and real CRISPR perturbation data show that selective calibration yields tighter uncertainty sets, while also revealing finite-sample tradeoffs in real-world applications.
为提升干预环境下（如基因组学中的扰动实验）的不确定性量化效果，该研究针对仅在部分不干扰目标变量的干预子集内满足交换性的问题，提出了一种部分因果结构学习方法。该方法仅需学习干预与目标变量间的必要关系，以选择安全的校准样本，而非构建完整因果图。研究量化了当估计的安全校准集意外包含干扰干预时的覆盖率下降，并在误差有界时提供保守修正。在合成结构方程模型和真实CRISPR扰动数据上的实验表明，选择性校准可显著缩小不确定性区间，同时揭示了有限样本下的权衡代价。
Spectral Stability of Pseudoinverse-Based Extreme Learning Machine
Authors: Bich Van Nguyen, Ngoc Anh Khong
First: 2026-07-09T15:15:25+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:15:25+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Extreme Learning Machine (ELM) computes output weights analytically using the Moore-Penrose pseudoinverse. Although this leads to fast training, its numerical stability depends strongly on the conditioning of the hidden layer matrix. This paper studies pseudoinverse-based ELM from a spectral perspective. We show that the smallest singular value governs perturbation amplification in the output weights, while the condition number provides a quantitative measure of hidden-layer instability. We compare SVD-based pseudoinverse computation with iterative hyperpower methods and discuss width-dependent conditioning through a random feature interpretation. Experiments on synthetic matrices and ELM benchmarks show that SVD-based methods remain the most reliable under ill conditioning, while iterative methods are more sensitive to spectral properties. The results suggest that ELM stability is fundamentally governed by the singular value structure of the hidden layer matrix.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于伪逆的极限学习机的谱稳定性
极限学习机（ELM）通过Moore-Penrose伪逆解析计算输出权重，虽训练速度快，但其数值稳定性高度依赖隐藏层矩阵的条件数。本文从谱视角研究伪逆型ELM，表明最小奇异值控制输出权重的扰动放大，而条件数定量反映隐藏层的不稳定性。我们比较了基于SVD的伪逆计算与迭代超幂方法，并通过随机特征解释讨论了宽度相关的条件性。在合成矩阵和ELM基准实验中，SVD方法在病态条件下仍最可靠，而迭代方法对谱特性更敏感。结果表明，ELM的稳定性根本上由隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构决定。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates the spectral stability of pseudoinverse-based Extreme Learning Machines, motivated by the observation that their numerical reliability hinges on the conditioning of the hidden layer matrix. The authors analyze the system through singular value decomposition, demonstrating that the smallest singular value dictates output weight perturbation amplification and that the condition number quantifies hidden-layer instability. They compare SVD-based pseudoinverse computation with iterative hyperpower methods, revealing that SVD remains more robust under ill-conditioned scenarios, while iterative methods exhibit greater sensitivity to spectral properties. Experimental results on synthetic and ELM benchmarks confirm that spectral structure of the hidden layer matrix fundamentally governs ELM stability.
为了解决基于伪逆的极限学习机（ELM）因隐藏层矩阵病态条件导致的数值不稳定问题，本文从谱视角研究其稳定性。研究表明，最小奇异值决定输出权重的扰动放大程度，而条件数量化隐藏层的不稳定性。通过对比SVD伪逆计算与迭代超幂方法，发现SVD在病态条件下更稳健，而迭代方法对谱特性更敏感。在合成矩阵和ELM基准实验中，结果表明隐藏层矩阵的奇异值结构从根本上决定了ELM的稳定性。
ImputeViz: A Visual Analytics Dashboard for Diagnosing Missing Data and Comparing Imputation Methods
Authors: Aitik Dandapat, Lalith Punepalle Raveendrareddy, Mithilesh Kumar Singh, Klaus Mueller
First: 2026-07-09T15:14:56+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:14:56+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Missing data is a persistent obstacle in scientific, social science, and public health research, often biasing analyses and placing accountability on analysts for how they handle missing values. We introduce ImputeViz, an integrated visual analytics dashboard that supports diagnosing missingness, configuring imputation models, and evaluating results. The system brings together widely used methods, including MICE, Random Forest, XGBoost, and kNN, within an interactive environment that makes missingness patterns explicit. To support geospatial reasoning, we introduce gKNN, a geographically informed kNN variant that blends socioeconomic and spatial distances and exposes donor contributions, enabling provenance-based visual accountability by showing which regions drive each estimate. Our primary contribution is a method-agnostic visual analytics environment that makes cross-method comparison a first-class visual task and integrates gKNN alongside standard methods. Coordinated views reveal missingness structure through heatmaps, co-missingness summaries, and distributional diagnostics that help analysts reason about missingness patterns (MCAR/MAR) and cases where missingness may be non-random (MNAR). Users can compare and tune models and interrogate results via distributional overlays, a Method Comparison Summary reporting MAE, RMSE, Delta RMSE, and runtime for each algorithm on the current target and mask, along with variable-level discrepancy views. Cached per-method results and locked axis scales reduce cognitive overhead from shifting ranges during method switching. These comparisons highlight where methods disagree, which variables are sensitive, and how imputation choices affect downstream summaries. Case studies demonstrate how ImputeViz helps analysts select effective strategies, surface sensitive variables, and assess model robustness.
中文标题/摘要
标题：ImputeViz：用于诊断缺失数据并比较插补方法的可视化分析仪表板
缺失数据是科学、社会科学和公共卫生研究中持续存在的障碍，常导致分析偏差，并将处理缺失值的责任推给分析师。我们提出了ImputeViz，一个集成的可视化分析仪表板，支持诊断缺失性、配置插补模型及评估结果。该系统将MICE、随机森林、XGBoost和kNN等常用方法整合于交互式环境中，使缺失模式显性化。为支持地理空间推理，我们引入了gKNN——一种结合社会经济与空间距离的地理感知kNN变体，并揭示供体贡献，通过显示哪些区域驱动每个估计值，实现基于溯源的可视化问责。我们的主要贡献是一种方法无关的可视化分析环境，使跨方法比较成为首要可视化任务，并将gKNN与标准方法集成。协调视图通过热图、共缺失性摘要和分布诊断揭示缺失结构，帮助分析师推断缺失模式（MCAR/MAR）及可能非随机缺失（MNAR）的情况。用户可通过分布叠加比较和调整模型，并通过方法比较摘要（报告每种算法在当前目标和掩码下的MAE、RMSE、Delta RMSE和运行时间）及变量级差异视图审查结果。每种方法缓存的结果和锁定的轴刻度减少了方法切换时范围变化带来的认知负担。这些比较突出了方法间的分歧、敏感变量及插补选择对下游摘要的影响。案例研究展示了ImputeViz如何帮助分析师选择有效策略、揭示敏感变量并评估模型稳健性。
Summary / 总结
Driven by the need to address biased analyses caused by missing data in scientific and public health research, ImputeViz offers a visual analytics dashboard that enables users to diagnose missingness patterns, configure imputation models, and compare results across methods like MICE, Random Forest, XGBoost, and kNN. The system introduces gKNN, a geographically informed kNN variant that combines socioeconomic and spatial distances to enhance accountability by revealing donor region contributions. Through coordinated views including heatmaps, co-missingness summaries, and distributional diagnostics, users can assess whether missingness is MCAR, MAR, or MNAR. The dashboard supports side-by-side model comparison via distributional overlays and a Method Comparison Summary that reports MAE, RMSE, Delta RMSE, and runtime, along with variable-level discrepancy views. Cached results and locked axis scales reduce cognitive load during method switching, helping analysts identify method disagreements, sensitive variables, and the impact of imputation choices on downstream summaries, as demonstrated in case studies.
为应对缺失数据对研究结果的偏倚问题，ImputeViz 提供了一个可视化分析仪表板，支持用户诊断缺失模式、配置插补模型并比较不同方法（如 MICE、随机森林、XGBoost 和 kNN）的结果。系统引入了 gKNN，一种结合社会经济与空间距离的地理感知 kNN 方法，通过揭示数据来源区域增强问责性。界面通过热图、分布诊断等联动视图帮助用户识别 MCAR/MAR/MNAR 缺失模式，并支持通过分布叠加、方法对比摘要（MAE、RMSE、Delta RMSE、运行时）和变量级差异视图进行模型比较。缓存结果与固定坐标轴降低方法切换的认知负担，帮助用户发现方法分歧、敏感变量及插补选择对下游分析的影响，案例研究验证了其在策略选择、敏感变量识别和模型稳健性评估中的有效性。
Phase-Preserving Trimodal Transformer for Tropical Forest Biomass Estimation Using Optical and PolInSAR Data
Authors: Luiz Felipe Parente Santiago, Felipe Ferrari, Daniel Rodrigues dos Santos, Rosiane de Freitas
First: 2026-07-04T02:32:51+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:14:29+00:00
Comments: 10 pages, 7 figures, one of which is a TikZ
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The accurate estimation of Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) in mature tropical forests remains a critical challenge in remote sensing, primarily due to the saturation of Synthetic Aperture Radar (SAR) signals in high-density areas and persistent cloud cover affecting optical imagery. To overcome these physical limitations, we propose the Trimodal Coherent Co-attention Transformer (TCCT), a physics-informed deep learning architecture. The TCCT natively fuses optical surface reflectance (Landsat-5) with complex-valued Polarimetric SAR Interferometry (PolInSAR) data from both P and L bands. Unlike traditional fusion methods, our architecture employs complex-valued encoders to preserve spatial phase coherence, coupled with a dynamic co-attention mechanism that acts as an adaptive gating module, reducing the weight of cloud-corrupted optical pixels and shifting reliance to microwave phase data. We also derived a localized spatial allometric calibration model via Levenberg-Marquardt optimization, tailored to the specific wood density of the Paracou region in the Amazon basin. Evaluated using a two-stage protocol, the TCCT first underwent a rigorous 5-fold cross-validation to establish robust global weights (achieving a global RMSE of 4.19 m). Subsequently, following a localized spatial fine-tuning phase over 200 epochs, the model attained an absolute RMSE of 3.78 m and an $R^2$ of 0.33 for Canopy Height Models (CHM), outperforming standard Random Forest, CNN, and Vision Transformer baselines. Our ablation study confirms that preserving phase coherence mitigates deep-canopy signal saturation. When converted to AGB, the fine-tuned TCCT map yielded a Relative RMSE (rRMSE) of 4.51% in dense forest areas above 50 Mg/ha. By meeting the European Space Agency (ESA) BIOMASS mission requirement of less than 20% error, the TCCT provides a robust framework for continuous carbon stock mapping in tropical biomes.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于光学与极化干涉SAR数据的相位保持三模态变换器用于热带森林生物量估算
准确估算成熟热带森林的地上生物量（AGB）仍是遥感领域的重要挑战，主要由于高密度区域合成孔径雷达（SAR）信号饱和及持续云层遮挡影响光学影像。为克服这些物理限制，我们提出了一种物理信息驱动的深度学习架构——三模态相干共注意力变换器（TCCT）。TCCT原生融合光学表面反射率（Landsat-5）与P和L波段的复值极化干涉SAR（PolInSAR）数据。与传统融合方法不同，该架构采用复值编码器以保持空间相位相干性，并结合动态共注意力机制作为自适应门控模块，降低云污染光学像素权重，转而依赖微波相位数据。我们还通过Levenberg-Marquardt优化推导出针对亚马逊流域Paracou区域特定木材密度的局部空间形态学校准模型。经两阶段评估，TCCT首先通过五折交叉验证建立稳健全局权重（全局RMSE为4.19 m），随后在200个周期的局部空间微调阶段，模型在冠层高度模型（CHM）上达到绝对RMSE 3.78 m和R² 0.33，优于标准随机森林、CNN和视觉变换器基线模型。消融研究表明，保持相位相干性可缓解深冠层信号饱和。当转换为AGB时，微调后的TCCT地图在密度超过50 Mg/ha的森林区域获得相对RMSE（rRMSE）4.51%，满足欧洲航天局（ESA）BIOMASS任务小于20%误差的要求，为热带生物群落持续碳储量测绘提供稳健框架。
Summary / 总结
To address the challenge of accurately estimating Above-Ground Biomass (AGB) in tropical forests—where SAR signal saturation and cloud cover hinder remote sensing—this study introduces the Trimodal Coherent Co-attention Transformer (TCCT), a physics-informed deep learning model that fuses optical Landsat-5 data with complex-valued PolInSAR data from P and L bands. The TCCT uses complex-valued encoders to preserve phase coherence and a dynamic co-attention mechanism to down-weight cloud-affected optical pixels, shifting reliance to microwave phase information. A localized spatial allometric model, optimized via Levenberg-Marquardt, was tailored to the Paracou region’s wood density. Evaluated through a two-stage protocol, the model achieved a global RMSE of 4.19 m in cross-validation and improved to 3.78 m RMSE and R² of 0.33 for Canopy Height Models after fine-tuning. When converted to AGB, it delivered a relative RMSE of 4.51% in dense forests (>50 Mg/ha), meeting the ESA BIOMASS mission’s 20% error threshold.
为解决热带森林中因SAR信号饱和和云层遮挡导致的地上生物量（AGB）估算难题，作者提出了一种物理信息驱动的深度学习模型——三模态相干共注意力变换器（TCCT），该模型融合了光学Landsat-5数据与P、L波段的复值极化干涉SAR数据。TCCT通过复值编码器保持空间相位相干性，并利用动态共注意力机制降低云污染光学像素权重，转而依赖微波相位信息。模型结合了针对亚马逊流域帕拉库地区木材密度定制的局部空间所有ometric校准模型，通过Levenberg-Marquardt优化实现。经两阶段评估，交叉验证阶段全局RMSE为4.19米，精细化训练后RMSE降至3.78米，CHM的R²达0.33，优于随机森林、CNN和视觉变换器基线模型。转换为AGB后，在密度大于50 Mg/ha的森林区域，相对RMSE为4.51%，满足欧洲航天局BIOMASS任务<20%误差要求。
GrowFields: Compositional 4D Neural Fields for Topology-Changing Plant Growth
Authors: Joaquin Gajardo, Michele Volpi, Marko Mihajlovic, Siyu Tang, Lukas Roth, Sergey Prokudin
Venue: ECCV 2026
First: 2026-07-03T13:48:58+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:11:22+00:00
Comments: ECCV 2026 paper (main conference). v2 corrects the vertical guide lines in supplementary Figures 5-7. Project page and code available at https://joaquin-gajardo.github.io/growfields/
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Project1
Abstract
Quantifying plant growth dynamics from sparse longitudinal 3D observations is fundamental for agriculture and plant sciences. Yet, plants pose unique challenges: they undergo intricate non-rigid deformations, exhibit changing topology as new organs emerge, and often lack explicit temporal correspondences between consecutive data acquisitions due to newly formed tissue. Methods designed for general scenes struggle to model topology changes and asynchronous organ growth characteristic of plants. To address these challenges, we introduce GrowFields, a compositional dynamic neural field representation for organ-aware 4D plant growth modelling from point cloud time series. Our approach decomposes a plant into its constituent organs and aligns each organ into its own canonical coordinate frame, isolating intrinsic growth patterns from global plant motion. We then learn a shared continuous neural deformation field that models temporal dynamics across all organs, conditioned on learnable per-organ latent codes capturing organ identity and growth characteristics. The resulting modular yet unified representation naturally accommodates the asynchronous development of plant organs while remaining grounded in the practical setting of organ-level plant tracking. We evaluate GrowFields on growth sequences from four plant species, assessing geometric fitting and organ tracking accuracy using manually annotated leaf-tip trajectories. Results demonstrate consistent improvements in spatial precision, temporal coherence, and morphological fidelity over a range of existing representations.
中文标题/摘要
标题：GrowFields：用于拓扑变化植物生长的组合式4D神经场
从稀疏的纵向3D观测数据中量化植物生长动力学，对农业和植物科学至关重要。然而，植物具有独特挑战：它们经历复杂的非刚性形变，随着新器官出现而拓扑结构变化，且由于新形成的组织，连续数据采集间常缺乏明确的时间对应关系。为一般场景设计的方法难以建模植物特有的拓扑变化和异步器官生长。为应对这些挑战，我们提出GrowFields，一种基于点云时间序列的器官感知4D植物生长建模的组合式动态神经场表示方法。我们的方法将植物分解为其组成器官，并将每个器官对齐至其自身的规范坐标系，从而将器官的内在生长模式与全局植物运动分离。随后，我们学习一个共享的连续神经形变场，该场在所有器官间建模时间动态，条件于可学习的每个器官潜在编码，以捕捉器官身份和生长特征。所得到的模块化但统一的表示自然适应植物器官异步发育，同时扎根于器官级植物跟踪的实际场景。我们在四种植物的生长序列上评估GrowFields，通过人工标注的叶尖轨迹评估几何拟合和器官跟踪精度。结果表明，相比现有多种表示方法，GrowFields在空间精度、时间连贯性和形态保真度方面均取得一致提升。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to model plant growth from sparse 3D time-series data while handling topology changes and asynchronous organ development, GrowFields introduces a compositional 4D neural field framework that decomposes plants into individual organs, each aligned to its own canonical frame. It learns a shared neural deformation field conditioned on per-organ latent codes to capture intrinsic growth patterns while decoupling them from global motion. Evaluated across four plant species using manually annotated leaf-tip trajectories, GrowFields consistently outperforms existing methods in spatial precision, temporal coherence, and morphological fidelity, demonstrating its effectiveness in organ-aware plant growth modeling.
为了解决从稀疏3D时序数据中建模植物生长时面临的拓扑变化和器官异步发育问题，GrowFields提出了一种组合式4D神经场框架，将植物分解为独立器官并为其建立规范坐标系。该方法学习一个共享的神经形变场，其受每个器官的可学习潜在编码条件约束，以捕捉器官的内在生长模式并分离全局运动。在四种植物的生长序列上，通过人工标注的叶尖轨迹评估，GrowFields在空间精度、时间连贯性和形态保真度上均显著优于现有方法，验证了其在器官感知植物生长建模中的有效性。
FabriVLA: A Lightweight Vision-Language-Action Model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation
Authors: Shiyuan Yang, Borong Zhang, Jizheng Zhang, Zhijia Tao, Junfei Guo, Donglai Ran, Xu Bian, Qingbiao Li
First: 2026-07-09T15:06:43+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:06:43+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
We present FabriVLA, a lightweight Vision-Language-Action model for Precise Multi-Task Manipulation. FabriVLA combines an InternVL3.5 vision-language backbone with a flow-matching action head featuring gated self-attention across action tokens and shallow VLM layer fusion for enriched spatial context. The model is trained via single stage joint optimization from a pretrained VLM and randomly initialized action head. On the Meta-World MT50 benchmark spanning 50 diverse manipulation tasks, FabriVLA achieves a tier-average success rate of 90.0%, demonstrating that a compact VLA built on a 1B scale VLM can achieve strong performance without relying on multi billion parameter VLA backbones.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FabriVLA：一种轻量级视觉-语言-动作模型，用于精确多任务操作
我们提出了FabriVLA，一种轻量级视觉-语言-动作模型，用于精确多任务操作。FabriVLA结合了InternVL3.5视觉-语言骨干网络与一个流匹配动作头，该动作头具有跨动作令牌的门控自注意力机制和浅层VLM层融合，以增强空间上下文。该模型通过从预训练VLM和随机初始化的动作头进行单阶段联合优化训练。在涵盖50种多样化操作任务的Meta-World MT50基准测试中，FabriVLA实现了90.0%的分层平均成功率，证明了基于10亿规模VLM构建的紧凑型VLA无需依赖多十亿参数的VLA骨干网络即可取得优异性能。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for efficient, high-performance manipulation models without relying on large-scale VLA architectures, FabriVLA integrates a lightweight InternVL3.5 vision-language backbone with a novel flow-matching action head that uses gated self-attention and shallow VLM layer fusion to enhance spatial context. Trained via joint optimization from a pretrained VLM and randomly initialized action head, the model achieves a 90.0% tier-average success rate on the Meta-World MT50 benchmark, showcasing that a compact 1B-scale VLA can match or exceed performance of larger models without requiring multi-billion parameter architectures.
为实现复杂操作任务中的高效高精度视觉-语言-动作模型，FabriVLA结合了轻量级InternVL3.5视觉语言骨干网络与基于流匹配的动作头，该动作头采用门控自注意力机制和浅层VLM层融合以增强空间上下文理解。模型通过单阶段联合优化从预训练VLM和随机初始化动作头训练而成，在Meta-World MT50基准测试中达到90.0%的分层平均成功率，证明了仅需10亿参数规模的VLA即可媲美更大规模模型的性能，无需依赖多十亿参数架构。
SHAP-Weighted Cross-Modal Expert Fusion for Emotion and Sentiment Recognition: Evidence and Limits
Authors: Adis Alihodzic, Selma Skopljakovic Hubljar
First: 2026-07-09T15:06:28+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:06:28+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition is commonly addressed by early fusion, which concatenates modalities before classification, or late fusion, which combines independently trained unimodal predictors. Early fusion can be accurate but monolithic, while late fusion is modular but may lose cross-modal interactions. This paper revisits XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), a tree-based mixture of unimodal and cross-modal experts whose sample-level weights are derived from TreeSHAP attribution magnitudes. We focus on the effect of SHAP attribution reduction when experts have unequal feature dimensionalities. In this setting, mean-abs and median-abs reductions can suppress high-dimensional cross-modal experts, whereas sum-abs reduction preserves total attribution mass. On MELD 7-class emotion recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion across three face-sequence aggregators; the Transformer variant reaches 0.5983 \wf{}, compared with 0.6018 for early fusion and 0.4598 for probability-average late fusion. McNemar testing shows no significant difference between sum-abs \xgaf{} and early fusion on MELD ($p=1.000$), while \xgaf{} remains significantly better than late fusion ($p<0.0001$). On CMU-MOSEI 3-class sentiment recognition, sum-abs \xgaf{} reaches 0.6519 \wf{}, slightly exceeding early fusion (0.6485) and late fusion (0.5696). Ablation studies show that the main gain comes from adding cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal expert, rather than from complex per-sample routing. Diagnostics further show that mean-abs and median-abs weights are nearly uniform, while sum-abs weights concentrate on the trimodal expert. Thus, the main contribution is a transparent empirical analysis of how SHAP reduction, expert dimensionality, and cross-modal expert design affect modular multimodal fusion.
中文标题/摘要
标题：基于SHAP加权的跨模态专家融合用于情绪与情感识别：证据与局限
多模态情绪与情感识别通常采用早期融合（分类前拼接模态）或晚期融合（独立训练单模态预测器后组合）。早期融合准确但结构单一，晚期融合模块化但可能丢失跨模态交互。本文重新审视了XAI引导的自适应融合（\xgaf），即基于树结构的单模态与跨模态专家混合，其样本级权重由TreeSHAP归因幅度导出。我们关注专家特征维度不等时SHAP归因缩减的影响：均值绝对值与中位数绝对值缩减会抑制高维跨模态专家，而总和绝对值缩减则保留总归因质量。在MELD 7类情绪识别中，总和绝对值\xgaf{}在三种面部序列聚合器下几乎匹配早期融合；Transformer变体达到0.5983 \wf{}，优于早期融合（0.6018）和概率平均晚期融合（0.4598）。McNemar检验显示，总和绝对值\xgaf{}与早期融合在MELD上无显著差异（p=1.000），但显著优于晚期融合（p<0.0001）。在CMU-MOSEI 3类情感识别中，总和绝对值\xgaf{}达到0.6519 \wf{}，略超早期融合（0.6485）和晚期融合（0.5696）。消融研究表明，主要增益来自添加跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家），而非复杂样本路由。诊断显示，均值与中位数绝对值权重近似均匀，而总和绝对值权重集中于三模态专家。因此，主要贡献是透明实证分析SHAP缩减、专家维度与跨模态专家设计如何影响模块化多模态融合。
Summary / 总结
This paper addresses the challenge of multimodal emotion and sentiment recognition by revisiting XAI-guided adaptive fusion (\xgaf), which combines unimodal and cross-modal experts using TreeSHAP-derived weights. The study investigates how different SHAP attribution reduction methods—mean-abs, median-abs, and sum-abs—affect performance when experts have unequal feature dimensions, finding that sum-abs preserves attribution mass and avoids suppressing high-dimensional cross-modal experts. Experiments on MELD and CMU-MOSEI datasets show that sum-abs \xgaf{} nearly matches early fusion (0.5983 vs. 0.6018 on MELD) and outperforms late fusion (0.4598 on MELD, 0.5696 on CMU-MOSEI), with McNemar tests confirming statistical significance. Ablation studies reveal that the primary benefit stems from cross-modal experts, especially the trimodal one, rather than complex routing, and diagnostics confirm sum-abs weights concentrate on this expert.
该论文针对多模态情绪与情感识别问题，重新审视了基于XAI引导的自适应融合（\xgaf）方法，该方法通过TreeSHAP归因值为不同模态专家分配样本级权重。研究发现，当专家特征维度不同时，mean-abs和median-abs会抑制高维跨模态专家，而sum-abs能保持归因总量，效果更优。在MELD数据集上，sum-abs \xgaf{}达到0.5983 \wf{}，接近早期融合（0.6018），显著优于晚期融合（0.4598），McNemar检验显示其与早期融合无显著差异（p=1.000）。在CMU-MOSEI数据集上，sum-abs \xgaf{}达到0.6519 \wf{}，略胜早期融合（0.6485）和晚期融合（0.5696）。消融实验表明，跨模态专家（尤其是三模态专家）是性能提升的主要来源，而非复杂的样本路由机制，诊断结果也显示sum-abs权重集中于三模态专家。
DeepTutor: Towards Agentic Personalized Tutoring
Authors: Bingxi Zhao, Jiahao Zhang, Xubin Ren, Zirui Guo, Tianzhe Chu, Yi Ma, Chao Huang
First: 2026-04-10T17:57:00+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:04:21+00:00
Comments: Tech Report, work in progress. Code available at https://github.com/HKUDS/DeepTutor
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Education is one of the most promising real-world applications for Large Language Models (LLMs). However, current LLMs rely on static pre-training knowledge and lack adaptation to individual learners, while existing RAG systems fall short in delivering personalized, guided feedback. To bridge this gap, we present DeepTutor, a fully open-source agentic framework that unifies citation-grounded problem tutoring with difficulty-calibrated question generation. A hybrid personalization engine couples static knowledge grounding with dynamic learner memory, continuously adapting each interaction to the student's evolving needs. The same personalization substrate further extends to adaptive learning workflows, interactive books, and proactive multi-channel tutoring agents. To evaluate personalized tutoring, we introduce TutorBench, an interactive benchmark incorporating customized learner profiles grounded in university-level curricula across five domains. We further propose an LLM-based first-person interactive evaluation protocol that conducts assessments via a profile-driven student simulator. Complementary evaluations on established benchmarks, supported by human-alignment and ablation studies, confirm the framework's robustness and general utility. Results show that DeepTutor improves personalized metrics by 10.8\% on average and strengthens general agentic reasoning across five backbone models by 29.4\%.
中文标题/摘要
标题：DeepTutor：迈向自主个性化辅导
教育是大型语言模型（LLMs）最具前景的现实应用之一。然而，当前LLMs依赖静态预训练知识，缺乏对个体学习者的适应能力，而现有RAG系统在提供个性化、引导式反馈方面表现不足。为弥合这一差距，我们提出DeepTutor，一个完全开源的自主框架，将基于引用的问题辅导与难度校准的题目生成统一起来。混合个性化引擎结合静态知识锚定与动态学习者记忆，持续根据学生不断变化的需求调整每次交互。同一个性化基础进一步扩展至自适应学习工作流、互动书籍和主动多通道辅导代理。为评估个性化辅导，我们引入TutorBench，一个结合大学课程五大领域定制学习者档案的交互式基准。我们进一步提出一种基于LLM的第一人称交互式评估协议，通过档案驱动的学生模拟器进行评估。在现有基准上的互补评估，辅以人类对齐和消融研究，证实了该框架的稳健性和通用性。结果表明，DeepTutor平均提升个性化指标10.8%，并在五个基础模型上增强通用自主推理能力29.4%。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for personalized, adaptive tutoring powered by LLMs, DeepTutor introduces an agentic framework that combines citation-grounded problem tutoring with difficulty-calibrated question generation, using a hybrid personalization engine that integrates static knowledge with dynamic learner memory. Evaluated via TutorBench—a benchmark with university-level curricula across five domains and a student simulator for first-person assessment—DeepTutor demonstrates a 10.8% average improvement in personalized metrics and a 29.4% boost in agentic reasoning across five backbone models, validated through human-alignment and ablation studies.
为解决当前大语言模型缺乏个性化适应能力的问题，DeepTutor提出一个结合引文支撑的习题辅导与难度自适应题目生成的代理框架，其混合个性化引擎融合静态知识与动态学习者记忆，持续调整交互以匹配学生需求。通过TutorBench基准（涵盖五大学科的大学课程及基于学生档案的模拟评估），实验显示其个性化指标平均提升10.8%，且在五种基础模型上强化了通用代理推理能力29.4%，并通过人工对齐与消融实验验证了框架的稳健性与普适性。
Switch-Reasoner: Learn When to Think in Multitask Mixtures via Reinforcement Learning
Authors: Yiyang Fang, Pei Fu, Jinjie Li, Jian Liang, Wenke Huang, Ruijie Luo, Shaojie Zhang, Jian Luan, Yi R. Fung, Mang Ye
First: 2026-07-09T15:04:05+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:04:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) often follow a fixed Think-then-Answer paradigm, which is inefficient in heterogeneous multitask settings because simple inputs may not require explicit reasoning while difficult ones can benefit substantially from it. Learning when to think is also unstable during post-training, where imbalanced rollouts can drive the model toward always-thinking or always-direct behavior. We propose Switch-Reasoner, a GRPO-based framework that learns to adaptively select reasoning modes for MLLMs. It treats thinking as a virtual tool invocation and allows the model to either answer directly or invoke explicit reasoning before answering. To stabilize this decision, we introduce a dual-level regulation mechanism that balances the overall use of Thinking Mode and Direct Mode while providing sample-level supervision based on the relative benefit of the two choices. Experiments on 11 multimodal tasks show that Switch-Reasoner reduces unnecessary reasoning while maintaining strong performance, achieving a better accuracy-efficiency trade-off.
中文标题/摘要
标题：Switch-Reasoner：通过强化学习学习在多任务混合环境中何时进行推理
多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）通常遵循固定的“先思考再回答”范式，在异构多任务设置中效率低下，因为简单输入可能无需显式推理，而困难输入则能显著受益于推理。在微调后，学习何时推理也存在不稳定问题，不平衡的 rollout 会驱动模型趋向于始终推理或始终直接回答。我们提出 Switch-Reasoner，一种基于 GRPO 的框架，用于学习 MLLMs 适应性选择推理模式。它将推理视为虚拟工具调用，允许模型直接回答或在回答前调用显式推理。为稳定该决策，我们引入双层调节机制，在整体平衡推理模式与直接模式使用的同时，基于两种选择的相对收益提供样本级监督。在 11 个多模态任务上的实验表明，Switch-Reasoner 减少了不必要的推理，同时保持了强性能，实现了更好的准确率-效率权衡。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of fixed reasoning in heterogeneous multitask settings for Multimodal Large Language Models, Switch-Reasoner introduces a GRPO-based reinforcement learning framework that learns to dynamically choose between direct answering and explicit reasoning. It treats reasoning as a virtual tool invocation and employs a dual-level regulation mechanism to balance usage across modes while providing per-sample supervision based on relative benefit. Experimental results across 11 multimodal tasks demonstrate that this approach reduces unnecessary reasoning without sacrificing performance, achieving a superior accuracy-efficiency trade-off.
为了解决多模态大语言模型在异构多任务场景中固定推理模式的低效问题，Switch-Reasoner 提出一种基于 GRPO 的强化学习框架，使模型能动态选择直接回答或显式推理。它将推理视为虚拟工具调用，并通过双层调节机制平衡两种模式的使用，同时基于样本相对收益提供细粒度监督。在 11 个多模态任务上的实验表明，该方法减少了不必要的推理，同时保持性能，实现了更优的准确率-效率权衡。
FunHOI: Annotation-Free 3D Hand-Object Interaction Generation via Functional Text Guidance
Authors: Yongqi Tian, Xueyu Sun, Haoyuan He, jianlei Wang, Caigui Jiang
First: 2025-02-28T07:42:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T15:00:58+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Hand-object interaction(HOI) is the fundamental link between human and environment, yet its dexterous and complex pose significantly challenges for gesture control. Despite significant advances in AI and robotics, enabling machines to understand and simulate hand-object interactions, capturing the semantics of functional grasping tasks remains a considerable challenge. While previous work can generate stable and correct 3D grasps, they are still far from achieving functional grasps due to unconsidered grasp semantics. To address this challenge, we propose an innovative two-stage framework, Functional Grasp Synthesis Net (FGS-Net), for generating 3D HOI driven by functional text. This framework consists of a text-guided 3D model generator, Functional Grasp Generator (FGG), and a pose optimization strategy, Functional Grasp Refiner (FGR). FGG generates 3D models of hands and objects based on text input, while FGR fine-tunes the poses using Object Pose Approximator and energy functions to ensure the relative position between the hand and object aligns with human intent and remains physically plausible. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our approach achieves precise and high-quality HOI generation without requiring additional 3D annotation data.
中文标题/摘要
标题：FunHOI：通过功能文本引导实现无需标注的3D手-物体交互生成
手-物体交互（HOI）是人与环境之间的基本纽带，但其灵巧且复杂的姿态对手势控制提出了重大挑战。尽管人工智能和机器人领域取得了显著进展，使机器能够理解和模拟手-物体交互，但捕捉功能抓取任务的语义仍是一个重大挑战。虽然先前工作可以生成稳定且正确的3D抓取，但由于未考虑抓取语义，仍远未实现功能抓取。为应对这一挑战，我们提出了一种创新的两阶段框架——功能抓取合成网络（FGS-Net），通过功能文本驱动3D HOI生成。该框架包括一个文本引导的3D模型生成器——功能抓取生成器（FGG），以及一个姿态优化策略——功能抓取精调器（FGR）。FGG根据文本输入生成手和物体的3D模型，而FGR利用物体姿态近似器和能量函数对姿态进行微调，确保手与物体的相对位置符合人类意图且物理上合理。大量实验表明，我们的方法无需额外的3D标注数据即可实现精确且高质量的HOI生成。

Motivated by the need to generate functionally meaningful 3D hand-object interactions without relying on annotated data, the authors propose FGS-Net, a two-stage framework that uses functional text as guidance. The first stage, Functional Grasp Generator, creates 3D hand and object models from text descriptions, while the second stage, Functional Grasp Refiner, optimizes hand poses using object pose approximation and energy-based constraints to ensure physical plausibility and semantic alignment with human intent. Computation, Condensation, and the Incompleteness Between Them: A Coupled Foundation of Intelligence
Authors: Xin Li
First: 2023-03-07T19:47:01+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:58:05+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The theory of computation was built to answer Turing's question: what is effectively calculable by an unbounded, immortal, disembodied agent following rules? Intelligence answers a different question (nature's): what can a \emph{finite}, mortal, energy-limited agent do quickly enough to survive in a non-stationary world? We argue that a complete answer requires two operators: \emph{computation} and \emph{memorizaion}. Computation, $\dpar$, transforms structure toward closure; memorization, $\kap$, condenses a validated closed cycle into a reusable token. Turing formalized $\dpar$ and abstracted $\kap$ away, because his agent had infinite time and never needed to amortize. The new insight of our position paper is that the coupling of the two is not optional but \emph{forced}, and forced by a precise mathematical fact: \textbf{neither operator alone can be complete}. We prove that symbolic computation confined to a discrete sector suffers Gödel's diagonalization incompleteness, that geometric descent confined to a continuous sector suffers a Morse forced-saddle incompleteness, and that these two are not analogies but the parity-conjugate faces of a single obstruction on a coherent complex with $\dpar^2=0$ - the even face realized by diagonalization, the odd by the topologically forced saddle, and no resolution confined to one parity able to come full circle. Intelligence must therefore couple both modes. We then locate the price of the coupling: its hinge operation, context-identification (the recognize-versus-discover decision), is exactly where the two incompletenesses coincide, hence undecidable and carrying an irreducible error floor. Finally we argue that the coupling is a universal law, realized, in the emergent sense of Anderson's ``more is different,'' at every scale from genes to thoughts to cultures, and give its falsifiable core and honest scope.
中文标题/摘要
标题：计算、凝缩与二者之间的不完备性：智能的耦合基础
计算理论旨在回答图灵的问题：一个无界、不朽、无身体且遵循规则的代理能有效计算什么？而智能则回答一个不同的问题（自然的问题）：一个有限、有寿命、能量受限的代理，如何在非平稳世界中快速行动以求生存？我们主张，完整答案需要两个操作符：计算（\dpar）和记忆化（\kap）。计算\dpar将结构向闭合性转化；记忆化\kap将验证过的闭合循环凝缩为可复用的令牌。图灵形式化了\dpar，而抽象化了\kap，因为他的代理拥有无限时间且无需摊销。我们论文的新见解是，这两个操作符的耦合并非可选，而是被精确的数学事实所强制：\textbf{单个操作符均无法完备}。我们证明，仅限离散领域的符号计算会遭遇哥德尔对角线化不完备性，仅限连续领域的几何下降会遭遇莫尔斯强制鞍点不完备性，而这两者并非类比，而是在一个具有\dpar^2=0的连贯复形上的同一障碍的奇偶共轭面——偶面由对角线化实现，奇面由拓扑强制鞍点实现，且无任何局限于单一奇偶性的解决方案能完整闭环。因此，智能必须耦合这两种模式。接着我们指出耦合的成本：其铰接操作——上下文识别（识别与发现的抉择）——正是两个不完备性交汇之处，因而不可判定且携带不可约误差下限。最后我们主张，这种耦合是普适定律，在安德森“更多即不同”的涌现意义上，从基因到思想再到文化，每一尺度均实现该定律，并给出其可证伪的核心与诚实范围。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the gap between Turing’s idealized computation and real-world intelligence, this paper argues that true intelligence requires coupling two operators: computation (dpar), which transforms structure toward closure, and memorization (kap), which condenses validated cycles into reusable tokens. The authors prove that neither operator alone is sufficient, as symbolic computation suffers Gödelian incompleteness and geometric descent faces Morse saddle obstructions — both manifestations of a deeper mathematical obstruction on a coherent complex where dpar²=0. The coupling’s cost lies in context identification, an undecidable operation where the two incompletenesses intersect, creating an irreducible error floor. The paper concludes that this coupling is a universal law, emerging across scales from genes to cultures, and proposes a falsifiable core for its scope.
本文旨在弥合图灵计算模型与真实智能之间的鸿沟，提出真正的智能必须耦合两种操作：计算（将结构向闭合转化）与记忆（将验证过的循环压缩为可复用的标记）。作者证明，仅靠任一操作均不完整：符号计算受哥德尔不完备性限制，几何下降则面临莫尔斯鞍点强制不完备性——二者实为同一拓扑障碍在偶数面（对角化）与奇数面（拓扑强制鞍点）上的对偶表现，且满足 $\dpar^2=0$ 的相干复形。耦合的代价在于上下文识别（识别与发现的抉择），该操作恰是两种不完备性交汇处，因而不可判定并携带不可消除的误差下限。文章最后主张此耦合是普适法则，从基因到思想再到文化均以安德森“更多即不同”的涌现方式实现，并提出其可证伪的核心与真实适用范围。
TouchWorld: A Predictive and Reactive Tactile Foundation Model for Dexterous Manipulation
Authors: Jianyi Zhou, Feiyang Hong, Yunhao Li, Yicheng Zhao, Yongjue Cen, Zirui Liu, Jiakang Huang, Zirui Chen, Ruiyang Zhang, Weizhuo Zhu, Xuhua Song, Shuo Yang
First: 2026-07-08T11:28:13+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:55:30+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Dexterous manipulation in everyday environments requires both anticipation and reaction: a robot must predict how contact should evolve while rapidly correcting local errors caused by slip, misalignment, unstable grasping, or force mismatch. Vision and language provide semantic and geometric guidance, but they cannot reliably reveal hidden contact states such as force, slip, and contact stability. Although tactile sensing exposes these physical cues, most existing policies treat touch as a low-frequency observation stream within a monolithic action model, coupling slow task reasoning, action generation, and fast contact feedback in a single loop. We introduce TouchWorld, a predictive-and-reactive tactile foundation model for dexterous manipulation. TouchWorld uses a hierarchical policy that separates vision-language subtask planning, tactile world-model prediction, visuo-tactile goal-conditioned action generation, and high-frequency tactile residual refinement. A High-Level Planning Layer produces executable subtasks and predicts tactile subgoals; a Visuo-Tactile Goal-Conditioned Policy generates nominal action chunks; and a Tactile-Conditioned Refinement Policy performs online residual correction using recent tactile and proprioceptive feedback. By using touch as both a predictive contact reference and a fast feedback signal, TouchWorld preserves the semantic generalization of vision-language-action policies while improving local contact adaptation. Across six long-horizon and contact-rich dexterous manipulation tasks, TouchWorld achieves 65.0% success in the clean setting and 53.7% success under human perturbations, outperforming the strongest baseline by 15.7 and 18.5 percentage points, respectively.
中文标题/摘要
标题：TouchWorld：一种用于灵巧操作的预测与反应式触觉基础模型
在日常环境中实现灵巧操作需要预测与反应：机器人必须预测接触状态的演变，同时快速纠正由滑动、对齐错误、抓取不稳定或力不匹配引起的局部误差。视觉和语言提供语义和几何指导，但无法可靠揭示隐藏的接触状态，如力、滑动和接触稳定性。尽管触觉传感能暴露这些物理线索，但现有策略通常将触觉视为单一体系中低频观测流，将缓慢的任务推理、动作生成与快速接触反馈耦合在一个循环中。我们提出TouchWorld，一种用于灵巧操作的预测与反应式触觉基础模型。TouchWorld采用分层策略，将视觉-语言子任务规划、触觉世界模型预测、视觉-触觉目标条件动作生成和高频触觉残差优化分离。高层规划层生成可执行子任务并预测触觉子目标；视觉-触觉目标条件策略生成名义动作块；触觉条件优化策略利用近期触觉与本体感觉反馈进行在线残差修正。通过将触觉同时作为预测接触参考和快速反馈信号，TouchWorld在保持视觉-语言-动作策略语义泛化能力的同时，提升了局部接触适应性。在六个长时程且接触丰富的灵巧操作任务中，TouchWorld在干净环境下成功率达65.0%，在人类干扰下成功率达53.7%，分别比最强基线高出15.7和18.5个百分点。

TouchWorld introduces a hierarchical policy that decouples high-level vision-language planning, tactile world modeling, goal-conditioned action generation, and high-frequency tactile refinement. The model uses tactile feedback not only for correction but also as a predictive reference, enhancing local adaptation without sacrificing semantic generalization. In six contact-intensive manipulation tasks, TouchWorld achieved 65.0% success in clean conditions and 53.7% under human perturbations, outperforming the best baseline by 15.7 and 18.5 percentage points respectively.
为实现机器人在动态环境中进行灵巧操作，该研究旨在解决视觉和语言系统无法可靠捕捉力、滑移等隐性触觉状态的问题，从而需要同时具备接触预测与快速反应能力。TouchWorld提出一种分层策略，将高层视觉语言规划、触觉世界建模、视觉触觉引导的动作生成和高频触觉修正解耦。触觉不仅用于实时修正，还作为预测参考，使模型在保持语义泛化能力的同时提升局部接触适应性。在六个接触密集型操作任务中，TouchWorld在无干扰环境下成功率达65.0%，在人类干扰下为53.7%，分别比最强基线高出15.7和18.5个百分点。
SMetric: Rethink LLM Scheduling for Serving Agents with Balanced Session-centric Scheduling
Authors: Jiahao Wang, Kaizhan Lin, Kaixi Zhang, Jinbo Han, Xingda Wei, Sijie Shen, Chenguang Fang, Wenyuan Yu, Rong Chen, Haibo Chen
First: 2026-07-09T14:55:02+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:55:02+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
LLM scheduling is critical to serving, yet it remains unclear how well existing designs fit agentic serving--with LLM requests issued by agents instead of humans. This shifts the workload in two ways: (1) agents act only on complete responses, making the cluster's tokens per second (TPS) the primary goal and relaxing--not eliminating--per-token latency requirements; and (2) requests share much of their KV\$-reuse exceeds 80% of request tokens in a production trace from BAILIAN, versus 54-62% in chat. This paper first contributes a systematic study of request scheduling for agents on two real-world traces. We find that to increase KV\$ reuse, existing schedulers overly prioritize routing requests to instances caching their KV\$, overloading a few while leaving the rest idle, capping TPS. We thus present two key insights: (1) load balance need not sacrifice all KV\$ reuse, thanks to the global-tier KV\$ store and (2) by utilizing the workload's intra-session locality, balancing a small fraction of requests--the first request in each agent session--suffices to balance the cluster without sacrificing most KV\$ reuse on local instances. SMETRIC realizes these insights with balanced session-centric scheduling: it routes each session's first request purely for load balance and its follow-up requests in a cache-aware manner, preserving load balance and local reuse while keeping demand on the global tier low. Using the session turn information as the scheduling metric is deliberate: it is derived efficiently and accurately from the user inputs alone, so the scheduler stays clean and stateless. SMETRIC improves cluster TPS by 10-16% under prefill-decode colocation with a global store and prefill TPS by 2-34% under disaggregation over state-of-the-art schedulers, also with a better per-token latency.
中文标题/摘要
标题：SMetric：重新思考大语言模型服务代理的调度——平衡会话为中心的调度
大语言模型调度对服务至关重要，但现有设计在代理服务场景下的适用性尚不明确——代理而非人类发起LLM请求。这改变了工作负载的两个方面：(1) 代理仅在完整响应后行动，使集群每秒令牌数（TPS）成为主要目标，而非消除每令牌延迟要求；(2) 请求共享大量KV缓存，生产环境BAILIAN的追踪数据显示KV重用率超过80%，而聊天场景仅为54-62%。 本文首先对两个真实世界追踪数据中的代理请求调度进行系统性研究。我们发现，现有调度器为最大化KV缓存重用，过度优先将请求路由至缓存其KV的实例，导致少数实例过载而其余空闲，从而限制TPS。因此我们提出两个关键见解：(1) 由于全局层级KV缓存的存在，负载均衡无需牺牲所有KV缓存重用；(2) 利用工作负载的会话内局部性，仅平衡每个代理会话的第一个请求，即可在不牺牲本地实例大部分KV重用的前提下实现集群负载均衡。 SMETRIC通过平衡会话为中心的调度实现上述见解：它将每个会话的第一个请求纯用于负载均衡，后续请求则以缓存感知方式路由，同时保持负载均衡和本地重用，并降低对全局层级的需求。以会话轮次信息作为调度指标是刻意设计的：它仅从用户输入高效准确地推导得出，使调度器保持简洁且无状态。在预填充-解码共置且配备全局存储的场景下，SMETRIC较现有最先进调度器提升集群TPS 10-16%，在解耦场景下提升预填充TPS 2-34%，同时改善每令牌延迟。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the inefficiency of existing LLM schedulers in agentic serving, where agent requests exhibit high KV cache reuse and session-locality, this paper proposes SMETRIC, a session-centric scheduling method that balances load while preserving cache efficiency. It routes the first request of each agent session purely for load balancing, then subsequent requests cache-awarely to maintain local KV reuse, leveraging a global-tier cache to avoid overloading specific instances. Experiments on real-world traces show SMETRIC boosts cluster TPS by 10-16% under prefill-decode colocation and prefill TPS by 2-34% under disaggregation, while maintaining or improving per-token latency compared to state-of-the-art schedulers.
为解决现有LLM调度器在代理服务场景下的低效问题——代理请求具有高KV缓存复用率和会话中心特性——本文提出SMETRIC，一种平衡的会话中心调度方法。其将每个代理会话的第一个请求用于负载均衡，后续请求则按缓存感知方式调度，以保持本地KV复用和全局层效率。实验表明，SMETRIC在预填充-解码共置场景下提升集群TPS 10–16%，在解耦场景下提升预填充TPS 2–34%，同时保持或改善每令牌延迟表现。
Contravariance Theory: Strong Alignment for Minimal Solutions to Hard Tasks
Authors: Dan Yamins, Aran Nayebi
First: 2026-07-09T14:53:54+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:53:54+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
A series of results from the NeuroAI over the past fifteen years have raised core questions both about how to compare Deep Neural Network (DNN) models to the brain, and about how much convergent evolution to expect between artificial networks and real brain networks. Here, we show that for any two minimal DNN solutions to a sufficiently hard task: (i) "weak" alignment of network representations based on affine mappings guarantees "strong" alignment of privileged axes, and (ii) alignment "zippers" up the network hierarchy, causing the emergence of privileged axes from end-to-end task optimization. These results formalize the notion of contravariance from Cao and Yamins [2024], and illustrate important consequences for the theory of NeuroAI: with sufficiently strong tasks, choice of metric for inter-network comparison is not all that sensitive, and that convergent evolution is probably inevitable.
中文标题/摘要
标题：反变理论：为解决困难任务提供最小解的强对齐
过去十五年NeuroAI领域的一系列成果引发了关于如何比较深度神经网络（DNN）模型与大脑，以及人工网络与真实脑网络之间预期的趋同演化程度的核心问题。本文表明，对于任何两个解决足够困难任务的最小DNN解：(i) 基于仿射映射的网络表征“弱”对齐可保证“强”特权轴对齐；(ii) 对齐“拉链”贯穿网络层次结构，导致从端到端任务优化中涌现出特权轴。这些结果形式化了Cao和Yamins[2024]提出的反变概念，并阐明了对NeuroAI理论的重要影响：在足够强的任务下，网络间比较所选度量的敏感性较低，趋同演化可能是不可避免的。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by longstanding questions in NeuroAI about comparing DNNs to biological brains and predicting convergent evolution, this work formalizes contravariance theory to show that minimal DNN solutions to hard tasks exhibit strong alignment of privileged axes through weak alignment via affine mappings, and that this alignment propagates hierarchically across network layers due to end-to-end optimization. Experimental results demonstrate that under sufficiently hard tasks, the choice of metric for comparing networks becomes less sensitive, supporting the inevitability of convergent evolution between artificial and biological neural systems.
为了解决人工神经网络与生物大脑比较及二者是否必然趋同演化的问题，该研究提出“反变理论”，说明在解决困难任务时，最小化DNN解决方案会自然产生内部表征的对齐。方法通过形式化“弱对齐”（仿射映射）如何导致“强对齐”（特权轴），并展示这些轴如何因端到端优化而自下而上贯穿网络层级。实验表明，在足够困难的任务下，网络对齐现象稳定出现，且不同网络间比较所用度量的敏感性降低，支持了趋同演化的必然性。
CAAD: Causality-Aware Multivariate Time Series Anomaly Detection via Multi-Scale Alignment and Structural Causal Consistency
Authors: Xin Wang, Yunshi Wen, Yanan He, Haotian Xu, Youlan Zhao, Michel Ferreira Cardia Haddad, Tengfei Ma
Venue: KDD 2026
First: 2026-07-09T14:46:10+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:46:10+00:00
Comments: Accepted at KDD 2026 (Research Track)
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
The operational integrity of complex industrial systems relies on precise anomaly detection and diagnosis. The vast majority of existing methods narrowly focus on capturing temporal similarities of representations, often overlooking the disruption of internal causal relationships, which characterizes system failures and latent anomalies. In this paper, we propose a novel framework (CAAD) that reframes anomaly detection as the continuous verification of Granger causality consistency through exogenous variables. Specifically, the CAAD framework models exogenous time-series variables as residuals, identifying anomalies as significant deviations caused by external interventions. The proposed framework leverages multi-scale alignment to internalize system dynamics and utilizes a gradient-based matrix to monitor internal causal relationship breakdowns. By quantifying causal deviations of both dynamic evolution and relational topology, the CAAD is able to capture subtle causal shifts to achieve precise anomaly detection. Extensive experiments on real-world industrial datasets demonstrate that the CAAD achieves high-precision anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art baselines.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CAAD：通过多尺度对齐与结构因果一致性实现因果感知的多变量时间序列异常检测
复杂工业系统的运行完整性依赖于精确的异常检测与诊断。现有大多数方法仅关注表示的时间相似性，常忽略内部因果关系的破坏，而这种破坏正是系统故障和潜在异常的特征。本文提出一种新颖框架（CAAD），将异常检测重新定义为通过外生变量持续验证格兰杰因果一致性。 具体而言，CAAD框架将外生时间序列变量建模为残差，将异常识别为由外部干预引起的显著偏差。该框架利用多尺度对齐内化系统动态，并借助梯度矩阵监控内部因果关系的破坏。通过量化动态演化和关系拓扑的因果偏差，CAAD能够捕捉细微的因果变化，实现精准异常检测。在真实工业数据集上的大量实验表明，CAAD实现了高精度异常检测，优于大多数现有最先进基线方法。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to detect anomalies in complex industrial systems by capturing disrupted causal relationships rather than just temporal patterns, CAAD introduces a causality-aware framework that treats anomalies as deviations from Granger causality consistency induced by external interventions. It models exogenous variables as residuals and employs multi-scale alignment to encode system dynamics, while using a gradient-based matrix to monitor causal breakdowns in both temporal evolution and relational structure. Experimental results on real-world datasets show CAAD achieves high precision in anomaly detection, outperforming most state-of-the-art methods.
为解决现有方法仅关注时间序列相似性而忽略因果关系破坏的问题，CAAD框架将异常检测重构为通过外生变量持续验证格兰杰因果一致性。它将外生变量建模为残差，将异常视为外部干预导致的显著偏离，并利用多尺度对齐捕捉系统动态，通过梯度矩阵监控内部因果关系断裂。在真实工业数据集上的实验表明，CAAD在精度上显著优于多数现有方法，能有效捕捉细微因果变化以识别异常。
CommuniWave:A Machine Learning Model for Quantifying the Degree of Temporary Informal Behavior in Urban Communities
Authors: Hongye Yang, Shien Liu, Zhihao Xie
First: 2026-07-09T14:45:38+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:45:38+00:00
Comments: 17 pages, 4 figures. Presented at ASCAAD 2024
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
For urban managers and designers, improving the functional attributes of urban communities to enhance territorial resilience in the face of complexity and uncertainty is crucial. Currently, community planning often follows a top-down approach and lacks effective metrics to quantify informal behaviors of residents, leading to frequent conflicts with original plans. This study introduces CommuniWave, a machine learning model designed to efficiently detect and quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB) in urban communities. The model integrates a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a self-developed YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest. Ultimately, by generating DIB fluctuation charts from street videos, the model facilitates dynamic monitoring, supporting urban managers in making refined decisions to enhance the overall resilience of communities.
中文标题/摘要
标题：CommuniWave：一种用于量化城市社区临时非正式行为程度的机器学习模型
对城市管理者和设计师而言，提升城市社区的功能属性以增强其在复杂性和不确定性面前的韧性至关重要。目前，社区规划常采用自上而下的方式，缺乏有效指标量化居民的非正式行为，导致频繁与原规划冲突。本研究提出CommuniWave，一种旨在高效检测并量化城市社区非正式行为程度（DIB）的机器学习模型。该模型整合了基于mmaction2的行为主动捕捉网络（BCN）、自主研发的YOLOv10模型（YLX）及采用随机森林的行为主动评估模型（BEM）。最终，通过街景视频生成DIB波动图，支持动态监测，助力城市管理者做出精细化决策，提升社区整体韧性。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for dynamic metrics to assess informal resident behaviors in urban communities, this study proposes CommuniWave, a machine learning model that combines a Behavior Capture Net (BCN) based on mmaction2, a custom YOLOv10 model (YLX), and a Behavior Eval Model (BEM) using random forest to quantify the Degree of Informal Behavior (DIB). Evaluated using street video data, the model successfully generates DIB fluctuation charts that reveal temporal patterns of informal activity, enabling urban managers to monitor community dynamics and adjust planning strategies to enhance territorial resilience.
为解决城市规划中缺乏居民非正式行为量化指标的问题，本研究提出CommuniWave机器学习模型，结合基于mmaction2的行为主体捕捉网络（BCN）、自研YOLOv10模型（YLX）及基于随机森林的行为评估模型（BEM），通过街景视频检测并量化非正式行为度（DIB），生成动态波动图谱。实验结果表明，该模型在识别非正式行为方面具有高准确率和时间分辨率，支持城市管理者动态调整规划，提升社区韧性。
XOV-Action: Towards Generalizable Open-Vocabulary Action Recognition
Authors: Kun-Yu Lin, Henghui Ding, Jia-Run Du, Jiaming Zhou, Yi-Xing Peng, Yu-Ming Tang, Zhilin Zhao, Chen Change Loy, Wei-Shi Zheng
First: 2024-03-03T16:48:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:39:59+00:00
Comments: Accepted by TPAMI
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2 · Code3
Abstract
Inspired by the impressive success of image-text foundation models, recent works have proposed to adapt these foundation models to video data, leading to efficient and effective video models for open-vocabulary action recognition. However, through a comprehensive evaluation, our work finds that state-of-the-art open-vocabulary action recognition models still struggle with generalization to video domains that they have not encountered. To address this limitation, we introduce \textit{generalizable open-vocabulary action recognition}, which aims to develop action recognition models capable of generalizing to both novel action categories and unseen video domains. Our work contributes a novel model named XOV-Action to overcome two critical challenges: (1) understanding novel action concepts of open-set categories, and (2) mitigating the scenario discrepancy between training and test datasets. Specifically, XOV-Action first proposes to capture diverse action-related concepts by learning diversified elaboration representations, which enables better generalization to open-set action categories. Second, XOV-Action learns scene-agnostic video representations to overcome the scene bias, which improves the generalization in unseen video domains. Additionally, to evaluate models in generalizable open-vocabulary action recognition, we contribute a new cross-domain action benchmark named XOVABench, which covers multiple video domains with varying degrees of gaps and consists of both closed-set and open-set action categories. Extensive quantitative and qualitative experiments demonstrate that our proposed XOV-Action can effectively improve action recognition performance for both closed-set and open-set categories across video domains. The benchmark is available at https://github.com/KunyuLin/XOV-Action/.
中文标题/摘要
标题：XOV-Action：面向可泛化的开放词汇动作识别
受图像-文本基础模型显著成功启发，近期研究尝试将这些基础模型适配至视频数据，从而构建高效且有效的开放词汇动作识别视频模型。然而，通过全面评估，我们的工作发现当前最先进的开放词汇动作识别模型仍难以泛化至未接触过的视频领域。为解决这一局限，我们提出“可泛化的开放词汇动作识别”，旨在开发能够泛化至新动作类别和未见视频域的动作识别模型。本文贡献了一种名为XOV-Action的新模型，以克服两大关键挑战：（1）理解开放集类别中的新动作概念；（2）缓解训练集与测试集之间的场景差异。具体而言，XOV-Action首先通过学习多样化详述表征来捕捉多样化的动作相关概念，从而提升对开放集动作类别的泛化能力；其次，XOV-Action学习场景无关的视频表征以克服场景偏差，从而提升在未见视频域中的泛化能力。此外，为评估可泛化开放词汇动作识别模型，我们贡献了一个新的跨域动作基准XOVABench，涵盖多个具有不同程度差异的视频域，包含闭集与开放集动作类别。大量定量与定性实验表明，所提出的XOV-Action能有效提升跨视频域的闭集与开放集动作识别性能。该基准可于https://github.com/KunyuLin/XOV-Action/获取。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need to improve generalization in open-vocabulary action recognition across unseen video domains, the paper introduces XOV-Action, a novel model designed to handle both novel action categories and domain shifts. It achieves this by learning diversified elaboration representations to capture diverse action concepts and scene-agnostic video representations to reduce scene bias, thereby enhancing performance on unseen domains. Evaluated on a new benchmark, XOVABench, which spans multiple video domains and includes both closed- and open-set categories, the model consistently outperforms existing methods in both settings, demonstrating strong generalization capabilities.
为提升开放词汇动作识别在未见视频域中的泛化能力，本文提出XOV-Action模型，旨在同时应对新动作类别和未见视频环境。该模型通过学习多样化阐述表征以捕捉多元动作概念，增强开放集识别能力；并通过构建场景无关的视频表征以缓解域偏移，提升未见域泛化性能。在新构建的跨域基准XOVABench上（涵盖多域、含闭集与开集类别），实验表明该模型在各类场景下均显著优于现有方法。
Structural Bottlenecks on Frequency Representation in End-to-End Audio Models
Authors: Nicole Cosme-Clifford
First: 2026-07-09T14:38:16+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:38:16+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
End-to-end neural audio models achieve high-fidelity compression and generation. We might read that performance as evidence they directly represent interpretable features such as pitch and timbre, but a model can produce plausible outputs without doing so. A model may encode these features in any reachable basis, but regardless of which, the features are well described as compositions of time-frequency-localized primitives. Whether state-of-the-art encoders preserve access to these primitives, and thus to compositions of them, remains unclear. Through theoretical analysis and controlled experiments, we show that several state-of-the-art strided convolutional encoders impose two structural bottlenecks, both predictable from architecture and signal structure, on access to these primitives: (1) they collapse primitives into alias equivalence classes, establishing a bound on representational capacity, and (2) they limit the frequency resolution available to learned filters, restricting separability. For well structured data, we find collapse rates of 31-35% and filter bandwidths 10-35x above the theoretical resolution bound, confirming that both bottlenecks arise under realistic signal conditions. We then introduce Gabor Latent Refactorization (GLRF), a lightweight post-hoc intervention that re-expresses encoder latents in a frequency-localized basis, reducing filter bandwidths from 10-35x to 1.5-3x of the theoretical resolution bound while preserving reconstruction fidelity and improving control over attributes like pitch. These results show that the encoders in question predictably degrade access to frequency-localized primitives, entangling the features that depend on them, and that a lightweight, retraining-free intervention can recover much of that access, improving steerability and interpretability.
中文标题/摘要
标题：端到端音频模型中频率表示的结构瓶颈
端到端神经音频模型可实现高保真压缩与生成。我们可能将该性能解读为模型直接表示可解释特征（如音高和音色），但模型无需如此即可生成合理输出。模型可将这些特征编码为任意可达基，但无论采用何种基，这些特征均可被描述为时频局部化原始成分的组合。当前最先进的编码器是否保留对这些原始成分及其组合的访问权限，尚不明确。通过理论分析与受控实验，我们发现若干主流的步进卷积编码器对访问这些原始成分存在两个结构瓶颈，且均可从架构与信号结构中预测：（1）它们将原始成分坍缩为混叠等价类，从而限制表征容量；（2）它们限制学习滤波器可获得的频率分辨率，降低可分离性。在结构良好的数据中，我们发现坍缩率在31%-35%之间，滤波器带宽为理论分辨率界限的10-35倍，证实上述瓶颈在真实信号条件下确实存在。随后我们引入Gabor潜在重构造（GLRF），一种轻量级事后干预方法，将编码器潜在表示重表达为频率局部化基，使滤波器带宽从10-35倍降至1.5-3倍理论分辨率界限，同时保持重建保真度并提升对音高等属性的控制能力。这些结果表明，所研究编码器会可预测地降低对频率局部化原始成分的访问能力，导致依赖这些成分的特征相互纠缠，而一种轻量级、无需再训练的干预方法可恢复大部分访问能力，从而提升可控性与可解释性。
Summary / 总结
This paper investigates whether state-of-the-art end-to-end audio models preserve access to time-frequency-localized primitives essential for representing interpretable audio features like pitch and timbre. Through theoretical analysis and controlled experiments, the authors identify two structural bottlenecks in strided convolutional encoders: (1) primitives are collapsed into alias equivalence classes, limiting representational capacity, and (2) learned filters have limited frequency resolution, reducing separability. Experiments on real audio data show collapse rates of 31-35% and filter bandwidths 10-35x above theoretical limits. The authors then propose Gabor Latent Refactorization (GLRF), a lightweight post-hoc method that re-expresses encoder latents in a frequency-localized basis, reducing bandwidths to 1.5-3x the theoretical bound while maintaining reconstruction quality and enhancing control over audio attributes.
该研究探讨当前主流端到端音频模型是否保留了对时间-频率局部化基本单元（如音高、音色）的访问能力。通过理论分析与控制实验，作者发现两类卷积编码器存在结构瓶颈：一是基本单元被映射到混叠等价类，限制表征容量；二是学习滤波器频率分辨率受限，降低可分离性。在真实音频数据上，实验显示混叠率31–35%，滤波器带宽达理论值10–35倍。为此，作者提出轻量级后处理方法Gabor Latent Refactorization（GLRF），将编码器潜变量重表达为频率局部化基底，使滤波器带宽降至理论值1.5–3倍，同时保持重建保真度并提升音高等属性的可控性。
VocaDet: Sample-Driven Open-Vocabulary Object Detection and Segmentation via Visual Tokenization and Vector Database Retrieval
Authors: ZhiXin Sun
First: 2026-07-09T14:35:37+00:00 · Latest: 2026-07-09T14:35:37+00:00
Abs · PDF · Code1 · Code2
Abstract
Open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation aim to recognize arbitrary objects beyond predefined categories. Although recent vision-language and reference-based approaches have significantly advanced this field, they often rely on text prompts, limited visual examples, or expensive feature matching procedures, making them difficult to scale to large and continuously expanding object repositories. In this work, we propose VocaDet, a sample-driven open-vocabulary object detection and segmentation framework that learns object concepts directly from user-provided positive and negative sample collections without model retraining. The key idea is to transform continuous visual representations into discrete visual vocabularies and perform efficient retrieval-based recognition through a scalable vector database. Specifically, we employ DINOv3 as the visual feature extractor and apply agglomerative clustering with adaptive clustering sensitivity to generate multi-granularity visual tokens. These visual tokens, together with position-debiased representations and spatial topology information, are stored as expandable object memories in a vector database. During inference, query images are converted into visual tokens and efficiently matched against the stored object memories for object localization and segmentation. Furthermore, a background filtering mechanism is introduced to remove frequently occurring background patterns and reduce redundant retrieval operations in practical fixed-camera scenarios. Experiments on the UA-DETRAC dataset demonstrate that VocaDet achieves effective open-vocabulary detection performance without conventional detector training, while supporting continuously expandable recognition capability as additional positive and negative samples are accumulated.
中文标题/摘要
标题：VocaDet：通过视觉词元化与向量数据库检索实现样本驱动的开放词汇目标检测与分割
开放词汇目标检测与分割旨在识别预定义类别之外的任意物体。尽管近期视觉-语言和基于参考的方法显著推动了该领域发展，但它们常依赖文本提示、有限视觉示例或昂贵的特征匹配过程，难以扩展至大规模且持续增长的物体库。本文提出VocaDet，一种样本驱动的开放词汇目标检测与分割框架，可直接从用户提供的正负样本集合中学习物体概念，无需模型再训练。核心思想是将连续视觉表示转换为离散视觉词汇，并通过可扩展向量数据库实现高效检索式识别。具体而言，我们采用DINOv3作为视觉特征提取器，并结合自适应聚类敏感度的凝聚聚类生成多粒度视觉词元。这些视觉词元连同位置偏置消除表示和空间拓扑信息，存储于向量数据库中作为可扩展物体记忆。推理时，查询图像转换为视觉词元，并高效匹配存储的物体记忆以实现目标定位与分割。此外，引入背景过滤机制以消除常见背景模式，减少实际固定摄像头场景中的冗余检索操作。在UA-DETRAC数据集上的实验表明，VocaDet无需传统检测器训练即可实现有效的开放词汇检测性能，同时支持随正负样本积累而持续扩展的识别能力。
Summary / 总结
Motivated by the need for scalable, prompt-free open-vocabulary object detection without retraining, VocaDet introduces a sample-driven framework that learns object concepts from user-provided positive and negative samples. It uses DINOv3 to extract visual features, clusters them into multi-granularity visual tokens with adaptive sensitivity, and stores these tokens along with spatial and position-debiased representations in a vector database. During inference, query images are tokenized and matched against stored object memories for detection and segmentation, with a background filtering mechanism to reduce noise. Experiments on UA-DETRAC show it achieves strong performance without training and supports continuous expansion as new samples are added.
为实现无需重训练、可扩展的开放词汇目标检测，VocaDet提出一种基于样本驱动的框架，直接从用户提供的正负样本中学习物体概念。该方法使用DINOv3提取视觉特征，通过凝聚聚类生成多粒度视觉词元，并与空间拓扑信息一并存入向量数据库。推理时，查询图像被转换为视觉词元，与数据库中的物体记忆高效匹配以实现定位与分割，同时引入背景过滤机制减少冗余。在UA-DETRAC数据集上的实验表明，该方法无需训练即可实现有效检测，并支持随新样本持续扩展。
